Under a value-to-decision error bound (VDB), second-order information for the marginal map \(Φ\) lifts to a second-order Dini formula for \(S\). We first work in the abstract inclusion model \(x\in H(p)\), where outer and inner estimates yield second-order semi-derivability of \(S\). We then specialize to structured feasible maps \(H(p)=\{x\inΩ:g(p,x)\in D\}\). Under Robinson metric regularity along \(Ω\), second-order regularity of \(Ω\) and \(D\), and directional second-order semi-derivability of the data, we obtain explicit formulas for \(\DD H\), \(\DDΦ\), and \(\DD S\). The framework is specialized to polyhedral inequality/equality systems and illustrated by a robust multi-objective portfolio model and a DC-dispatch model for electricity markets, with a brief discussion of complementarity-based extensions.

</details>


### [104] [Sharp $O(1/k)$ convergence rate for the Sinkhorn algorithm via a local analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28973)
*Guillaume Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明Sinkhorn算法在$\ell_1$范数边际误差和联合相对熵上以$O(1/k)$速率收敛，该速率在渐近可扩展情形下是紧的。证明基于分析熵正则化最优传输问题的二分图，区分分配正质量的边与未分配正质量的边，得到具有紧速率的局部收敛界，并利用作者先前结果将其提升为全局界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Sinkhorn算法在熵正则化最优传输问题中达到已知最优的$O(1/k)$收敛速率。

Method: 通过分析熵正则化最优传输问题的二分图结构，区分最优传输计划中分配正质量的边和未分配正质量的边，分别处理以获得局部收敛界，再结合作者先前几乎紧（差对数因子）的结果提升为全局界。

Result: Sinkhorn算法在$\ell_1$范数边际误差和联合相对熵上达到$O(1/k)$的收敛速率，该速率是紧的。

Conclusion: Sinkhorn算法在熵正则化最优传输问题中达到已知最优的收敛速率。

Abstract: We prove that the Sinkhorn algorithm converges at the rate of $O(1/k)$ in $\ell_1$-norm marginal error and in joint relative entropy, which is known to be sharp in the asymptotically scalable case. The proof is based on examining the bipartite graph associated to the entropy-regularized optimal transport problem, and treating differently the edges that are assigned a positive mass in the optimal transport plan vs. those that are not. This yields a local convergence bound with the sharp rate, which is bootstrapped into a global bound using the author's previous result in arXiv:2604.26265 where we showed an almost-sharp rate up to a logarithmic factor.

</details>


### [105] [Faster than Fast-LTS: Robust Regression and Outlier Detection with DC Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28974)
*Marah-Lisanne Thormann,Phan Tu Vuong,Alain B. Zemkoho,Tri-Dung Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的算法（sBDCA）来解决最小修剪平方（LTS）鲁棒回归问题，将LTS重新表述为带 capped 单纯形约束的凹最小化问题，并引入预处理矩阵以从单一初始点实现鲁棒性。实验表明，该算法比Fast-LTS快3.25倍，目标函数值降低高达90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LTS鲁棒回归问题精确求解是组合优化问题，计算成本过高；实践中常用的Fast-LTS启发式方法虽然有效，但其核心过程需要大量初始点才能获得鲁棒结果，存在改进空间。

Method: 将LTS问题重新表述为带capped单纯形约束的凹最小化问题；提出逐次增强的DC（Difference of Convex）函数算法（sBDCA）作为求解方法；推导并集成问题特定的预处理矩阵以确保单初始点下的鲁棒性；利用Łojasiewicz性质证明算法以线性速率收敛到局部解。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，sBDCA比Fast-LTS快3.25倍，目标函数值降低高达90%，尤其是在高维设置下表现优异。

Conclusion: sBDCA是一种高效、鲁棒的LTS求解方法，从单一初始点即可实现良好性能，并提供了Python中鲁棒回归的实用指南，所有代码开源。

Abstract: When datasets contain outliers, robust regression is a well-established alternative to Ordinary Least Squares. A commonly employed robust estimator is Least Trimmed Squares (LTS), which computes the regression coefficients from a subset of observations. Determining the exact solution corresponds to a combinatorial problem with prohibitive computational costs, even for instances of moderate dimension. Thus, the most prevalent approach in practice remains a heuristic known as Fast-LTS. Although the heuristic often performs effectively, certain elements of the approach remain open to improvement. In particular, its core procedure provides robust results only when initialized with a large number of starting points. To address the heuristic's limitations, this paper reformulates the LTS problem as a concave minimization problem subject to a capped simplex constraint, and proposes the successive Boosted Difference of Convex Functions Algorithm (sBDCA) as a solution method. Theoretically, we establish via the Łojasiewicz property that sBDCA converges to a local solution with a linear rate in the fastest case. To ensure robustness from a single initialization in practice, we derive and integrate a problem-specific preconditioning matrix into the algorithmic setup. Building on this theoretical foundation, we conduct numerical studies on various synthetic and real-world datasets to demonstrate the effectiveness of sBDCA with preconditioning. Specifically, we show that our approach is up to 3.25 times faster than Fast-LTS and achieves up to 90% lower objective function values, particularly in high-dimensional settings. As all code is openly available, this paper further provides a practical guide to robust regression in Python.

</details>


### [106] [An Approximate Conjugate Subgradient Algorithm with Matrix Parameter for Derivative-Free Nonsmooth Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29084)
*Morteza Kimiaei,Saman Babaie-Kafaki,Zohre Aminifard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无导数的矩阵共轭次梯度方法，用于求解局部Lipschitz函数的无约束非光滑优化问题。该方法通过函数值构造离散梯度并对Goldstein次微分进行有限采样，结合矩阵记忆校正、系数阻尼、对角缩放、有界角度校正和矩阵稳定性保护等机制提高鲁棒性。采用两点线搜索过程，在适当一致性假设下能生成满足安全下降性质的方向并计算近似Goldstein稳定点。数值实验表明，该方法在中低精度需求下鲁棒性强，矩阵共轭次梯度变体在严格容差下最可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑优化问题广泛存在于实际应用中，但传统的基于导数的方法无法直接适用。现有无导数方法在处理高维非光滑问题时面临收敛速度慢、鲁棒性不足等挑战，亟需开发更有效的算法来满足不同精度要求的求解需求。

Method: 该方法利用函数值构造离散梯度，对Goldstein次微分进行有限采样；在凸包中计算最小范数元素作为平稳性度量和下降方向参考向量；引入矩阵记忆校正、系数阻尼、对角缩放、有界角度校正和矩阵稳定性保护等机制提升鲁棒性；采用带富集策略的两点线搜索过程获得有效步长或改进局部模型。

Result: 在维度高达1000的非光滑测试问题上进行数值实验，结果表明所提出的变体在中低精度需求下具有良好鲁棒性，其中矩阵共轭次梯度变体在最严格容差下表现最为可靠。

Conclusion: 本文提出的无导数矩阵共轭次梯度方法能有效求解高维非光滑优化问题，具有良好的鲁棒性和可靠性，尤其矩阵共轭次梯度变体在严格精度要求下表现出色。

Abstract: We propose a derivative-free matrix conjugate-subgradient method for unconstrained nonsmooth optimization of locally Lipschitz functions. The method constructs discrete gradients using only function values and forms a finite sampled model of the Goldstein subdifferential. A minimal-norm element of the convex hull of the sampled discrete gradients is then computed and used both as a stationarity measure and as the reference vector for generating descent-oriented directions. To improve robustness beyond the basic steepest-descent direction, we introduce a matrix memory correction together with coefficient damping, diagonal scaling, bounded-angle correction, and matrix-stability safeguards. A two-point line-search procedure with enrichment is used to obtain either a serious step or an improved local model. Under suitable consistency assumptions on the discrete-gradient approximation and line-search sampling, the method generates directions satisfying a safeguarded descent property and computes approximate Goldstein stationary points. Numerical experiments on nonsmooth test problems with dimensions up to 1000 show that both proposed variants are robust for lower and medium accuracy requirements, while the matrix conjugate-subgradient variant remains the most reliable under the strictest tolerance.

</details>


### [107] [Modern Theory of Gradient-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29206)
*Bin Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于梯度优化的新兴技术，重点探讨常微分方程视角与离散Lyapunov分析之间的相互作用，从NAG-SC和重球法的加速机制出发，通过高分辨率ODE建模和Lyapunov函数构建阐述梯度修正项的核心作用，并扩展到FISTA、ADMM、PDHG等算法的统一分析框架，最后讨论了极小极大优化的最新进展与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理梯度优化中ODE视角与Lyapunov分析的最新进展，为加速优化算法提供统一的理论框架。

Method: 通过高分辨率ODE建模揭示梯度修正项的加速机制，系统构造Lyapunov函数建立收敛性分析，并将ODE近似与Lyapunov技术扩展到ADMM、PDHG等算法。

Result: 揭示了NAG-SC和重球法中梯度修正项是加速的核心驱动力；建立了NAG及其近亲FISTA在梯度范数加速收敛、欠阻尼加速、强凸线性收敛等方面的成果；将统一框架成功扩展到ADMM、PDHG及其加速变体。

Conclusion: ODE和Lyapunov方法为理解和设计加速优化算法提供了强大的统一工具，未来可进一步拓展至鞍点问题的分析。

Abstract: In this review, we offer a comprehensive survey of emerging techniques in gradient-based optimization, with a particular emphasis on the interplay between ordinary differential equation (ODE) perspectives and their extensions into discrete Lyapunov analysis. We begin by examining the acceleration mechanisms underlying Nesterov's accelerated gradient method for strongly convex functions (NAG-SC) and Polyak's heavy-ball method, identifying the gradient-correction term as the primary driver of acceleration. This mechanistic insight is substantiated through high-resolution ODE modeling and the systematic construction of Lyapunov functions. We then synthesize recent advancements in convex optimization regarding NAG and its proximal generalization, the fast iterative shrinkage-thresholding algorithm (FISTA). Key topics include the accelerated convergence of gradient norms, underdamped acceleration, linear convergence under strong convexity, and novel Lyapunov frameworks for establishing convergence and monotonicity properties of generalized accelerated methods. Furthermore, we demonstrate how these ODE approximations and Lyapunov techniques can be extended to provide a unified framework for analyzing advanced optimization algorithms, including the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), the primal-dual hybrid gradient (PDHG) method, and their respective accelerated variants. Finally, we discuss recent progress in minimax optimization and outline future directions for extending Lyapunov-based analysis to saddle-point problems.

</details>


### [108] [Approximation and Controllability of Nonlinear Control-Affine Systems via Semiautonomous Neural Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29338)
*Dev Prakash Jha,Raju K. George*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出受控半自主神经常微分方程（controlled SA-NODE），用于非线性受控动力系统的逼近与学习，在保持较低参数复杂度的同时实现轨迹重构与可控性，并给出逼近误差估计与可控性保持的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经常微分方程在处理受控（带控制输入）非线性动力系统时存在参数复杂度过高的问题，亟需一种既能保持结构简洁又能有效逼近控制系统的框架。

Method: 将半自主神经常微分方程扩展至控制仿射系统，引入与时间无关的可训练系数以降低参数复杂度；基于Sobolev和Barron正则性假设推导定量逼近误差界，并证明受控SA-NODE保持原系统的近似可控性。

Result: 建立了受控SA-NODE的一致逼近定理（紧集上），定量误差为$\mathcal{O}(P^{-1/2}+Q^{-1/2})$；在受控摆和Duffing振子实验中，以远少于经典神经ODE架构的参数实现了精确的轨迹重构和可控性表现。

Conclusion: 受控SA-NODE是一种参数高效、理论保证充分的神经ODE框架，适用于非线性受控动力系统的学习与控制。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce controlled semiautonomous neural ordinary differential equations (controlled SA-NODEs) for the approximation and learning of nonlinear controlled dynamical systems. The proposed framework extends semiautonomous neural ODEs to control-affine systems while preserving reduced parameter complexity through time-independent trainable coefficients. We establish a universal approximation theorem showing that controlled SA-NODEs approximate trajectories of nonlinear controlled systems uniformly on compact sets of initial conditions and admissible controls. Under additional Sobolev and Barron regularity assumptions, we derive quantitative approximation estimates of order $\mathcal{O}(P^{-1/2}+Q^{-1/2})$. We further prove that approximate controllability properties of the original nonlinear system are preserved under the controlled SA-NODE approximation. Numerical experiments on controlled pendulum and Duffing oscillator systems demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves accurate trajectory reconstruction and controllability performance with significantly fewer trainable parameters than classical neural ODE architectures.

</details>


### [109] [Turnpike and Sparse Optimal Control for Semiautonomous Neural ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29343)
*Dev Prakash Jha,Raju K. George*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究控制仿射半自主神经常微分方程(SA-NODE)的长时间最优控制问题，采用ℓ1正则化控制。建立了三个主要结果：指数转向性质（最优状态-控制对指数级接近稳态最优对）、单边时间稀疏性（最优控制在初始弧段全幅激活，之后完全消失）、以及积分转向估计。数值实验验证了转向轮廓和单边稀疏结构，相比普通NODE实现了30倍的参数缩减且不损失稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将神经ODE的长时间最优控制问题与ℓ1正则化相结合，研究其转向性质和稀疏性结构，旨在实现参数高效且性能不损失的控制策略。

Method: 结合耗散不等式、庞特里亚金最优性系统的一致伴随界估计，以及针对半自主架构的时间重缩放论证；数值实验采用Duffing振子和阻尼摆模型。

Result: 建立了指数转向性质、单边时间稀疏性（控制仅在初始区间[0,T*]活跃）、积分转向估计；在Duffing振子和阻尼摆上验证了三阶段转向轮廓和单边稀疏结构，实现30倍参数缩减且无性能损失。

Conclusion: ℓ1正则化SA-NODE的最优控制具有指数转向性质和单边时间稀疏性，能够在不牺牲稳定性能的前提下大幅减少参数使用量。

Abstract: We study long-time optimal control of control-affine semiautonomous neural ordinary differential equations (SA-NODEs) with $\ell^1$-regularized controls. Three results are established. First, optimal state-control pairs satisfy an \emph{exponential turnpike property}: they remain exponentially close to a stationary optimal pair for most of the time horizon, with decay rate and prefactor independent of the horizon length $T$. Second, $\ell^1$ penalisation induces \emph{one-sided temporal sparsity}: optimal controls are active at full amplitude on an initial arc $[0,T^*]$ and vanish identically on $(T^*,T)$, where $T^*$ is independent of $T$ for $T$ large. Third, an integral turnpike estimate shows the time-averaged deviation from the stationary pair is bounded uniformly in $T$. The proofs combine dissipativity inequalities, uniform adjoint bounds via the Pontryagin optimality system, and a time-rescaling argument adapted to the semiautonomous architecture. Numerical experiments on a Duffing oscillator and a damped pendulum confirm the three-phase turnpike profile and the one-sided sparsity structure, and demonstrate a $30\times$ parameter reduction over vanilla NODEs with no loss of stabilization performance.

</details>


### [110] [An Exact Algorithm for Mixed-Integer Bilevel Stochastic Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29344)
*Tomás Lagos,Dmytro Matsypura*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对混合整数双层随机规划问题，提出了一种结合确定值函数重构与场景分解的精确算法，通过随机次梯度割平面方法动态生成最优性割并更新拉格朗日乘子，在有限步内收敛到全局最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层混合整数随机规划具有层次化结构和双层离散变量，属于Σ₂ᵖ-难问题，标准单层重构方法计算上不可行，亟需高效的精确求解算法。

Method: 提出扩展单层重构及其松弛以实现场景分解；引入随机次梯度割平面方案，动态生成跟随者最优性割并更新拉格朗日乘子。

Result: 在有界性假设下，算法在有限时间内收敛到全局最优解，并在执行过程中提供有效的上下界。

Conclusion: 该算法为Σ₂ᵖ-难的混合整数双层随机规划问题提供了首个具备有限收敛保证的精确求解方法。

Abstract: We study a class of mixed-integer bilevel stochastic programs where the leader commits to a first-stage decision before uncertainty is realized, and the follower solves a subsequent mixed-integer optimization problem for each revealed scenario. Due to the hierarchical structure and the presence of discrete variables at both levels, these problems are inherently $Σ_2^p$-hard, making standard single-level reformulations computationally intractable. To address this significant computational challenge, we develop an exact algorithm that combines deterministic value-function reformulations with stochastic scenario-wise decomposition. Specifically, we propose an extended single-level reformulation and a corresponding relaxation that enable scenario decomposition. We then introduce a stochastic subgradient cutting-plane scheme that dynamically generates follower optimality cuts while updating the Lagrange multipliers. We prove that, under boundedness assumptions, our algorithm converges in finite time to a true global optimum while providing valid upper and lower bounds throughout its execution.

</details>


### [111] [Solver-Verified Formulation Generation and Selection for Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29366)
*Jintao Xu,Yingzheng Ma,Jiong Dong,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang,Dongyang Geng,Anni Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出ORLA框架，利用大语言模型结合求解器反馈，自动将半结构化/自然语言的多仓库库存分配需求转化为可求解的OR模型并选择最优方案，在京东29个生产批次中实现4.5个百分点的分配准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电商供应链中多仓库库存分配的平衡决策问题频繁出现，但实际中的分配需求常以半结构化或自然语言形式表达，而非现成的运筹学公式，需要自动化的OR建模方法。

Method: 提出ORLA框架，包含：(1) 自动"问题-模型-代码(PMC)"生成；(2) 基于学习的公式化选择；(3) 可行性恢复机制。开发三种MIP公式化家族（偏差最小化、软区间合规、背包启发式分配），结合MILP重构、模块化约束扩展和基于惩罚的松弛机制。LLM生成候选公式和求解代码，求解器提供验证信号，通过评分感知聚合选择最优组合。

Result: 在京东29个生产评估批次上，最优单一OR公式化比现有方法提高3.4个百分点准确率；完整ORLA框架整体提升4.5个百分点，并在29批次中的26个批次上改进了分配准确率。

Conclusion: ORLA框架有效弥合了自然语言分配需求与可求解OR模型之间的鸿沟，通过LLM与求解器的协同验证显著提升了多仓库库存分配的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Balance-oriented multi-warehouse inventory allocation is a recurring decision problem in large-scale e-commerce supply chains, in which a fixed replenishment quantity is distributed across warehouses to balance post-allocation inventory coverage while accounting for demand forecasts and heterogeneous allocation constraints. In practice, allocation requirements are often scenario-dependent and expressed in semi-structured or natural-language form rather than as ready-to-solve operations research (OR) formulations. We propose an OR-guided Large Language Model (LLM) for Allocation (ORLA) that uses solver feedback to generate, verify, and select OR formulations. ORLA integrates automatic "Problem-Model-Code (PMC)" generation, learning-based formulation selection, and feasibility restoration. We develop three complementary mixed-integer programming formulation families based on deviation minimization, soft band compliance, and knapsack-inspired allocation, together with solver-ready mixed-integer linear programming reformulations, modular constraint extensions, and a penalty-based relaxation mechanism for infeasible cases. The LLM component generates candidate formulations and executable solver code from textual or semi-structured specifications, while the solver provides verification signals for executability, feasibility, and solution quality. To address instance heterogeneity, ORLA estimates the expected quality of candidate formulations, selects promising candidates, and combines their outputs through score-aware aggregation. Experimental results on 29 production evaluation batches from JD.com show that the best single OR formulation improves allocation accuracy by 3.4 percentage points over the incumbent approach, while the full ORLA framework achieves a 4.5 percentage-point overall improvement and improves allocation accuracy in 26 of the 29 evaluation batches.

</details>


### [112] [Fractional Stochastic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29438)
*Yuecai Han,Jianming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由分数布朗运动驱动的具有残差动力学的分数阶随机神经网络，推导了离散随机最大值原理和伴随递推，证明了投影样本随机梯度下降的均方收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了其在长记忆恢复和鲁棒性方面相对于布朗运动和确定性基线的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机神经网络通常采用标准布朗运动驱动，但布朗运动缺乏长程依赖性和非马尔可夫特性。本文旨在引入分数布朗运动（fractional Brownian motion）来驱动神经网络残差动力学，以更好地捕捉时间序列中的长记忆效应，并提高模型在结构化扰动下的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 构建分数布朗运动驱动的残差随机神经网络；2) 引入离散随机最大值原理，推导对应的伴随递推（adjoint recursion），用于高效计算梯度；3) 在确定性网络参数假设下，证明投影样本随机梯度下降（projected samplewise stochastic gradient descent）的均方收敛性；4) 设计多项数值实验验证方法有效性。

Result: 数值实验包括：闭式收敛测试、带不确定性量化的有噪回归、长记忆时间序列生成及结构化扰动下的图像分类。结果表明，在某些设定下，分数阶驱动相比布朗运动和确定性基线，能显著改善长记忆恢复能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 分数布朗运动驱动的随机神经网络在捕捉长程依赖性和抗扰动方面优于标准布朗运动驱动和确定性模型，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a fractional stochastic neural network with residual dynamics driven by fractional Brownian motion. By introducing a discrete stochastic maximum principle for the network, we construct the corresponding adjoint recursion. For deterministic network parameters, we prove mean square convergence of projected samplewise stochastic gradient descent. Numerical experiments include a closed form convergence test, noisy regression with uncertainty quantification, long memory time series generation and image classification under structured perturbations. The results identify settings in which fractional drivers improve long memory recovery or robustness relative to Brownian and deterministic baselines.

</details>


### [113] [A Posteriori Error Analysis for Decoupled Neural Approximations of Fully Coupled FBSDEs with Control Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29474)
*Xichuan Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类全耦合正倒向随机微分方程（FBSDEs）解耦神经近似的后验误差分析框架，建立了连续时间和离散时间下的稳定性估计，给出了仅依赖于终端缺陷、路径残差和控制不匹配项的可计算后验误差界，并通过数值实验验证了所提指标的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际深度学习实现中，FBSDEs的神经近似通常采用解耦策略，即引入一个可能与神经网络逼近的倒向分量不同的辅助控制过程。这种解耦会导致控制不匹配问题，但现有的误差分析并未充分考虑这一因素。因此需要建立包含控制不匹配项的后验误差分析框架，以评估和保证数值近似的质量。

Method: 首先建立了全耦合FBSDEs在漂移、扩散、生成元、终端条件和辅助控制输入扰动下的连续时间稳定性估计；然后将该估计转化到离散时间框架，推导出仅依赖于终端缺陷、路径残差和控制不匹配项的可计算后验误差界。当辅助控制与倒向近似一致时，不匹配项消失，误差界退化为标准的两项形式。

Result: 在线性-二次型FBSDE（有显式参考解）和多维Burgers型FBSDE（无参考解）上的数值实验表明，所提出的后验误差指示器能够有效诊断近似质量，且控制不匹配惩罚项对数值近似的一致性和可复现性具有重要贡献。

Conclusion: 本文建立了包含控制不匹配项的全耦合FBSDEs解耦神经近似后验误差分析框架，为实际深度学习方法提供了理论保障和实用诊断工具。

Abstract: This paper develops an a posteriori error analysis framework for decoupled neural approximations of fully coupled forward--backward stochastic differential equations (FBSDEs). It provides an a posteriori error-analysis for the idealized discrete adapted trajectory. The main feature of the proposed formulation is the use of an auxiliary control process in the forward coefficients, which may differ from the backward component approximated by the neural network. This decoupling is useful in practical deep learning implementations, but it creates a control mismatch that must be included in the error analysis. We first establish a continuous-time stability estimate for fully coupled FBSDEs under perturbations of the drift, diffusion, generator, terminal condition, and auxiliary control input. We then transfer this estimate to the discrete-time setting and derive computable a posteriori error bounds depending only on the terminal defect, the pathwise residual, and the control mismatch. When the auxiliary control is identified with the backward approximation, the mismatch term vanishes and the bound reduces to the standard two-term form. Numerical experiments on a linear--quadratic FBSDE with an explicit reference solution and a multidimensional Burgers-type FBSDE without a reference solution illustrate the diagnostic role of the proposed indicators and the contribution of the mismatch penalty to the consistency and reproducibility of the numerical approximations.

</details>


### [114] [The Simple Strategy-Iteration Method is Strongly Polynomial for the Turn-Based Deterministic Forward Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29568)
*Sanyou Mei,Chunlin Sun,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了回合制确定性前向博弈（TBDFG），证明其前向结构能保证结构无关的策略迭代算法具有强多项式复杂度（O(n^6m^4log⁴ n)），并提出利用该结构的反向SCC传播算法可将复杂度提升至O(n³m²log² n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索回合制确定性零和博弈中一种弱于无环性的结构性质——前向条件（TBDFG），即不存在包含双方玩家动作的有向环。该结构允许单方玩家内部存在复杂循环，但排除混合玩家反馈环。旨在理解该结构对算法复杂性的影响。

Method: 1) 分析结构无关的策略迭代算法[11,14]在TBDFG上的表现，证明其强多项式收敛性；2) 提出利用TBDFG结构的反向SCC传播算法，通过求解一系列确定性MDP子问题来加速。

Result: 结构无关算法在TBDFG上最多需要O(n⁶m⁴log⁴ n)次单纯形转轴步；而结构感知算法将复杂度降至O(n³m²log² n)。

Conclusion: 前向结构既能正则化通用策略迭代方法的收敛性，也能支持更高效的结构感知算法。该性质充当了结构证书，即使算法不知道输入具有该性质也能保证收敛。

Abstract: We study Turn-Based Deterministic Forward Games (TBDFGs), the subclass of turn-based deterministic zero-sum games in which no directed cycle contains actions controlled by both players. This forward condition is strictly weaker than acyclicity: recurrent behavior may be arbitrarily rich within one player's states, while mixed-player feedback cycles are excluded. Our main contribution separates two algorithmic consequences of this structure. First, we analyze the simple strategy-iteration method of [11,14], a generic method for TBSGs whose execution neither tests for nor uses the TBDFG property. We prove that this structure-oblivious algorithm nevertheless has a strongly polynomial guarantee on every TBDFG. In particular, it terminates after at most $O(n^6m^4\log^4 n)$ simplex pivot steps. Thus, the forward property acts as a structural certificate for convergence even when the algorithm is not informed that the input has this property. Second, when the TBDFG structure is known in advance, a backward SCC propagation algorithm is proposed that solves a sequence of deterministic-MDP subproblems and improves the bound to $O(n^3m^2\log^2 n)$ simplex pivot steps. Together, these results show that forward structure both regularizes the convergence of a general strategy-iteration method and supports a sharper structure-aware algorithm.

</details>


### [115] [AdaGrad does not adapt to Hölder-smoothness for composite objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29893)
*Matia Bojovic,Saverio Salzo,Massimiliano Pontil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个简单的一维确定性凸复合优化问题，在该问题上AdaGrad算法无法达到与Hölder光滑目标相关的经典收敛率$\mathcal{O}(n^{-(1+ν)/2})$，揭示了AdaGrad累积机制与复合最优性之间的基本不匹配。主要原因在于光滑项的梯度在最优解处可能不为零，导致AdaGrad过度减小步长而收敛变慢。文章还讨论了基于梯度映射或连续梯度差的替代累积机制如何避免这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AdaGrad算法在凸复合优化问题中收敛率退化的根本原因，指出经典AdaGrad累积机制与复合优化问题结构之间的不匹配。

Method: 通过构造一个一维确定性的凸复合优化反例，理论分析AdaGrad在该问题上的收敛行为，并与基于梯度映射和连续梯度差的替代累积机制进行对比分析。

Result: AdaGrad在构造的反例上收敛率劣于$\mathcal{O}(n^{-(1+ν)/2})$，原因在于光滑项梯度在最优解处非零导致步长过度衰减；而梯度映射和连续梯度差机制可以避免该问题。

Conclusion: 标准的AdaGrad累积机制不适用于光滑项梯度在最优解处非零的复合优化场景，需要采用更合适的累积方式（如基于梯度映射或连续梯度差）来保证最优收敛率。

Abstract: We exhibit a simple deterministic one-dimensional convex composite optimization problem for which AdaGrad scheme does not achieve the classical convergence rate $\mathcal{O}(n^{-(1+ν)/2})$ associated with Hölder-smooth objectives. The example highlights a basic mismatch between classical AdaGrad accumulation and composite optimality. A main insight is that the gradient of the smooth term may not vanish at the optimum, causing AdaGrad to keep reducing its stepsize excessively and converge more slowly. We also discuss why alternative accumulation mechanisms based on gradient mappings or on successive gradient differences, avoid this pathology.

</details>


### [116] [Semi-discrete convex order and Laguerre tessellation fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29913)
*David P. Bourne,Thomas Gallouët,Quentin Mérigot,Andrea Natale*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了给定细胞体积和重心条件下重构Laguerre镶嵌问题，建立了几何解释并将其转化为Wasserstein投影问题，在材料科学EBSD图像分析中得到了应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laguerre镶嵌是参数化欧氏空间凸分割的高效工具，广泛应用于计算几何、成像和数值分析。然而，如何从指定细胞体积和重心逆向重构Laguerre镶嵌是一个尚未解决的重要问题，该问题的解决对于材料科学等领域中的微观结构建模具有重要意义。

Method: 将重构问题转化为在凸序支配的离散测度集合上计算Wasserstein投影问题，建立了问题的几何解释，表明重构可通过求解一个最优传输问题近似实现。

Result: 证明了重构问题可以通过计算Wasserstein投影来近似求解，并将方法推广到拟合任意重心集合的Laguerre镶嵌。在材料科学中成功应用于拟合钢的EBSD图像。

Conclusion: 建立了一种基于最优传输理论的Laguerre镶嵌重构方法，实现了从指定体积和重心反向构建镶嵌结构，并验证了其在材料微观结构分析中的实用性。

Abstract: Laguerre tessellations offer an efficient way to parameterize a large class of convex partitions of Euclidean space using only a set of points and scalar weights. For this reason, they have become popular in computational geometry, imaging and numerical analysis, both as a modeling and a discretization tool. In this paper we study the problem of reconstructing a Laguerre tessellation with prescribed cell volumes from the barycenters of its cells. We establish a geometric interpretation of this problem in terms of the set of discrete measures dominated in convex order by an absolutely continuous measure. In particular, we show that the reconstruction problem can be solved approximately by computing a Wasserstein projection onto this set. More generally, our method can also be applied to fit a Laguerre tessellation to an arbitrary set of barycenters. We give a concrete application of this in materials science, of fitting a Laguerre tessellation to an electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) image of a steel.

</details>


### [117] [Factorized low-rank matrix recovery problem, Schatten-$q$ quasi-norm, Error bound for critical point, Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz property, Inexact proximal alternating linearized minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29973)
*Yongjun Chen,Defeng Sun,Liping Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Schatten-$q$拟范数正则化低秩矩阵恢复的因子化模型，解决了临界点误差界未探索以及$\|\cdot\|_2^q$近端算子缺乏闭式解的问题。提出了S-临界点的概念，建立了其与全局最优解的关系并推导了误差界；设计了非精确近端交替线性化最小化方法，给出了可计算的近端算子及收敛性分析，证明了在最小二乘损失下KL指数为$1/2$，迭代线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schatten-$q$拟范数作为非凸秩替代被广泛应用，但其因子化模型存在两个未解决问题：(i) 临界点的误差界未被探索；(ii) $\|\cdot\|_2^q$的近端算子对一般$q$缺乏闭式解，导致算法只能固定使用$q=1/2$或$2/3$。

Method: 首先分析因子化问题临界点的性质，发现Schatten-$q$拟范数相比核范数隐式赋予临界点列正交性；由此引入S-临界点概念，给出可操作的判别准则。然后提出非精确近端交替线性化最小化方法，设计实践中可计算的非精确近端算子及满足非精确性条件的判据。最后在Kurdyka–Łojasiewicz条件下建立全序列收敛性和收敛率保证，并证明最小二乘损失下S-临界点处KL指数为$1/2$。

Result: 证明了全局最优点必为S-临界点，推导了S-临界点与真实矩阵之间的误差界；所提算法具有全序列收敛性和线性收敛率保证；大量数值实验验证了算法有效性和理论性质。

Conclusion: 本文系统解决了Schatten-$q$拟范数因子化模型的两个核心问题，为低秩矩阵恢复提供了理论更完备、算法更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: The Schatten-$q$ quasi-norm is a widely used nonconvex rank surrogate and matrix factorization is an effective approach to reduce computational cost. In this paper, we consider the equivalent group-sparse factorized reformulation of Schatten-$q$ norm regularized low-rank matrix recovery problem. Though this factorized model exhibits favorable performance, two issues remain: (i) the error bound of critical points is unexplored; (ii) the proximal operator of $\|\cdot\|_2^q$ lacks a closed-form solution for general $q$, limiting algorithms to adopt fixed $q$ like $1/2$ or $2/3$. This paper addresses both issues. We investigate the properties of critical points for the factorized problem and show that, compared to nuclear norm, the Schatten-$q$ norm implicitly endows critical points with column orthogonality. From this insight, we introduce the notion of S-critical points under mild conditions that ensure column orthogonality with easily operable criterion for identifying. We show that global optimal points must be S-critical points and we derive an error bound between S-critical points and the true matrix. We further present an inexact proximal alternating linearized minimization method for the factorized problem, along with practically computable inexact proximal operator for $\|\cdot\|_2^q$ and criteria to find solutions satisfying inexactness conditions, and we establish the whole sequence convergence and a convergence rate guarantee under Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz condition. Moreover, we prove that the factorized model with least-squares loss has KL exponent $1/2$ at S-critical points, then the iteration converges linearly under suitable condition. Extensive numerical experiments validate the effectiveness of our algorithm and confirm the theoretical properties of the factorized model.

</details>


### [118] [A Restart-Free Accelerated Algorithm for Non-Convex Minimization: Continuous and Discrete Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30050)
*Kansei Ushiyama,Shun Sato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了两种新的非凸优化一阶方法，在无需重启机制且不预知Lipschitz常数的情况下，达到O(ε^{-7/4})的复杂度，通过ODE离散化推导并基于PEP框架进行收敛分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖重启机制来实现O(ε^{-7/4})复杂度，这导致实现复杂且末次迭代不可微。本文旨在设计无需重启、实现简单且可自适应估计Lipschitz常数的一阶方法。

Method: 通过离散化新引入的常微分方程(ODE)连续时间模型，推导出两种一阶算法；方法一直接实现且末次迭代可微，方法二自适应估计Lipschitz常数；收敛分析采用Performance Estimation Problem (PEP)框架并行进行连续和离散时间分析。

Result: 两种算法均达到O(ε^{-7/4})的函数值和梯度评估复杂度；方法一实现简单且末次迭代可微；方法二无需预知Lipschitz常数且在部分问题上数值表现优于现有无参数方法。

Conclusion: 提出的重启无关设计避免了现有方法的局限，为高效非凸优化提供了新的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: We propose two novel first-order methods for minimizing nonconvex functions with Lipschitz-continuous gradients and Hessians. These algorithms attain an $\varepsilon$-approximate first-order stationary point in $\mathrm{O}(\varepsilon^{-7/4})$ function and gradient evaluations, without using $\varepsilon$ as an input parameter. While existing methods rely on restart mechanisms to achieve this complexity, our methods do not. Consequently, the first algorithm enjoys a simple implementation, making its last iterate differentiable with respect to the initial point. By estimating the Lipschitz constants adaptively, we develop the second algorithm that does not require prior knowledge of the constants. This algorithm exhibits better numerical performance than existing parameter-free methods for certain problems, which can be attributed to its restart-free design. Both algorithms are derived by discretizing a newly introduced continuous-time model represented by an ordinary differential equation, and their continuous- and discrete-time convergence analyses proceed in a parallel manner under the Performance Estimation Problem framework.

</details>


### [119] [Relative Weak Convexity and Projected Subgradient Methods: Analysis and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30138)
*Morteza Rahimi,Masoud Ahookhosh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了相对弱凸函数类，该函数类通过相对于距离生成函数度量非凸性来推广经典弱凸概念。研究了该类函数的基本性质、刻画定理、微积分规则和实例，分析了优化景观并识别了无鞍点的全局最小化集邻域。基于此几何结构，提出了投影次梯度算法(PSGA)及其多种步长策略，在sharpness误差界下证明了从该邻域初始化时算法的全局收敛性，并建立了几何衰减步长策略下的线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典弱凸函数的非凸性度量缺乏灵活性，无法适应不同几何结构的问题。需要一种更一般的函数类，能根据问题结构自适应地度量非凸性，从而扩展可处理优化问题的范围。

Method: (1) 定义了相对弱凸函数，其中非凸性通过距离生成函数来衡量；(2) 建立了该类函数的刻画定理和微积分运算规则；(3) 分析了优化景观，识别出全局极小值集的无鞍点邻域；(4) 提出了投影次梯度算法(PSGA)，设计了多种步长策略；(5) 在sharpness误差界条件下证明了收敛性。

Result: (1) 建立了相对弱凸函数的完整理论框架；(2) 识别了全局最优解集周围的鞍点自由邻域；(3) 提出的PSGA算法在从该邻域初始化时收敛到全局最优解；(4) 几何衰减步长策略实现了线性收敛速率。

Conclusion: 相对弱凸函数类为处理具有特定几何结构的非凸优化问题提供了新工具，PSGA算法在良好初始化条件下具有理论保证的收敛性能，拓展了可收敛到全局最优的非凸优化算法边界。

Abstract: We introduce the class of relatively weakly convex functions, which extends the classical notion of weak convexity by measuring nonconvexity relative to a distance-generating function. We investigate the fundamental properties of this function class, establishing characterization results, calculus rules, and illustrative examples. We further analyze the associated optimization landscape and identify a neighborhood of the set of global minimizers that is free of saddle points. Motivated by this geometric structure, we propose the Projected SubGradient Algorithm (PSGA) with several step-size strategies. Under a sharpness error bound, we prove that, when initialized within this saddle-point-free neighborhood, the iterates generated by PSGA converge to a global minimizer for each of the proposed step-size strategies. Furthermore, linear convergence is established for the geometrically decaying step-size strategy.

</details>


### [120] [A survey of trust-region radius update mechanisms. Part I: First-order analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30202)
*Jérémy Rieussec,Fabian Bastin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了弱可接受和强可接受两类信任域半径更新机制的充要条件，证明了弱可接受性保证梯度收敛，强可接受性保证O(ε⁻²)的最优最坏情况复杂度，并验证了五类经典机制（固定因子、步驱动、回溯、临界锚定、梯度缩放）的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信任域方法中半径更新规则多基于经验设计，缺乏统一的收敛性和复杂度分析框架。本文旨在抽象出信任域半径更新的核心结构条件，从而统一分析不同机制的理论性质。

Method: 通过三个结构条件（梯度范数的下界、不成功迭代的收缩、成功迭代的受控扩张）定义了弱可接受和强可接受机制。利用均匀有界和线性增长的模型Hessian假设，分别证明了收敛性和复杂度。对五类机制（固定因子、步驱动、回溯、临界锚定、梯度缩放）进行了可接受性验证，并扩展了Curtis-Scheinberg和Wang-Yuan框架。

Result: 弱可接受性在均匀有界模型Hessian下保证梯度趋于零；强可接受性在均匀有界下达到O(ε⁻²)最优复杂度，在线性增长模型Hessian下仍保持收敛。五类机制均满足可接受性条件，其中回溯机制在线性增长Hessian下的收敛性为首次证明。

Conclusion: 信任域半径更新机制的结构条件具有普适性，为分析和设计新机制提供了理论框架；强可接受性条件在实际应用中具有最优复杂度保障。

Abstract: We isolate three structural conditions on trust-region radius update rules for smooth unconstrained nonlinear optimisation, and study the class of mechanisms they define. The conditions act on the radius directly: a lower bound relative to the gradient norm, a contraction on unsuccessful iterations, and a controlled expansion on successful ones. A mechanism is \emph{weakly admissible} if it satisfies the first two conditions, and \emph{strongly admissible} if it satisfies the lower bound together with the controlled-expansion condition. Under uniformly bounded model Hessians, weak admissibility yields $\lim_{k\to\infty}\|\nabla f(x_k)\|=0$, and strong admissibility yields the optimal worst-case complexity $O(ε^{-2})$ for first-order stationarity. Strong admissibility extends the convergence guarantee to linearly growing model Hessians. We verify admissibility for five mechanism classes: fixed-factor, step-driven, retrospective, criticality-anchored, and gradient-scaled. Along the way, we prove convergence of the retrospective update under linearly growing model Hessians and revisit the framework of Curtis and Scheinberg (2020), and Wang and Yuan (2022): we extend it to three distinct scaling factors with decoupled step acceptance (covering $η= 0$), and specialise its stochastic version to the deterministic gradient-scaled

</details>


### [121] [Fast Adaptive Tensor Methods Under Local Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30225)
*Sadok Jerad*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的快速自适应正则化方法，在局部Lipschitz光滑条件下，非凸问题达到最优的𝒪(|log(ε)|ε^{-(p+1)/p})复杂度，凸问题达到𝒪(|log(ε)|ε^{-1/p})复杂度，与全局Lipschitz假设下的标准张量方法界相匹配（仅差对数项）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准张量方法通常假设全局Lipschitz光滑性，而实际优化问题往往只满足局部光滑条件。作者希望设计一种自适应正则化方法，在更现实的局部Lipschitz光滑假设下仍能达到理论最优复杂度。

Method: 遵循标准张量方法框架，但采用适当选择的正则化项和相应修改，实现自适应调节以适应局部光滑性，而非依赖全局Lipschitz常数。

Result: 非凸问题：𝒪(|log(ε)|ε^{-(p+1)/p})复杂度达到一阶ε-稳定点；凸问题：𝒪(|log(ε)|ε^{-1/p})迭代次数使最优间隙低于ε。与全局Lipschitz下的标准张量方法界匹配（仅对数项差异）。

Conclusion: 该方法在理论上达到了最优复杂度，数值实验在非凸回归问题上展示了相比标准自适应三次正则化方法的潜力。

Abstract: A new, fast adaptive regularization methods is proposed and analyzed under local Lipschitz smoothness of the $p$-th order tensor. For nonconvex problems, it achieves the optimal $\mathcal{O}\!\left(|\log(ε)|ε^{-(p+1)/p}\right)$ complexity to obtain first-order $ε$-stationary points and in the convex case, it yields $\mathcal{O}\!\left(|\log(ε)|ε^{-1/p}\right)$ iterations to drive the optimality gap below $ε$, thus matching the complexity bounds of standard tensor methods under global Lipschitz smoothness yp to logarithmic terms. The proposed algorithm follows the line of standard tensor methods with an appropriately chosen regularization and suitable modifications. Initial numerical experiments and comparisons for some nonconvex regression problems are made with the standard adaptive cubic regularization where we showcase some potential of the proposed method.

</details>


### [122] [When Natural Variables Are Not Enough: Teaching Integer Programming with Sudoku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30227)
*Aled Williams*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文以数独为教学案例，对比了两种整数线性规划建模方法：二元赋值变量模型（虽不直观但约束简单易扩展）与整数单值变量模型（直观但需大量大M不等式线性化全异约束），并提供了AMPL代码及唯一性认证的整数规划方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过数独这一简洁熟悉的场景，深入教学整数线性规划中的一个重要洞见：最自然的决策变量不一定能产生有效或便利的线性模型。

Method: 对比两种数独的整数线性规划公式：(1) 二元赋值变量模型，使用简单指派约束编码规则；(2) 整数单值变量模型，使用大M不等式线性化全异约束。提供AMPL代码实现，并推导出用于唯一性认证的第二个整数规划。

Result: 二元赋值变量模型虽然从棋盘角度看不太自然，但通过简单指派约束即可编码规则，且易于扩展到杀手数独等变体；整数单值变量模型更自然但需大量大M不等式表达全异约束，导致模型更复杂。

Conclusion: 教学上应用确的数学工具（整数线性规划）和计算复杂性理论来理解数独问题，强调标准9×9数独本身不属于渐近问题类别。

Abstract: Sudoku is a compact and familiar setting for teaching a surprisingly deep lesson in integer linear programming, namely that the most natural decision variables are not always enough to produce an effective or convenient linear model. This paper compares two formulations of Sudoku. The first uses binary assignment variables indicating whether a particular digit is assigned to a particular cell. This formulation is less natural from the perspective of the puzzle board itself, but it encodes the puzzle rules through simple assignment constraints and extends easily to variants such as Killer Sudoku. The second formulation uses the more natural approach of assigning one integer variable to represent the value in each cell, but then the central requirement is that the values in each row, column, and block must be all different. When this all-different requirement must be expressed using linear constraints while retaining the cell-value variables, the formulation becomes a large collection of pairwise disjunctions linearised by big-M inequalities. The paper includes AMPL code for both the assignment model and the natural all-different model, and derives a second integer program for certifying uniqueness. The paper also uses generalised Sudoku as a careful entry point to computational complexity, while emphasising that the standard nine-by-nine puzzle is not itself an asymptotic problem class.

</details>


### [123] [A Distributionally Robust Framework for Learned Reconstructions in Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30230)
*Floor van Maarschalkerwaart,Subhadip Mukherjee,Christoph Brune,Marcello Carioni*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构化分布鲁棒优化（DRO）框架，通过将模糊集约束在与数据采集过程对齐的结构化扰动上（如条件分布P(Y|X)），学习对分布偏移鲁棒的重建算子。该框架相比标准Wasserstein DRO更符合物理测量过程，理论建立了强对偶性和显式对偶表示，核心结果是诱导Tikhonov正则化的最坏情况风险界。数值实验在去模糊和正弦图到CT重建任务上验证了改进的鲁棒性、稳定性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习型逆问题重建算子通常在固定噪声分布下训练，测试时分布发生变化时泛化性能差。标准Wasserstein DRO对联合分布进行均匀扰动，过于保守且忽略测量过程的物理结构。

Method: 提出结构化DRO框架，将模糊集限制在与数据采集过程对齐的结构化扰动上（如P(Y|X)），建模前向算子和噪声模型的不确定性。建立了强对偶性，推导了联合分布、边际分布和条件分布扰动的有限维对偶表示，得到显式最坏情况风险界（诱导Lipschitz常数的Tikhonov正则化）。

Result: 数值实验表明，在去模糊和正弦图到CT重建任务中，所提方法相比标准DRO和MSE基线具有更好的鲁棒性、稳定性和可解释性。在线性设定下，学习到的算子呈现低秩特性，截断在数据的本质维度，恢复了截断SVD正则化的数据驱动类比。

Conclusion: 结构化DRO通过利用测量过程的物理结构，在保持鲁棒性的同时比标准DRO更少保守，为学习鲁棒逆问题重建算子提供了理论上有原则、实践上有效的方法。

Abstract: Learned reconstruction operators for inverse problems are typically trained under a fixed noise model, and generalize poorly when the distribution during testing differs from the one assumed during training. Distributionally robust optimization (DRO) addresses this by optimizing against the worst-case distribution within a prescribed ambiguity set, but standard Wasserstein DRO perturbs the full joint distribution uniformly, which can be overly conservative and ignores the physics of the measurement process. We develop a structured DRO framework in which the ambiguity set is restricted to structured perturbations aligned with the data-acquisition process. This allows us to learn data-driven reconstruction operators that remain robust to distributional shifts. By constraining perturbations to subsets such as $P(Y|X)$, our framework models uncertainty in the forward operator and noise model more faithfully, accommodating any noise model expressible as a stochastic forward operator. We establish strong duality for this general formulation and derive explicit finite-dimensional dual representations for perturbations in the joint, marginal, and conditional distributions. A central result is an explicit worst-case risk bound that induces Tikhonov regularization on the Lipschitz constant of the reconstruction operator, and is less conservative relative to standard DRO for well-posed problems. Numerical experiments on deblurring and sinogram-to-CT reconstruction demonstrate improved robustness, stability, and interpretability over standard DRO and MSE baselines. In the linear setting, the learned operator becomes effectively low-rank, truncating at the intrinsic dimension of the data and recovering a data-driven analogue of truncated-SVD regularization.

</details>


### [124] [Global $o(1/k^2)$ Merit Complexity of Regularized Newton Methods for Convex Multiobjective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30250)
*Yuqia Wu,Yue Wang,Yaohua Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对无约束凸多目标优化问题，提出了一种正则化牛顿方法，证明了在紧致性假设下全局渐近收敛率为$o(1/k^2)$，并构造了一个一维双目标凸族表明该指数2在一致多项式意义上是本质上紧致的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对凸多目标优化的牛顿型方法缺乏全局收敛率分析，尤其是高阶收敛率（如超线性或二次收敛）通常仅在局部成立。本文旨在填补这一空白，为多目标优化的正则化牛顿方法建立全局渐近率保证，并探讨该率的最优性。

Method: 1) 在每个迭代步，最小化局部二次模型的带二次正则化的max-envelope；2) 采用Tanabe型价值函数（merit function）进行分析；3) 在初始分量水平集紧致性假设下证明全局渐近$o(1/k^2)$衰减率；4) 构造一维凸双目标反例族论证指数2的紧致性。

Result: 1) 证明了目标函数的Tanabe型价值函数以$o(1/k^2)$的全局渐近率衰减；2) 该结果在单目标情形下退化为经典结论；3) 构造的反例族表明对于任意固定的$δ>0$，不存在一致的$\mathcal O(k^{-(2+δ)})$估计，因此指数2在一致多项式意义下是紧的。

Conclusion: 本文为正则化牛顿方法在凸多目标优化中建立了全局$o(1/k^2)$收敛率，并证明了该率在一致多项式意义下不可改进，因而在理论上刻画了该方法的最坏情况性能边界。

Abstract: We investigate a regularized Newton method for unconstrained convex multi-objective optimization with twice continuously differentiable objectives whose Hessians are Lipschitz continuous. At each iteration, the method minimizes the quadratically regularized max-envelope of the local quadratic models. Using a Tanabe-type merit function, we prove that this merit decays at the global asymptotic rate $o(1/k^2)$ under the compactness assumption on the initial component-wise lower level set. This result also covers the single-objective case as a special case. Finally, we construct an explicit one-dimensional convex bi-objective family showing that no uniform merit estimate of order $\mathcal O(k^{-(2+δ)})$ can hold for any fixed $δ>0$. Thus the exponent $2$ is essentially sharp in the uniform polynomial sense, despite the $o(1/k^2)$ decay on each fixed trajectory.

</details>


### [125] [Preference-Based Reward Learning under Partial Observability with Inexact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30271)
*Reza Zolnouri,Semih Cayci*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分可观测性和不精确的隐状态推断如何影响基于偏好的奖励学习。针对有限log-linear POMDP和过参数化神经softmax POMDP模型，建立了信念滤波器对参数模型误差的稳定性，推导了轨迹级特征扰动下的奖励估计有限样本保证，将统计误差与不可约的模型失配偏差解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际强化学习任务中，智能体通常面临部分可观测环境，且用于推断隐状态的模型往往存在误差。现有基于偏好的奖励学习方法大多假设完全可观测或精确的状态推断，忽略了模型误差随时间的累积效应。本文旨在阐明在这种不完美条件下奖励学习是否仍然可行，以及误差如何影响学习保证。

Method: 在有限log-linear POMDP框架下，利用混合条件建立信念滤波器对参数模型误差的稳定性，给出期望和高概率下的信念偏差界；将该稳定性机制扩展到过参数化神经网络的神经softmax POMDP模型；将信念误差传播为轨迹级特征扰动，进而推导约束Bradley-Terry奖励估计的有限样本保证。

Result: 建立了部分可观测条件下信念滤波器对模型误差的稳定性界；将统计误差与不可约的模型失配偏差成功解耦；给出了偏好奖励估计的有限样本保证，明确了在何种条件下基于偏好的奖励学习在部分可观测环境中仍然可行。

Conclusion: 偏好奖励学习在部分可观测和模型不精确的条件下仍具有可行性，但存在一个不可约的模型失配偏差，这为实际应用中设计鲁棒的奖励学习方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how partial observability and inexact latent-state inference affect reward learning from preferences. To that end, we study preference-based reward learning under partial observability, where the learner forms latent-state estimates using an inexact learned POMDP model, so model error can accumulate over time. For finite log-linear POMDPs, we characterize this error term by establishing the stability of the belief filter to parametric model error under certain mixing conditions, yielding bounds on the belief mismatch in expectation and in high probability. We further extend this stability mechanism beyond the log-linear setting to neural-softmax POMDP models with overparameterized neural networks. We then propagate these errors into trajectory-level feature perturbations and derive finite-sample guarantees for constrained Bradley--Terry reward estimation from preferences. Our results decouple statistical error from an irreducible model-mismatch bias, and clarify when preference-based reward learning remains feasible under partial observability with imperfect dynamics.

</details>


### [126] [Continuous-Time Information Design for Hurricane Evacuation: Disclosure, Congestion, and Optimal Phasing under Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30320)
*Furkan Sezer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究应急疏散中的连续时间信息设计问题，证明分阶段疏散在信息设计层面具有最优性，并发现了信息层面的Braess悖论效应。以飓风丽塔为案例校准的模型显示，最优策略可将社会成本降低89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统应急疏散研究未充分考虑信息设计（公共预警精度）与分层疏散调度之间的策略性交互，以及信念与拥堵外部性之间的耦合效应。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 构建Stackelberg博弈模型：领导者（应急管理机构）通过信息精度和分层疏散时间表两个杠杆进行信息设计；跟随者（疏散区域）进行容量约束下的拥堵博弈。采用跳跃扩散过程描述飓风潜在演化，利用精确有限维信念滤波器跟踪快速增强期。将问题转化为潜能博弈，进而简化为凸控制问题；领导者问题被刻画为Isaacs方程的唯一粘性解。

Result: ①跟随者博弈可归约为凸控制问题的潜能博弈；②领导者分布鲁棒相对熵问题由Isaacs方程刻画，其值为唯一粘性解；③无转移支付时，一阶条件保留均衡响应项，最优信息设计充当"二级拥堵收费"；④分阶段疏散优于同时预警，分阶段疏散内生出现为最优信息设计；⑤公共信号精度符号模糊，存在"信息Braess效应"——模糊预警在无分层调度时最优；⑥校准至飓风丽塔后，模型重现I-45走廊拥堵，最优策略消除几乎全部途中拥堵暴露，社会成本降低89%，仅分层披露降低70%。

Conclusion: 分阶段疏散在信息设计层面具有内生最优性；信息设计可作为拥堵管理的二级工具；存在信息Braess效应意味着增加信息精度不一定有益；实际疏散管理应同时关注信息精度与调度时序的协同优化。

Abstract: We study continuous-time information design for emergency evacuation, where an Emergency Management Agency (the Stackelberg leader) steers strategic evacuation zones via two levers: public advisory precision (information design) and a tiered release schedule. The latent storm is a jump-diffusion process with publicly observed rapid-intensification epochs tracked by an exact finite-dimensional belief filter. Zones play a capacity-constrained congestion game on shared corridors with belief-weighted hazard exposure. The running cost couples beliefs to a convex congestion externality, making disclosure double-edged: sharper information reduces false-alarm departures but synchronizes genuine ones, and convex congestion penalizes that synchronization. We prove that: (i) the followers' game admits a potential reduction to a convex control problem; (ii) the leader's distributionally robust relative-entropy problem is characterized by an Isaacs equation whose value is the unique viscosity solution, with verification valid for non-smooth bang-bang feedback; and (iii) without transfers, the leader's first-order condition retains an equilibrium-response term, positioning optimal information design as a second-best congestion toll. Structurally, we show that a staggered evacuation order dominates simultaneous advisories; phased evacuation emerges endogenously as optimal information design. Furthermore, public-signal precision is sign-ambiguous due to an informational Braess effect, where vague advisories are optimal unless complemented by a staggered order. Calibrated to Hurricane Rita using NHC archives, TxDOT capacities, and HRRC surveys, the model reproduces the observed gridlock along the Interstate 45 (I-45) evacuation corridor in Texas. The optimal policy removes essentially all in-transit congestion exposure, reducing social cost by 89%, while staggered disclosure alone yields a 70% reduction.

</details>


### [127] [Value Functions of Separable Convex Integer Programs are Periodically Convex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30330)
*Koen Ligthart*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了整数规划中值函数的周期性凸性，证明了对于任意可分凸目标函数，值函数在特定子域上具有周期性凸性，并利用该性质设计了针对块结构整数规划的FPT算法，显著改进了现有算法的运行时间并将可优化目标函数从线性扩展到可分凸函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已知的线性目标函数值函数的周期性凸性（Eisenbrand & Rothvoss, SODA 25）推广到更广泛的可分凸目标函数类别，并利用该推广设计更高效的参数化算法。

Method: 首先证明沿直线的周期性凸性等价于扩张多面体的整数分解性质；然后利用Graver基技术将一维周期性凸性推广到任意固定维度的域上。

Result: 证明了存在仅依赖于约束矩阵A的正整数M，使得对任意可分凸目标函数f，值函数在形式为r+MZ^m的子域上是凸可扩展的；基于该周期性凸性，设计了针对两阶段随机整数规划和n-fold整数规划的FPT算法，在给定参数化下指数级改进了现有算法运行时间，并将可优化目标函数从线性扩展到可分凸。

Conclusion: 周期凸性在整数规划值函数中具有普适性（从线性目标扩展到可分凸目标），并可用于设计更高效的FPT算法。

Abstract: We consider the periodic behavior of the value functions $b\mapsto\min\{f(x)\ \vert\ Ax=b,\,x\in\mathbb Z_{\ge0}^n\}$ of integer programs. We show that there exists a positive integer $M$ depending only on the constraint matrix $A\in\mathbb Z^{m\times n}$ so that the value function is convex extensible on any subdomain of the form $r+M\mathbb Z^m$ for any $r\in\mathbb Z^m$ and any separable convex objective function $f$. With this, we extend the known periodic convexity of such functions for linear objective functions $f$, as established by Eisenbrand and Rothvoss (SODA 25), to the broader class of separable convex objective functions. We derive our main periodic convexity result by first showing that periodic convexity along lines is equivalent to the integer decomposition property of dilated polyhedra. Subsequently, we use Graver basis techniques to extend the $1$-dimensional periodic convexity to domains of arbitrary fixed dimension. We apply this periodic convexity to show that value function reformulations of block-structured integer programs become periodically convex, which yields fixed-parameter tractable (FPT) algorithms. More specifically, we optimize two-stage stochastic integer programs and $n$-fold integer programs in FPT time when parameterized by the block dimensions and coefficient size of the local blocks of the constraint matrix, allowing the coefficients of the global blocks to be large. In the setting of this parameterization, which was recently introduced by Cslovjecsek, Koutecký, Lassota, Pilipczuk, and Polak (TheoretiCS 2025), our algorithms exponentially improve on the running times of the previous optimization algorithms and extend the class of objective functions that can be optimized from linear to separable convex.

</details>


### [128] [Local-Minima-Preserving Continuous Relaxation of Ising Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30333)
*Debraj Banerjee,Santanu Mahapatra,Kunal N. Chaudhury*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对广义伊辛问题中的单翻转局部最小值，构建了一个多项式松弛方法，并证明了景观等价定理（松弛问题的局部最小值与原始伊辛问题的单翻转最小值一一对应），从而将伊辛问题转化为光滑函数的局部最小值求解，可使用ADAM等梯度优化器。实验在自旋玻璃模型、MAX-CUT和NPP等挑战性基准上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义伊辛问题涵盖MAX-CUT、数划分问题（NPP）和最大独立集等众多困难组合优化问题，但直接求解原始伊辛问题的离散局部最小值非常困难。作者希望将离散优化问题转化为连续可微的优化问题，从而利用高效的梯度下降方法进行求解。

Method: 论文构建了一个多项式松弛（polynomial relaxation），并证明了景观等价定理（Landscape Equivalence Theorem），即松弛问题的局部最小值与原始伊辛问题的单翻转局部最小值之间存在一一对应关系。该松弛将离散优化转化为光滑函数的连续优化，从而可以使用Adam等基于梯度的优化器进行求解。

Result: 该方法具有可扩展性，在自旋玻璃模型、MAX-CUT和NPP等挑战性基准测试上取得了强 competitive 的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过构建多项式松弛并证明景观等价定理，将离散的伊辛问题转化为连续可微优化问题，使得梯度优化器可直接用于求解伊辛模型的单翻转局部最小值，为大规模组合优化问题提供了一种新的有效求解范式。

Abstract: The generalized Ising problem captures a broad spectrum of hard combinatorial problems, including MAX-CUT, Number Partitioning (NPP), and Maximum Independent Set. In this work, we consider the notion of one-flip local minima for this problem. We construct a polynomial relaxation and prove the landscape equivalence theorem: there exists a one-to-one correspondence between the local minima of the relaxation and the one-flip minima of the original Ising problem. This guarantee reduces the Ising problem to finding the local minima of a smooth function, allowing us to leverage gradient-based optimizers such as ADAM. We demonstrate that our method is scalable and it achieves strong performance across challenging benchmarks, including spin-glass models, MAX-CUT, and NPP.

</details>


### [129] [Infinite-Horizon Linear-Quadratic Difference Games with Coupled Affine Inequality Constraints: Open-Loop Generalized Nash Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30343)
*Partha Sarathi Mohapatra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类带有耦合仿射不等式约束的确定性无限时域线性二次型差分博弈，推导了开环广义纳什均衡存在的必要条件，并在附加假设下建立了充分性条件，最终将问题转化为大规模线性互补问题的求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对无限时域下带有耦合状态-控制约束的LQ差分博弈的开环广义纳什均衡的理论分析尚不充分，缺乏系统性的必要条件与充分性条件。

Method: 通过关联两个无限时域耦合线性互补系统的平方可和解来推导必要条件与充分性条件，并将均衡计算问题转化为大规模线性互补问题。

Result: 建立了开环广义纳什均衡存在的必要条件与（附加假设下的）充分性条件；将均衡计算归结为大规模线性互补问题求解并需验证附加条件。

Conclusion: 所提理论框架能够有效处理带有耦合约束的无限时域LQ差分博弈，并在车辆队列控制实例中得到验证。

Abstract: In this technical note, we study a class of deterministic infinite-horizon linear-quadratic difference games with coupled affine inequality constraints involving both state and control variables. We derive necessary conditions for the existence of open-loop generalized Nash equilibria and establish their sufficiency under additional assumptions by relating square-summable solutions of two associated infinite-horizon coupled linear complementarity systems. We further reformulate these conditions and show that computing open-loop generalized Nash equilibria reduces to solving a large-scale linear complementarity problem together with verifying additional conditions. Finally, we illustrate our results using a vehicle platooning example with constraints.

</details>


### [130] [A note on the maximal covering location problem with customer preference ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30379)
*Elisabeth Gaar,Markus Sinnl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明带顾客偏好排序的最大覆盖选址问题可轻松转化为经典MCLP，且转化后实例规模更小，因此可直接使用经典MCLP的现有算法求解，实验表明转化后的精确算法求解速度至少提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前一系列论文针对带顾客偏好排序的最大覆盖选址问题提出了混合整数双层优化模型、单层重构以及多种启发式算法，但忽视了该问题与经典MCLP之间的简单转换关系，导致不必要的复杂性。

Method: 将带顾客偏好排序的MCLP实例转化为经典MCLP实例，该转化过程简单且能缩减实例规模，从而可直接使用经典MCLP的既有算法（包括精确算法）求解。

Result: 转化后的实例规模小于原实例，且使用经典MCLP的精确算法求解时，计算速度提升至少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 带顾客偏好排序的最大覆盖选址问题本质上并不比经典MCLP更复杂，无需专门设计复杂的双层模型或专用启发式算法，直接转化为经典MCLP并利用现有算法即可高效求解。

Abstract: Recently a series of papers introduced and investigated the maximal covering location problem with customer preference ordering, a variant of the classical maximal covering location problem (MCLP). In these papers, mixed-integer bilevel optimization models and single-level reformulations were presented for this problem, as well as various heuristics such as a GRASP, a Tabu search and a variable neighborhood search. In this short note we show that instances of this new problem can actually be easily transformed into instances of the classical MCLP and this transformation even reduces the size of the instance. Thus, existing algorithms for the classical MCLP can be used to solve it. We provide a short computational study to show that this transformation leads to speed-ups of at least a magnitude when considering exact algorithms.

</details>


### [131] [Discovering the Kalman-Bucy-Koopman Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30487)
*Umesh Vaidya*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出卡尔曼-布西-库普曼（KBK）滤波器，一种基于Koopman算子谱理论的新型非线性状态估计框架，通过将非线性估计问题转化为Koopman坐标下的Riccati型演化，实现了非线性滤波的线性算子模拟，并证明了其优于扩展卡尔曼滤波的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非线性状态估计问题，克服传统方法（如扩展卡尔曼滤波）依赖显式模型线性化的局限性，寻求一种能够保持非线性结构同时具备线性滤波效率的谱方法。

Method: 将非线性估计问题形式化为最大似然（Mortensen）估计，其解由Hamilton-Jacobi偏微分方程刻画；通过用主Koopman特征函数参数化HJ值函数，将非线性问题转化为Koopman坐标下的Riccati型演化；提出路径积分形式计算特征函数，并引入动力学启发、特征线法启发的基函数构造进行近似。

Result: 推导了值函数和状态估计近似的理论误差界；仿真结果表明KBK滤波器性能优于扩展卡尔曼滤波，并能在无显式模型线性化的数据驱动场景下有效运行。

Conclusion: KBK框架成功地将经典卡尔曼-布西滤波的线性算子思想拓展到非线性领域，为非线性状态估计提供了一种新颖的、可数据驱动的谱方法。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Kalman-Bucy-Koopman (KBK) filter, a novel framework for nonlinear state estimation grounded in Koopman operator spectral theory. The nonlinear estimation problem is formulated as a maximum-likelihood (Mortensen) estimator whose solution is characterized by a Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) partial differential equation. The proposed KBK filter provides a spectral, operator-theoretic realization of this nonlinear filtering problem by parameterizing the HJ value function in terms of principal Koopman eigenfunctions. This transformation converts the nonlinear estimation problem into a Riccati-type evolution in Koopman coordinates, yielding a linear-operator analogue of the classical Kalman-Bucy filter while preserving nonlinear structure in the original state variables. We develop a path-integral formulation for computing principal Koopman eigenfunctions and introduce a dynamics-informed, characteristics-inspired basis construction for their approximation. Theoretical error bounds are derived for value-function and state-estimation approximations. Simulation results demonstrate improved performance over the extended Kalman filter and illustrate the ability of the KBK framework to operate in data-driven settings without explicit model linearization.

</details>


### [132] [Robust McKean--Vlasov Variational Systems with Asymmetric Loss Aversion: Well-Posedness, Stability, and Propagation of Chaos for the Forward and Regularized Backward Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30526)
*Tcheick Kayembe,Rostin Mabela,Jean-Pierre Bosonga,Eugène Mbuyi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类在非占优概率测度族模型不确定性下的鲁棒前向-后向McKean-Vlasov变分系统，通过Wasserstein空间不动点论证、Yosida正则化及Minty-Brézis识别方法，建立了前向动力学的存在唯一性与稳定性，并给出了粒子近似的传播混沌结果及显式收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型不确定性（由非占优概率测度族刻画）下，研究具有平均场相互作用和非光滑损失厌恶的前向-后向McKean-Vlasov变分系统的鲁棒解的存在性、唯一性与稳定性，并建立相应的数值近似理论。

Method: 采用Wasserstein空间中的不动点论证、Yosida正则化、一致先验估计、正则化解收敛性分析以及Minty-Brézis识别论证；在粒子近似中建立传播混沌结果并给出显式收敛率。

Result: 建立了鲁棒前向动力学的存在唯一性与稳定性；构造了粒子近似并证明了前向动力学在非占优族上一致收敛的传播混沌；对正则化后向分量建立了定量传播混沌，并指出在非光滑阈值附近需要额外非接触假设才能获得关于正则化参数一致的结果。

Conclusion: 本文为具有模型不确定性和非对称损失厌恶的平均场变分系统提供了严格的数学理论框架，并为数值逼近奠定了理论基础，同时揭示了非光滑性对一致收敛性估计带来的本质困难。

Abstract: We study a class of robust forward--backward McKean--Vlasov variational systems under model uncertainty represented by a non-dominated family of probability measures. Mean-field interactions are described through nonlinear collective observables acting on the laws of the forward and backward components. To model asymmetric loss aversion, we introduce a nonsmooth convex functional whose subdifferential defines a law-dependent maximal monotone operator acting on the forward state. We establish existence, uniqueness, and stability of the robust forward dynamics by a fixed-point argument in Wasserstein space. The backward component is formulated as a selected backward variational system rather than a classical backward stochastic variational inequality. Our analysis relies on Yosida regularization, uniform a priori estimates, convergence of the regularized solutions, and a Minty--Brézis identification argument, yielding a canonical solution associated with the minimal norm selection. We further construct a particle approximation and prove propagation of chaos for the forward dynamics with explicit convergence rates uniformly over the non-dominated family. For each fixed regularization parameter, we also establish quantitative propagation of chaos for the regularized backward component and explain why estimates uniform in both the number of particles and the regularization parameter require additional non-contact assumptions near the nonsmooth threshold.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Algorithmic exploration of the unit distance problem in the rational plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29415)
*Panteleimon Rodis*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的算法来探索有理平面生成单位距离图，超越了基于网格的结构的局限性，并获得了超越近期理论下界的图密度缩放指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单位距离图生成方法多依赖网格结构，存在限制性假设，无法充分捕获单位距离图的通用性质。需要一种不受生成限制的算法来探索更优的图密度下界。

Method: 采用算法化探索有理平面的方法，通过局部广度优先搜索在有界有限元素集上生成单位距离图，避免了网格结构的限制。

Result: 生成的单位距离图在密度上超越了近期理论下界，缩放指数也优于已有结果。

Conclusion: 该算法有效克服了网格结构的局限性，提供了可复现的实验证据，表明单位距离图密度可以超过当前理论预期。

Abstract: This paper presents reproducible experimental evidence on unit-distance graph density that surpasses recent theoretical lower bounds. Our approach is based on a novel algorithmic exploration of the rational plane for the generation of unit-distance graphs. An efficient algorithm for this utility must perform a local-breadth search on a bounded and finite set of elements and generate a graph that potentially encompasses the general properties of a unit-distance graph, not affected by restrictions on its generation. To this end, we show that our approach accomplishes this purpose by overcoming the limitations of grid-based structures used in the literature for generating unit-distance graphs. Furthermore, the scaling exponent of the generated graph surpasses recent results.

</details>


### [134] [EASE: Parametric garment design with explicit and local ease control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29419)
*Kristijan Bartol,Frieda Hentschel,Nataliya Sadretdinova,Benjamin Russig,Melinos Averkiou,Yordan Kyosev,Stefan Gumhold*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将余量（ease）作为独立设计变量的服装表示方法，通过嵌入人体模型的三角网格和显式各向异性尺度场，实现余量的直接指定、编辑、跨体型迁移和姿势自适应重分布，无需重新运行物理模拟或优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服装设计管线中，余量（松量）通常只是几何或模拟的副产物，而非独立设计变量，导致难以指定、编辑、迁移或重新分布，每次修改都需要重新运行模拟或优化，效率低下。

Method: 在参数化人体模型表面嵌入三角网格，将余量显式表示为随空间变化、各向异性的每三角面片尺度因子。这些尺度作为主要设计变量，将材料余量的指定与物理变形解耦。给定由参数化/用户自定义曲面裁剪和局部尺度场定义的设计后，优化生成满足规定余量分布及几何/缝份约束的缝纫版片。

Result: 实验验证了三种能力：（1）直接在人体表面指定和编辑局部余量；（2）无需重新运行模拟即实现余量分布保真的跨体型迁移；（3）通过重新分布余量缓解高拉伸区域的过度应变，适用于姿态适应场景。优化后余量保持良好，目标姿态下应变显著降低，余量分布准确迁移至目标体型。

Conclusion: 该方法将余量提升为独立设计变量，实现了服装设计的解耦与灵活控制，并以虚拟试穿框架形式实现，使用基于物理的布料模拟进行最终可视化。代码和文档将公开发布。

Abstract: Garment fit and comfort depend critically on ease, the local allowance of excess material relative to the body. In existing design pipelines, ease is typically a byproduct of geometry or simulation rather than an independent design variable, making it difficult to specify, edit, transfer, or redistribute without re-running simulation or optimization. We propose a garment representation that embeds meshes directly on the surface of a parametric human body model and represents ease explicitly as spatially varying, anisotropic per-triangle scales. These scales act as primary design variables, decoupling the specification of material allowance from its physical deformation. Given a design specified by parametric and user-defined surface cuts together with local scale fields, we optimize sewing patterns that enforce the prescribed ease distribution while satisfying geometric and seam constraints. The representation enables three capabilities that are unavailable without explicit ease control: (1) direct specification and editing of local material allowance on the body surface; (2) intent-preserving transfer to new body shapes that reproduces the specified ease distribution without re-running simulation; and (3) intent-modifying pose adaptation that redistributes ease to relieve strain in high-stretch regions. We verify each of these experimentally: ease is closely retained after optimization, excessive strain is significantly mitigated for target poses, and the ease distribution is accurately transferred to target shapes. The approach is implemented as a virtual try-on framework, with physics-based cloth simulation used for final garment visualization. We will publicly release our framework and detailed documentation.

</details>


### [135] [Computing the Integral R2 Indicator by Perspective Mapping and Box Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30530)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双向视角映射，将连续积分R2指标的计算转化为锚定轴对齐盒子并集的积分问题。通过将理想点平移至原点，近似点成为严格正损失向量，下加权切比雪夫包络在权重单纯形上的子图映射到倒数目标空间中锚定盒子并集的补集。雅可比矩阵给出了绝对R2公式作为密度为$(x_1+\cdots+x_N)^{-(N+1)}$的加权补集体积，R2值的差异成为有限加权超体积差异。因此，可复用现有超体积算法的盒子分解，将普通盒子体积替换为闭式加权盒子积分。对于N目标，这给出了对M盒分解的$O(2^N M)$输出敏感开销，固定N时为$O(M)$。利用现有盒子分解方法，积分R2可在$O(n \log n)$（N=2,3）、$O(n^2)$（N=4）及$O(n^{\lfloor (N-1)/2\rfloor+1})$（N≥4）内计算。下界方面，两目标时已有$Ω(n\log n)$下界，对每个固定N≥2同样成立，当N为输入部分时精确计算为#P-hard。该视角映射为在锚定盒子并集与超体积理论及积分R2计算之间转移算法和结构结果提供了强大工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续积分R2指标作为经典有限权向量R2指标的Pareto合规改进，在性能评估、a-posteriori多目标优化的有界归档及数据库天际线选择中具有重要应用。现有方法在计算效率上存在瓶颈，尤其在高维目标空间下缺乏高效的算法框架。本文旨在建立积分R2计算与锚定盒子并集积分之间的理论联系，从而利用超体积算法的成熟成果来加速积分R2的计算。

Method: 提出双向视角映射：将最小化问题的理想点平移至原点，使近似点成为正损失向量；下加权切比雪夫包络在权重单纯形上的子图映射到倒数目标空间中锚定盒子并集的补集。利用雅可比矩阵推导绝对R2公式为加权补集体积，密度函数为$(x_1+\cdots+x_N)^{-(N+1)}$。通过该映射，将超体积算法的盒子分解直接复用于积分R2计算，仅需将普通盒子体积替换为闭式加权积分。

Result: 对于N个目标，算法达到$O(2^N M)$输出敏感开销（M为盒子数），固定N时为$O(M)$。利用现有盒子分解方法，积分R2计算复杂度：N=2,3时为$O(n\log n)$，N=4时为$O(n^2)$，N≥4时为$O(n^{\lfloor (N-1)/2\rfloor+1})$。下界方面，两目标时$Ω(n\log n)$下界成立并推广至每个固定N≥2；当N为输入部分时，精确计算为#P-hard。

Conclusion: 提出的双向视角映射成功建立了积分R2计算与锚定盒子并集积分之间的理论等价性，使得超体积领域的算法和结构成果可直接迁移至积分R2计算。这一方法不仅提供了高效的算法实现路径，也为两个领域的理论交叉奠定了基础。

Abstract: The continuous integral R2 indicator is a Pareto-compliant refinement of the classical finite-weight-vector R2 indicator, used in performance assessment, bounded archiving for a-posteriori multi-objective optimization, and skyline selection in databases. This work introduces a bidirectional perspective mapping between continuous integral R2 computation and integration over unions of anchored axis-aligned boxes. After translating the ideal point of a minimization problem to the origin, approximation points become strictly positive loss vectors, and the subgraph of the lower weighted Tchebycheff envelope over the weight simplex maps to the complement of an anchored-box union in reciprocal objective space. The Jacobian gives an absolute R2 formula as a weighted complement volume with density $(x_1+\cdots+x_N)^{-(N+1)}$, while differences of R2 values become finite weighted hypervolume differences. Hence, hypervolume algorithms that emit box decompositions can be reused by replacing ordinary box volumes with closed-form weighted box integrals. For $N$ objectives, this gives an output-sensitive overhead $O(2^N M)$ for an $M$-box decomposition, or $O(M)$ for fixed $N$. Using existing box-decomposition approaches, the integral R2 can be computed in $O(n \log n)$ for $N=2,3$, in $O(n^2)$ for $N=4$, and in $O\left(n^{\lfloor (N-1)/2\rfloor+1}\right)$ for $N\geq4$, with $n$ denoting the size of the approximation set. On the lower-bound side, exact value computation has an $Ω(n\log n)$ lower bound in the algebraic decision-tree model already in two objectives, this bound lifts to every fixed $N\geq2$, and exact computation is $\#P$-hard when $N$ is part of the input. Together, the proposed perspective mapping provides a powerful tool for transferring algorithmic and structural results between anchored-box union and hypervolume theory and integral R2 computation.

</details>
