### [136] [Classification of Boolean Cubic Forms in Ten Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28473)
*Kirill Khoruzhii,Patrick Gelß,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对GF(2)上10维变量中的布尔三次型进行了完整的GL(10,2)-等价分类，得到了3691560个非零轨道，并给出了每个轨道的代表元、稳定子阶、交替秩及分解。通过极化，首次完整分类了GF(2)上10维交替三线性型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对布尔三次型进行系统分类是计算代数和组合学中的基础问题，此前缺乏对高维（n=10）情形的完整分类结果。

Method: 采用秩分层枚举方法进行分类，利用Burnside轨道计数和轨道-稳定子恒等式独立验证完备性，并构造了快速完全的GL(10,2)-不变量。

Result: 成功分类了GL(10,2)下10变量布尔三次型的所有3691560个非零轨道，提供了每个轨道的代表元、稳定子阶、交替秩及显式分解。

Conclusion: 该分类填补了布尔三次型在10维情形下的空白，并通过极化首次完成了GF(2)上10维交替三线性型的完全分类。

Abstract: We classify Boolean cubic forms in ten variables up to GL(10,2)-equivalence. The catalog contains all 3691560 nonzero orbits. For every orbit we provide a representative with small monomial count, the stabilizer order, and the alternating rank together with an explicit decomposition. The classification is obtained by rank-stratified enumeration. We verify completeness by the Burnside orbit count and independently by the orbit--stabilizer identity. We also provide a fast, complete GL(10,2)-invariant. By polarization, this gives the first complete classification of alternating trilinear forms in dimension 10 over GF(2).

</details>


### [137] [On Brocard's problem with Padovan and Perrin numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28577)
*Eric F. Bravo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明Brocard-Ramanujan方程n!+1=x^2中，当x为Padovan数或Perrin数时，唯一解为(n,x)=(4,5)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Padovan数列和Perrin数列与阶乘相关的指数丢番图方程的解的存在性，填补该领域的研究空白。

Method: 利用三元递推数列的性质（Padovan和Perrin数列，均由多项式X^3-X-1生成），结合阶乘n!的算术性质和数论方法，对方程进行求解和约束。

Result: 对于两类数列，Brocard-Ramanujan方程n!+1=x^2的唯一解均为(n,x)=(4,5)。

Conclusion: Padovan数和Perrin数中仅存在一组平凡解满足Brocard-Ramanujan方程，推广了该类方程的解集研究。

Abstract: The Padovan sequence $\{P_{m}\}_{m\ge 0}$ is a ternary recurrence sequence with companion polynomial $X^{3}-X-1$ and initial conditions $P_{0}=P_{1}=P_{2}=1$. The Perrin sequence $\{R_{m}\}_{m\ge 0}$ is defined by the same companion polynomial as the Padovan sequence, but has initial values $R_{0}=3$, $R_{1}=0$, and $R_{2}=2$. We solve the Brocard-Ramanujan equation $n!+1=x^{2}$, where $n!$ is the factorial of $n$ and $x$ is a Padovan number or a Perrin number. In both cases, we prove that $(n,x)=(4,5)$ is the only solution.

</details>


### [138] [Riesz Means of Quadratic Class Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28657)
*Olivia Beckwith,Tushar Karmakar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Hurwitz类数和实二次类数的加权Riesz均值的渐近公式，通过引入权重1/2的适度增长半调和Maass形式的L-函数，并推广了mock模形式的Riesz均值公式，最终应用于Duke-Imamoglu-Tóth引入的半调和Maass形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Hurwitz类数和实二次类数的加权Riesz均值的渐近公式，推广已有mock模形式的Riesz均值结果至更一般的半调和Maass形式。

Method: 引入权重1/2的适度增长半调和Maass形式的L-函数，推导对应的广义mock模形式的Riesz均值公式，并将该公式应用于Duke-Imamoglu-Tóth的特定半调和Maass形式。

Result: 得到了Hurwitz类数和实二次类数的加权Riesz均值的渐近公式。

Conclusion: 成功将mock模形式的Riesz均值结果推广至半调和Maass形式，并为类数研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove an asymptotic formula for a weighted Riesz mean of Hurwitz class numbers and real quadratic class numbers. To do this, we introduce L-functions for weight $\frac {1}{2} $ sesquiharmonic Maass forms of moderate growth and prove a formula for the Riesz means of the corresponding generalized mock modular forms, generalizing a recent result of the first author with Diamantis, Gupta, Rolen, and Thalagoda for mock modular forms. We then apply this formula to a sesquiharmonic Maass form that was first introduced by Duke, Imamoglu, and Tóth.

</details>


### [139] [To be or not to be local](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28818)
*Christophe Breuil,Florian Herzig,Yongquan Hu,Karol Koziol,Stefano Morra,Benjamin Schraen,Sug Woo Shin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究GL₂(K)的模p光滑表示π是否仅依赖于底层伽罗瓦表示ρ̄这一局部性问题，特别在[K:Qₚ]=2时，利用完美空间几何构造了与ρ̄相关的无限维模p光滑表示，并推测其为π中不可约超奇异子商在Borel子群上的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在K为Qₚ的有限非分歧扩张时，Shimura曲线的模p上同调中出现的GL₂(K)光滑表示π是否仅由其底层2维伽罗瓦表示ρ̄决定，即局部性问题的推广。

Method: 受K=Qₚ情形的启发，采用完美空间几何（perfectoid geometry）技术，在[K:Qₚ]=2时，从ρ̄出发构造一个无限维模p光滑表示，并将其与π的超奇异子商建立联系。

Result: 对扩张次数为2的情形，成功构造了依赖于ρ̄的无限维模p光滑表示，该表示被推测为π的不可约超奇异子商在⾏矩阵群(K^× K;0 1)上的限制。

Conclusion: 本文为局部Langlands对应在模p情形的推广提供了新思路，尤其是通过完美空间几何工具将伽罗瓦表示与光滑表示直接关联，但完全解决局部性问题仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime number and $K$ a finite unramified extension of $\mathbf{Q}_p$. For a smooth representation $π$ of $\mathrm{GL}_2(K)$ occurring in some Hecke eigenspace of the mod $p$ cohomology of a Shimura curve, we explore different strategies (inspired by the case $K=\mathbf{Q}_p$) to attack the locality question: does $π$ depend only on the underlying $2$-dimensional representation $\overlineρ$ of ${\rm Gal}(\overline K/K)$? In particular when $[K:\mathbf{Q}_p]=2$, crucially using perfectoid geometry, we associate to $\overlineρ$ an infinite-dimensional mod $p$ smooth representation of $\begin{pmatrix}K^\times&K\\0&1\end{pmatrix}$ which we hope is the restriction to $\begin{pmatrix}K^\times&K\\0&1\end{pmatrix}$ of the (irreducible) supersingular subquotient of $π$.

</details>


### [140] [On Carmichael numbers of the form $2^np^m+1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28829)
*Florian Luca*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了当固定奇数 m≥5 时，形如 2^np^m+1 的卡尔迈克尔数只有有限多个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究特定形式的卡尔迈克尔数的有限性问题，特别是对于形如 2^np^m+1 的数，其中 m 为固定奇数且至少为5。

Method: 采用数论分析方法，利用卡尔迈克尔数的性质以及指数和模运算的条件进行推导。

Result: 若 m≥5 为固定奇数，则形如 2^np^m+1（n为正整数，p为素数）的卡尔迈克尔数仅有有限个。

Conclusion: 该结果限制了特定指数形式下卡尔迈克尔数的存在性，对理解卡尔迈克尔数的分布有所贡献。

Abstract: Here, we show that if $m\ge 5$ is fixed and odd, then there are only finitely many Carmichael numbers of the form $2^np^m+1$ for positive integers $n$ and prime $p$.

</details>


### [141] [On the Monotonicity of Higher-Fold Representation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28849)
*Csaba Sándor,Quan-Hui Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了集合B的h重表示计数函数R_{B,h}(n)的差分增长，证明了对于h≥3，R_{B,h}(n+1)-R_{B,h}(n)具有n^{h-2}阶多项式界，并构造了一个余有限集C使得R_{C,h}(n)严格递增，解决了Dombi在2002年提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shallit证明了R_{B,3}(n)严格递增，推翻了Dombi 2002年的猜想。本文旨在将这一结果推广到更高阶（h≥3），并回答Dombi提出的关于是否存在余有限集使得所有h≥3的表示计数函数严格递增的问题。

Method: 利用R_{B,3}(n+1)-R_{B,3}(n)的线性界以及卷积论证，推广到一般h≥3的情形；通过构造特定集合C来满足严格递增性质。

Result: 对于每个h≥3，存在常数c_h, C_h>0，使得c_h n^{h-2} ≤ R_{B,h}(n+1)-R_{B,h}(n) ≤ C_h n^{h-2}对所有n≥1成立；构造了一个满足lim_{n→∞}C(n)/n=1的余有限集C，使得R_{C,h}(n)对所有h≥3严格递增。

Conclusion: 解决了Dombi 2002年提出的问题，并提出了若干进一步研究的问题。

Abstract: For a positive integer $h$, let $R_{A,h}(n)$ denote the number of ordered representations $n=s_1+\cdots+s_h$ with all $s_i\in A$. Let \[ B=\{0\}\cup\{m\ge 1:\text{ the base-4 expansion of }m\text{ begins with }1\text{ or }2\}. \] Shallit proved that $R_{B,3}(n)$ is strictly increasing, thereby disproving a 2002 conjecture of Dombi. In this paper, by using linear bounds for $R_{B,3}(n+1)-R_{B,3}(n)$ and a convolution argument, we prove the polynomial order of $R_{B,h}(n+1)-R_{B,h}(n)$ for every integer $h\ge 3$. More precisely, for every integer $h\ge 3$, there exist constants $c_h,C_h>0$, depending only on $h$, such that \[
  c_h n^{h-2}\le R_{B,h}(n+1)-R_{B,h}(n)\le C_h n^{h-2} \] for all integers $n\ge 1$. We also construct a co-infinite set $C\subset\mathbb N$ satisfying $\lim_{n\to\infty}C(n)/n=1$ such that $R_{C,h}(n)$ is strictly increasing for every integer $h\ge 3$. This answers a problem of Dombi posed in 2002. We also pose some problems for further research.

</details>


### [142] [Maximal Gaps for Dilated Lacunary Integer Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28860)
*Yuval Peres,Bohan Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了lacunary序列（a_n）在环面T上生成的有限轨道{a_1x,...,a_Nx}的最大空圆周间隙G_N(x)。证明了对Lebesgue几乎处处x，极限下界为1/2，上界为(q+1)/(q-1)；若满足可整除条件a_n|a_{n+1}，则极限为1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究lacunary序列在环面上生成轨道的间隙分布规律，特别是最大空圆周间隙的渐近行为。

Method: 采用测度论和数论方法，对lacunary序列满足a_{n+1}≥q a_n（q>1）的条件，分析Lebesgue几乎处处x下G_N(x)的极限上下界；进一步在可整除条件下精确化结果。

Result: 对于一般lacunary序列，liminf NG_N(x)/log N ≥ 1/2，limsup ≤ (q+1)/(q-1)；若a_n|a_{n+1}，则极限等于1（几乎处处）。

Conclusion: Lacunary序列的轨道最大空间隙具有对数阶增长，且整除条件可导致精确极限值1。

Abstract: Let \((a_n)_{n\ge1}\subset\mathbb{N}\) be a lacunary sequence, \(a_{n+1}\ge q a_n\) for \(q>1\). For \(x\in\mathbb{T}\), we study the maximal empty circular gap \(G_N(x)\) of the finite orbit \(\{a_1x,\ldots,a_Nx\}\). We prove that, for Lebesgue-almost every \(x\), \[
  \frac{1}{2}
  \le \liminf_{N\to\infty}\frac{NG_N(x)}{\log N}
  \le \limsup_{N\to\infty}\frac{NG_N(x)}{\log N}
  \le \frac{q+1}{q-1}\,. \] If, in addition, \(a_n\mid a_{n+1}\) for every \(n\), then this can be improved to \[
  \lim_{N\to\infty}\frac{NG_N(x)}{\log N}=1 \] for Lebesgue-almost every \(x\).

</details>


### [143] [Bessel Distributions and Kloosterman Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29852)
*Li Cai,Jingsong Chai,Yadi Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了p-adic域上分裂约化群G的Kloosterman积分的Germ展开，并利用此结果证明了若G的所有Levi子群的Kloosterman和具有非平凡界，则Bessel分布在所有一般表示上都是正则的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为一般线性群以外的约化群建立Bessel分布的正则性，这需要将Kloosterman积分进行Germ展开，并建立与Kloosterman和界限的联系。

Method: 给出分裂约化群上Kloosterman积分的Germ展开，利用该展开将Bessel分布的正则性问题归结为Levi子群上Kloosterman和的非平凡界限条件。

Result: 证明了在所有Levi子群的Kloosterman和具有非平凡界的条件下，Bessel分布在所有一般表示上都是正则的。

Conclusion: Kloosterman积分的Germ展开是证明Bessel分布正则性的关键工具，而该正则性依赖于Kloosterman和的界限性质。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a split reductive group over a $p$-adic field. We give germ expansions of Kloosterman integrals for $G$. As an application, we prove that Bessel distributions are regular for all generic representations on $G$ provided that Kloosterman sums for any Levi subgroups of $G$ have nontrivial bounds.

</details>


### [144] [An Explicit Cubic Ramanujan--Sato Series for $1/π$ on $Γ_0(2)^+$ at $D=-163$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28966)
*Vedran Menđušić*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个关于 $1/π$ 的显式三次Ramanujan-Sato公式，该公式基于 $Γ_0(2)^+$ 在 $D=-163$ 时的构造，产生了约15.01位十进制数字的几何收缩，略优于经典的Chudnovsky公式，但系数位于三次代数域中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找比经典Chudnovsky公式（每次迭代产生约14位十进制数字）更高效的 $1/π$ 级数展开公式，通过引入三次Ramanujan-Sato类型公式进一步提升收敛速度。

Method: 利用模群 $Γ_0(2)^+$ 和虚二次域判别式 $D=-163$（即Q(√-163)）的CM（复乘法）理论，构造显式的三次Ramanujan-Sato公式，得到极小的三次CM参数。

Result: 该公式每次迭代产生约15.01位十进制数字的几何收缩，收敛速度略快于Chudnovsky公式（约14位/项）；但系数位于三次代数域而非有理整数中。

Conclusion: 该公式在收敛速度上超越了经典的Chudnovsky公式，但代价是系数复杂度从有理整数提升到三次代数域，这可能在计算实现中带来额外开销。

Abstract: An explicit cubic Ramanujan--Sato formula for $1/π$ on $Γ_0(2)^+$ at $D=-163$ is presented. The construction produces a very small cubic CM parameter, giving about $15.01$ decimal digits of geometric contraction per term. This is slightly sharper than the classical Chudnovsky contraction, but the coefficients lie in a cubic algebraic field.

</details>


### [145] [Moment Estimates and Discrepancy for Sums of Square Roots Modulo One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28986)
*Yixiu Xiao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了形如√a₁+⋯+√aₖ的k重平方根和的模1分布，通过建立S(h,n)的二阶和四阶矩估计，结合指数和估计与Erdős–Turán不等式，得到了关于任意子区间偏差的上界D_k(n)≤n^{-ρ_k+o(1)}，其中ρ_k=(71k+26)/(26k+116)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究k≥2固定时，k个平方根之和模1的分布均匀性问题，这是指数和与均匀分布理论中的重要课题。

Method: 1) 证明S(h,n)的二阶矩估计∑_{H/2≤h≤H}|S(h,n)|²≪_{\varepsilon,δ} Hn^{1+\varepsilon}，对H≥n^{1/2+δ}一致成立；2) 证明S(h,n)的四阶矩估计∑_{H/2≤h≤H}|S(h,n)|^4≪_{\varepsilon,δ} Hn^{2+\varepsilon}，对n^{1/2+δ}≤H≤n^{2/3}一致成立；3) 将二阶矩界与点态指数和估计及Erdős–Turán不等式结合，推导偏差上界。

Result: 建立了匹配对角尺度（至多n^ε因子）的二阶和四阶矩估计，并得到偏差上界D_k(n)≤n^{-ρ_k+o(1)}，ρ_k=(71k+26)/(26k+116)。

Conclusion: 通过矩估计方法改进了对平方根和模1分布偏差的理解，给出了明确的指数上界。

Abstract: Let $k\ge 2$ be fixed. We study the distribution modulo one of the $n^k$ sums \begin{equation*} \sqrt{a_1} + \cdots + \sqrt{a_k}, \qquad 1\le a_1, \dots, a_k \le n, \end{equation*} counted with multiplicity. For \begin{equation*} S(h,n) = \sum_{n/2\le a\le n} \mathbf{e}(h\sqrt{a}), \qquad \mathbf{e}(x) = \exp(2πi x), \end{equation*} we prove second- and fourth-moment estimates matching the diagonal scale up to a factor $n^\varepsilon$. More precisely, \begin{equation*} \sum_{H/2\le h\le H} \left| S(h,n) \right|^2 \ll_{\varepsilon,δ} Hn^{1+\varepsilon} \end{equation*} uniformly for $H\ge n^{1/2+δ}$, and \begin{equation*} \sum_{H/2\le h\le H} \left| S(h,n) \right|^4 \ll_{\varepsilon,δ} Hn^{2+\varepsilon} \end{equation*} uniformly for $n^{1/2+δ} \le H \le n^{2/3}$, where $0<δ<1/6$ in the fourth-moment estimate. Combining the second-moment bound with pointwise exponential-sum estimates and the Erdős--Turán inequality, we obtain \begin{equation*} D_k(n) \le n^{-ρ_k+o(1)}, \qquad ρ_k = \frac{71k+26}{26k+116}, \end{equation*} as $n\to\infty$, where $D_k(n)$ denotes the discrepancy with respect to arbitrary subintervals of $[0,1)$.

</details>


### [146] [Point counts of abelian varieties over finite fields determining their zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28989)
*Shiva Chidambaram,Timo Keller*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 对于有限域上的阿贝尔簇，当q相对于维数g足够大时，前g个点计数即可确定其zeta函数（即Frobenius特征多项式），从而确定其等元类。该界在g=2和g=4时最优，但g=3时两个点计数已足够，而单个计数永远不够。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上阿贝尔簇的zeta函数需要多少个点计数才能唯一确定，即探讨点计数与等元类之间的信息充分性。

Method: 结合L-多项式的函数方程与Newton恒等式，通过归纳误差分析控制逆Frobenius特征值的幂和，使其精度足以通过四舍五入恢复为整数。

Result: 当q相对于g足够大时，g个点计数（#A(F_{q^i})，i=1,...,g）可确定zeta函数；g=2和g=4时该界最优，g=3时2个点计数已足够，1个永远不够。

Conclusion: 给出了由点计数恢复阿贝尔簇等元类所需的最小计数个数及充分条件，揭示了维数与点计数信息量之间的精确关系。

Abstract: Let $A$ be an abelian variety of dimension $g$ over a finite field $\mathbf{F}_q$. We show that if $q$ is sufficiently large relative to $g$, the $g$ point counts $\#A(\mathbf{F}_{q^i})$ for $1 \leq i \leq g$ determine the zeta function of $A$, equivalently the characteristic polynomial of its Frobenius endomorphism, and hence the isogeny class of $A$. This count is best possible for $g=2$ and $g=4$, but not in general: for $g=3$ two point counts already determine the zeta function, whereas a single count never does. The proof combines the functional equation of the $L$-polynomial with Newton's identities and an inductive error analysis that controls the power sums of the inverse Frobenius eigenvalues with enough precision to recover them, as integers, by rounding.

</details>


### [147] [Distribution of random multiplicative functions in short intervals, with proper normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29040)
*Adam J. Harper,Kannan Soundararajan,Max Wenqiang Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Steinhaus随机乘性函数在短区间上的部分和极限分布。当区间长度y→∞但y=o(x)时，经过适当归一化，极限分布为高斯分布。关键发现是当y非常接近x时，归一化因子不同于标准差√y；而当y与x同阶时，不存在使极限分布为非退化高斯的归一化方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机乘性函数在短区间上的部分和统计行为，特别是确定在何种条件下极限分布为高斯分布，以及归一化因子如何随区间长度变化。

Method: 利用概率数论方法，分析Steinhaus随机乘性函数在短区间[x,x+y]上的部分和，研究y→∞但y=o(x)条件下的极限分布，通过适当的归一化处理得到高斯极限。

Result: 当y→∞且y=o(x)时，部分和经适当归一化后极限分布为高斯分布。当y非常接近x时，归一化因子与标准偏差√y不同。当y与x同阶（y≍x）时，不存在能使极限分布为非退化高斯的归一化方式。

Conclusion: Steinhaus随机乘性函数短区间部分和的极限分布行为依赖于区间长度y与x的相对大小，在y增长但远小于x时存在高斯极限，但归一化因子需根据y接近x的程度进行调整。

Abstract: We determine the limiting distribution of partial sums of a Steinhaus random multiplicative function $\sum_{x\le n \le x+y} f(n)$ over short intervals $[x, x+y]$, where $y \rightarrow \infty$ but $y=o(x)$. We show that with appropriate normalization, the limiting distribution is Gaussian for all such $y$. A key new feature of our result is that the normalization factor is different from the standard deviation $\sqrt{y}$ when $y$ is very close to $x$. In contrast, when $y \asymp x$ there is no normalization for which the limiting distribution is a non-degenerate Gaussian.

</details>


### [148] [On Dense Orbit Transversality for Endomorphisms of Abelian Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29057)
*Kaiwen Lu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了数域上光滑射影簇上自映射的轨道分布，证明了在任意阿贝尔簇上存在稠密的代表元集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Pasten和Silverman关于轨道代表元集合Zariski稠密性的结果，从几何单阿贝尔簇推广到所有阿贝尔簇。

Method: 利用数域上阿贝尔簇的结构性质和自映射的动力学性质，通过构造性方法证明存在稠密的代表元集合。

Result: 对于所有阿贝尔簇（不仅限于几何单的情形），均存在稠密的(f,K)-轨道代表元集合。

Conclusion: 成功将Pasten和Silverman的结果从几何单阿贝尔簇推广到一般阿贝尔簇，完善了该方向的理论。

Abstract: Let $X/K$ be a smooth projective variety defined over a number field and $f:X\to X$ be a morphism defined over $K$. Assuming there exists a point in $X(K)$ whose $f$-orbit is Zariski dense in $X$ and up to replacing $K$ by a finite extension, Pasten and Silverman studied the distribution of grand $(f,K)$-orbits and proved that many sets of representatives of grand $(f,K)$-orbits on various classes of varieties are Zariski dense. In particular, they showed that if $X$ is a geometrically simple abelian variety, then all such sets of representatives are Zariski dense. We demonstrate the existence of a dense set of representatives for maps on all abelian varieties.

</details>


### [149] [A Resolution of Erdős Problem 731 under Dyadic Regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29062)
*Eric Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Erdos问题731，证明了在二进区间上二项式系数中位数$A(n)$的对数渐近行为为$\sqrt{(\log2)\log n}+\frac14\log\log n+O_{\rm dens}(1)$，并证明了不存在二进正则的确定性尺度使$A(n)/f(n)\to1$在自然密度下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Erdos问题731，即在"合理"的二进正则形式化下，研究$A(n)$（不整除$\binom{2n}{n}$的最小正整数）的分布规律和渐近行为。

Method: 采用显式二进正则形式化，保留精确的最小公倍数整除条件，用移动基受限数字方差定理替代启发式的跨基独立性假设。

Result: 证明了在二进区间上$A(n)$的分布概率界限，得出$\log A(n)=\sqrt{(\log2)\log n}+\frac14\log\log n+O_{\rm dens}(1)$，并证明了二进非集中性质。

Conclusion: 解决了Erdos问题731，建立了$A(n)$的渐近公式，并证明了不存在二进正则的确定性尺度函数能使$A(n)$与其比值趋近于1。

Abstract: We resolve Erdos Problem 731 under the explicit dyadic-regularity formalization of "reasonable." Let $A(n)$ be the least positive integer not dividing $\binom{2n}{n}$. On dyadic intervals $X\le n<2X$, put $L=\log(2X)$ and ${\mathcal F}_X=\sqrt2(\log2)^{1/4}L^{1/4}\exp\sqrt{(\log2)L}$. Uniformly for $1\le z\le Z(X)=o(L^{1/4})$, we prove ${\mathbb P}_X(A(n)\le {\mathcal F}_X\exp(-z))\asymp \exp(-2z)$ and ${\mathbb P}_X(A(n)>{\mathcal F}_X\exp(z))\ll \exp(-2z)$. Consequently $\log A(n)=\sqrt{(\log2)\log n}+\frac14\log\log n+O_{\rm dens}(1)$. We also prove dyadic nonconcentration: no scalar center on a large dyadic block, and hence no dyadically regular deterministic scale $f$, can satisfy $A(n)/f(n)\to1$ in natural density. The proof retains the exact least-common-multiple divisibility condition and replaces heuristic cross-base independence by a moving-base restricted-digit variance theorem.

</details>


### [150] [Gap-Sums via Quasi-Arithmetic Means with Applications to Fibonacci and Lucas Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29168)
*Omid Khormali,Ghaya Mtimet,Nuh Aydin,Mohammad K. Azarian*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一框架研究递增整数序列中连续项之间缺失整数的间隙和，将Barry的算术间隙和扩展到几何与调和情形，并证明了一般稀疏求和定理。在三种毕达哥拉斯均值上的特化分别恢复、推导了显式公式。在几何情形导出Fuss-Catalan数的乘积恒等式；在调和情形应用于Fibonacci和Lucas序列，证明了调和间隙和以指数速度收敛于ln(α)，推导了倒Fibonacci/Lucas级数尾部的显式双项渐近展开，并建立了精确误差项的渐近公式。进一步得到了Euler常数γ与Fibonacci/Lucas指标处调和数的级数恒等式，以及倒Fibonacci常数ψ与倒Lucas常数ψ_L之间的新关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究整数序列中连续项之间的缺失整数提供一个统一的数学框架，将算术间隙和概念推广到几何与调和情形，并探索其在特殊序列（如Fibonacci、Lucas数列）及常数（Euler常数、黄金分割比等）中的应用。

Method: 利用拟算术均值理论统一刻画三种间隙和，证明一般稀疏求和定理；通过特化到算术、几何、调和均值分别推导显式公式；结合生成函数、渐近分析及经典展开（如Hardy-Wright调和数展开）进行渐近与恒等式推导。

Result: 1) 三种间隙和统一解释为间隙大小乘以缺失整数的相应均值；2) 恢复al-Kāshī算术公式，得到几何与调和情形的显式公式；3) 几何情形导出Fuss-Catalan数乘积恒等式；4) Fibonacci/Lucas的调和间隙和指数收敛于ln(α)；5) 倒Fibonacci/Lucas级数尾部双项渐近展开；6) H_{F_n} ~ n ln(α)及H_{L_n} ~ n ln(α)带显式O(1)误差项；7) Euler常数γ的新级数恒等式；8) 倒Fibonacci常数ψ与倒Lucas常数ψ_L的新关系式。

Conclusion: 本文建立的统一框架不仅统一了算术、几何、调和三种间隙和，还揭示了其与均值理论的深层联系，并通过Fibonacci/Lucas序列的应用展示了调和间隙和在渐近分析和数论常数推导中的强大效用。

Abstract: We develop a unified framework for studying the integers missing between consecutive terms of an increasing integer sequence, extending Barry's arithmetic gap-sum to geometric and harmonic analogues via the theory of quasi-arithmetic means. All three gap-sums admit a common interpretation: each equals the gap size multiplied by the appropriate mean of the missing integers. Building on this, we prove a general sparse summation theorem expressing the sum of a strictly monotonic function over a sparse integer sequence as the full range sum corrected by the gap-sums of the missing portions. Specializing on the three Pythagorean means recovers a classical formula of al-Kāshī from the fifteenth century in the arithmetic case, and yields explicit formulas in the geometric and harmonic cases. As a concrete application of the geometric case, we derive a product identity involving the Fuss--Catalan numbers. Applying the harmonic case to the Fibonacci and Lucas sequences, we establish that the harmonic gap-sum converges to $\ln(α)$ exponentially, where $α$ is the golden ratio, and derive explicit two-term asymptotic expansions for the tails of the reciprocal Fibonacci and Lucas series with closed-form coefficients, and establish the asymptotic formula $H_{u_n} \sim n\ln(α)$ for both $u_n = F_n$ and $u_n = L_n$, with explicit $O(1)$ error terms that differ due to their distinct initial conditions. As a further consequence, by comparing the gap-sum expansions with the classical Hardy--Wright expansion of harmonic numbers, we derive exact series identities expressing Euler's constant $γ$ in terms of harmonic numbers at Fibonacci and Lucas indices, and obtain a new identity relating the reciprocal Fibonacci constant $ψ$ and the reciprocal Lucas constant $ψ_L$.

</details>


### [151] [On the diffraction spectrum of the set of visible points in lattices and certain cut-and-project sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30132)
*Rishi Kumar,Carlos Ospina*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了从原点可见的格点集合（即$\mathbb{Z}^k$中与原点坐标互质的点集）的衍射性质，并将其推广到从有限个格点集合同时可见的点集，以及某些cut-and-project集合中从原点可见的点集，证明了这些集合都具有平移有界的纯点衍射谱，并给出了衍射系数的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知从原点可见的格点集具有平移有界的纯点衍射谱，本文旨在将其推广到更一般的情形：从有限个格点同时可见的点集，以及某些cut-and-project集合中的可见点集，并研究其衍射性质。

Method: 通过分析从有限个格点同时可见的点集的结构，推导了其衍射谱系数的显式公式。然后将方法推广到特定类的cut-and-project集合中从原点可见的点集。

Result: 证明了从有限个格点同时可见的点集具有平移有界的纯点衍射谱，并给出了衍射系数的显式公式。进一步证明了某些cut-and-project集合中从原点可见的点集也具有平移有界的纯点衍射谱。

Conclusion: 本文将可见格点集的衍射性质从单一原点情形推广到了多点和更一般的cut-and-project集合结构，丰富了准晶和点集衍射理论的结果。

Abstract: Let $k\geq 2$ be a positive integer. It is known that the set of visible lattice points from the origin in $\mathbb{Z}^k$ has a translation bounded pure point diffraction spectrum. We investigate these properties for sets of points simultaneously visible from a finite set of lattice points $ \{\mathbf{x}_1,\dots,\mathbf{x}_n\} \subseteq \mathbb{Z}^k$. We provide explicit formulas for the coefficients of the diffraction spectrum. Additionally, we generalize our procedure to show that the set of visible points from the origin in certain classes of cut-and-project sets has a translation bounded pure point diffraction spectrum.

</details>


### [152] [The van der Corput property for sums of two squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29185)
*Steve Fan,Andrew Lott*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了S_N（可表示为两个平方数之和的整数集合）的van der Corput性质的一个幂次节省形式，并由此得到了一个强Sárközy型结果：若A⊆[N]中任意两个数的差都不等于两个平方数之和，则|A|≪_ε N^{7/8+ε}，改进了Rice之前拟多项式界。该界的形状是最优的，因为Younis构造了一个|A|≫N^{1/2}且满足(A-A)∩S_N=∅的集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进对集合A⊆[N]中无平方和差问题的上界估计，此前Rice仅得到拟多项式界，本文希望得到多项式形式的更优界。

Method: 证明S_N的van der Corput性质的幂次节省形式，利用该性质推导出Sárközy型结果的上界。

Result: 得到|A|≪_ε N^{7/8+ε}的上界，并指出形状最优（匹配下界N^{1/2}）。

Conclusion: 本文给出了平方和差无集问题的一个多项式上界，且该上界的指数7/8在形状上已达到最优，但上下界之间的指数差距仍存在（7/8 vs 1/2）。

Abstract: Let $S_N=\{1\le d\le N:d=x^2+y^2\text{ for some }x,y\in\mathbb Z\}.$ We prove a power-saving form of the van der Corput property for $S_N$. As a consequence, we obtain a strong Sárközy-type result: if $A\subseteq [N]$ has no nonzero difference equal to a sum of two squares, then $|A|\ll_\varepsilon N^{7/8+\varepsilon}$ for every $ε>0$, improving upon an earlier quasipolynomial bound due to Rice. The shape of this bound is optimal, as a construction of Younis yields a set $A\subseteq [N]$ with $|A|\gg N^{1/2}$ such that $(A-A)\cap S_N=\emptyset$.

</details>


### [153] [Procounting measures and the Bateman--Horn conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29250)
*Luca Demangos,Ignazio Longhi,Francesco Maria Saettone*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了全局域上S-整数环D的profinite完备化上的Bateman-Horn猜想的profinite版本，并基于新提出的"procounting measure"概念（作为R上计数函数的profinite类比）与经典猜想进行了首次比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Bateman-Horn猜想及其推广在数论中具有重要地位，但在profinite完备化这一算术对象上尚未有对应版本。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立profinite框架下的Bateman-Horn猜想。

Method: 引入"procounting measure"这一新概念，作为实轴上计数函数的profinite类比，用以处理Haar测度为0的profinite子集（对应于R中密度为零的子集），从而建立profinite版本的Bateman-Horn猜想。

Result: 成功提出了全局域S-整数环的profinite完备化上的Bateman-Horn猜想的profinite版本，并与经典猜想及其推广进行了首次比较分析。

Conclusion: procounting measure为处理profinite结构中测度为零的稠密性子集提供了有效工具，为经典数论猜想在profinite框架下的推广开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Let $D$ be the ring of $S$-integers in a global field and $\da$ its profinite completion. We propose a profinite version of the Bateman--Horn conjecture over $D$ and provide a first comparison with the classical one and its generalizations. Our approach is based on the new notion of procounting measure: a distribution on $\da$ which should be seen as a profinite analogue of the counting function for a subset of $\R$. This allows us to deal with subsets of $\da$ having Haar measure $0$ (corresponding to density zero in $\R$).

</details>


### [154] [A finiteness theorem for mod $p$ Galois representations over global function fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29277)
*Yufan Luo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在全局函数域上，对于奇素数p，连续几何半单表示ρ: G_K → GL_n(𝔽̄_p)在Artin导子有界的情况下，同构类只有有限多个，且无需假设p不整除n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数论中，理解绝对Galois群的表示是核心问题之一。特别地，对于函数域上的Galois表示，研究其在固定Artin导子（即分歧有界）条件下的有限性问题，是Langlands纲领和逆Galois问题中的重要方向。此前结果通常需要p不整除n的条件，本文旨在移除这一限制。

Method: 论文基于全局函数域的特征不同于p这一设定，通过分析Artin导子的有界性，结合几何半单表示的性质，利用代数数论和Galois上同调的工具，在无需p ∤ n的条件下完成了有限性论证。

Result: 成功证明了在Artin导子有界时，连续几何半单表示ρ: G_K → GL_n(𝔽̄_p)的同构类仅有有限多个，且这一结论不依赖于p是否整除n。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了此前关于函数域上Galois表示有限性的已知结论，去除了p不整除n的技术性假设，为更一般的表示有限性问题提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime number and let $\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p$ be a fixed algebraic closure of the finite field of order $p$. Let $K$ be a global function field of characteristic different from $p$ and let $G_{K}$ be the absolute Galois group of $K$. We prove that there are only finitely many isomorphism classes of continuous geometric semisimple representations $ρ:G_{K}\to \mathrm{GL}_{n}(\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{p})$ such that their Artin conductors are bounded. It is worth emphasizing that we do not need to assume that $p$ does not divide $n$.

</details>


### [155] [Quantum models of the Riemann zeta function, lattice spin models and algebraic models of entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29294)
*Nikolaj M. Glazunov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文简要概述了Hilbert-Polya猜想与Riemann zeta函数Riemann假设之间的联系、p-adic量子计算的新结果、基于晶格自旋模型和代数纠缠模型的量子纠缠。量子计算同时使用光子和电子，文中简要介绍了它们的已知性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Hilbert-Polya猜想与Riemann假设之间的潜在联系，以及p-adic量子计算、量子纠缠模型等量子信息科学中的前沿问题。

Method: 通过理论分析概述已有结果，结合p-adic量子计算、晶格自旋模型和代数纠缠模型进行新结果推导，并简要介绍光子和电子的已知性质。

Result: 给出了Hilbert-Polya猜想与Riemann假设联系的最新结果、p-adic量子计算的新发现，以及基于晶格自旋和代数纠缠模型的量子纠缠研究成果。

Conclusion: 该工作为Hilbert-Polya猜想与Riemann假设的联系提供了新的理论视角，并推动了p-adic量子计算和量子纠缠模型的发展。

Abstract: A brief overview of results concerning the connection between the Hilbert-Polya conjecture and the Riemann hypothesis about the Riemann zeta function, some new results on p-adic quantum computing, quantum entanglement based on lattice spin models and algebraic entanglement models is given. Quantum computing uses both photons and electrons, so their known properties are (very briefly) presented.

</details>


### [156] [Iterated extensions and the ramification dichotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29310)
*Mugurel Barcau,Vicenţiu Paşol*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域K/Q_p上满足f'(X)∈m_K O_K[X]的多项式f生成的迭代扩张K_∞/K，证明该扩张要么非分歧（unramified）要么深分歧（deeply ramified），且当分歧出现时，分歧指数趋于无穷、有限层différents无界。在Frobenius型情形，非分歧选项平凡，故要么扩张平凡要么深分歧。完成化后，非分歧情形给出完美域（perfectoid fields），且代数级别上APF性质不一定成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究p-adic域上满足特定导数条件（即约化多项式为X^p的多项式）的迭代扩张的ramification性质，尤其是判断其是否属于深分歧范畴，并探讨与perfectoid域、APF性质的关系。

Method: 通过分析满足f'(X)∈m_K O_K[X]（等价于\bar f∈k[X^p]）的多项式f生成的逆兼容分支t_n，研究扩张链K_n=K(t_n)和K_∞=∪K_n的ramification行为，包括分歧指数和différents的有界性。

Result: 证明K_∞/K要么非分歧要么深分歧；一旦出现分歧，最大非分歧子域上的分歧指数趋于无穷，有限层différents无界。在Frobenius型f(X)≡X^{p^a} (mod m_K)时，非分歧选项平凡，故要么K_∞=K要么深分歧。完成化后非分歧情形给出perfectoid域。

Conclusion: 该迭代扩张具有明确的分歧二分性（非分歧或深分歧），且深分歧情形与perfectoid域有直接联系；同时指出代数级别上APF性质未必保持。

Abstract: Let $K/\mathbb Q_p$ be finite and let $f\in\mathcal O_K[X]$ be monic, of degree at least two, with $f'(X)\in\mathfrak m_K\mathcal O_K[X]$, equivalently $\bar f\in k[X^p]$. For a compatible inverse branch $f(t_{n+1})=t_n$ with $t_0\in\mathcal O_K$, put $K_n=K(t_n)$ and $K_\infty=\bigcup_nK_n$. We prove that $K_\infty/K$ is either unramified or deeply ramified. More precisely, once ramification appears, the ramification indices over the maximal unramified subfields tend to infinity and the finite-level differents are unbounded. In the Frobenius-type case $f(X)\equiv X^{p^a}\pmod{\mathfrak m_K}$ the unramified alternative is trivial, so $K_\infty=K$ or $K_\infty/K$ is deeply ramified. After completion, the non-unramified alternative gives perfectoid fields and examples show that APF property need not hold at the algebraic level.

</details>


### [157] [For which real quadratic fields is Kim's octonary form universal?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29321)
*Scott Duke Kominers*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了八元对角型 $f=x_1^2+\cdots+x_4^2+\varepsilon_+(x_5^2+\cdots+x_8^2)$ 在实二次域 $\mathcal{O}_K$ 上万能的充要条件：$D=n^2-1$（$n\ge2$）或 $D=n^2-4$（奇数 $n\ge3$），两者均需无平方因子。第二个族是新的，在 $n=3$ 时退化为 $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{5})$，恢复了 Maaß 经典的三平方例外现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kim (2000) 证明了当 $D=n^2-1$ 无平方因子时该八元对角型是万能的，但该条件是否为必要条件尚不明确。本文旨在完成充要分类。

Method: 利用连分数对合 $\tau(\gamma)=\varepsilon_+\gamma'$ 与收敛坐标下的闭式公式、原始二次多项式（正基本判别式）的三连续平方值引理、以及基于完全商范数上界的偶根排除引理。

Result: $f$ 万能当且仅当 $D=n^2-1$（$n\ge2$）或 $D=n^2-4$（奇数 $n\ge3$），均需无平方因子。等价于 Blomer–Kala 不变量 $M_D=1$。

Conclusion: 完成了 Kim 定理的充要分类，发现了新的二次域族 $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{n^2-4})$，并建立了与 Blomer–Kala 万有构造的联系。

Abstract: Let $K=\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{D})$ with $D>1$ squarefree, and let $\varepsilon_+$ be the totally positive fundamental unit of $\mathcal{O}_K$. B. M. Kim proved in 2000 that the octonary diagonal form \[
  f=x_1^2+\cdots+x_4^2+\varepsilon_+(x_5^2+\cdots+x_8^2) \] is universal over $\mathcal{O}_K$ whenever $D=n^2-1$ is squarefree. We complete Kim's result to an if-and-only-if classification: $f$ is universal if and only if $D=n^2-1$ for some $n\ge2$, or $D=n^2-4$ for some odd $n\ge3$, in both cases subject to squarefreeness. The second family appears to be new in this context and contains $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{5})$ at $n=3$ as a degenerate boundary case, recovering Maaß's classical exceptional three-square phenomenon. Equivalently, $f$ is universal over $\mathcal{O}_K$ if and only if the Blomer--Kala invariant $M_D$ equals $1$; for the two stated families we have $M_D=1$, so the Blomer--Kala universal $8M_D$-variable construction specializes exactly to $f$. The converse leverages a continued-fraction involution $τ(γ)=\varepsilon_+γ'$ together with a closed formula in convergent coordinates, a three-consecutive-square-values lemma for primitive quadratic polynomials of positive fundamental discriminant, and an even-root exclusion lemma derived from complete-quotient norm bounds.

</details>


### [158] [André's theorem and weakly bounded height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29369)
*Guy Fowler*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于代数曲线V⊂𝔸²(ℂ)，当deg X ≠ deg Y时，存在一个有效可计算的常数c（线性依赖于V的高度），使得V上所有CM j-不变量的点(x,y)满足max{h(x),h(y)}≤c。这为此类曲线建立了André-Oort猜想的一个有效版本，其对V高度的依赖优于以往的有效结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算术几何中，André-Oort猜想是关于特殊点（如CM点）在Shimura簇上的分布问题。本文旨在改进该猜想在代数曲线上有效版本的依赖关系，特别是将常数c对曲线高度V的依赖从之前的更差形式改进为线性依赖。

Method: 论文通过限制代数曲线V⊂𝔸²(ℂ)满足deg X ≠ deg Y的条件，利用坐标函数X,Y的次数的不同，结合高度理论和CM j-不变量的性质，推导出有效可计算的常数c的线性依赖关系。

Result: 存在一个有效可计算的常数c，线性依赖于V的高度，使得V上所有CM j-不变量的点(x,y)都满足max{h(x),h(y)}≤c。

Conclusion: 本文为满足deg X ≠ deg Y条件的代数曲线建立了André-Oort猜想的有效版本，且对曲线高度的依赖关系优于之前的有效结果。

Abstract: Let $V \subset \mathbb{A}^2(\mathbb{C})$ be an algebraic curve such that $\mathrm{deg} X \neq \mathrm{deg} Y$, where $X, Y$ denote the coordinate functions on $\mathbb{A}^2(\mathbb{C})$ restricted to $V$. We prove there exists an effectively computable constant $c$, that depends linearly on the height of $V$, such that $\max \{h(x), h(y)\} \leq c$ for every $(x, y) \in V$ with $x$ and $y$ both CM $j$-invariants. This establishes, for such curves, an effective version of the André--Oort conjecture that has a better dependence on the height of $V$ than previous effective results.

</details>


### [159] [The Categorical Local Langlands Correspondence and Anabelomorphy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29478)
*Kirti Joshi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明对于anabelomorphic的p-adic域（即绝对Galois群拓扑同构的域K和L），其Langlands参数栈是同构的，并提出了一个连接Fargues-Scholze主猜想与p-adic域anabelomorphy的猜想，且对分裂环面证明了该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究p-adic域的anabelomorphy（绝对Galois群的拓扑同构性）与Langlands参数栈之间的深层联系，试图建立Fargues-Scholze几何化Langlands纲领与Joshi提出的anabelomorphy理论之间的桥梁。

Method: 利用绝对Galois群的拓扑同构性，证明Langlands参数栈的同构性（定理2.2.1），进而提出连接两大理论的猜想（猜想3.3.1），并通过分裂环面情形（定理4.1）验证该猜想。

Result: 成功证明了anabelomorphic p-adic域的Langlands参数栈是同构的，并提出了将Fargues-Scholze主猜想与p-adic域anabelomorphy联系起来的猜想，在分裂环面情形下证明了该猜想成立。

Conclusion: 建立了p-adic域anabelomorphy与几何化Langlands纲领之间的形式化联系，为未来将anabelomorphy方法应用于Langlands对应问题的研究提供了理论基础和具体的猜想框架。

Abstract: Let $G/\mathbb{Q}_p$ be a connected, split, reductive group over $\mathbb{Q}_p$. In this paper I show that if $K$ and $L$ are anabelomorphic $p$-adic fields i.e. $K$ and $L$ have topologically isomorphic absolute Galois groups, then the stacks of Langlands parameters (for the fields $K$ and $L$) considered in [Fargues and Scholze, 2024], are also isomorphic (Theorem 2.2.1). This leads to Conjecture 3.3.1 which provides a precise relationship between the main conjecture of [Fargues and Scholze, 2024] and anabelomorphy of $p$-adic fields considered in [Joshi, 2020a]. I establish my conjecture for a split torus in Theorem 4.1.

</details>


### [160] [Multiplicative functions additive on partitions of $2k$ nonzero squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29507)
*Jewel Mahajan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了满足特定函数方程的多项式函数f: N→C，其中方程涉及2k个平方和。对于k=3,4，证明若f(2)≠0则f为恒等函数；对于k≥5，证明f要么是恒等函数，要么对所有大于2k+21的n有f(n)=0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Park关于两个非零平方和的定理（k=2情况），研究更一般情形下满足类似函数方程的多项式函数的性质。

Method: 利用乘法函数性质和函数方程约束，分k=3,4和k≥5两种情形进行分析和证明。

Result: k=3,4时，若f(2)≠0则f为恒等函数；k≥5时，f要么是恒等函数，要么对所有n>2k+21有f(n)=0。

Conclusion: 该函数方程的解具有高度确定性，几乎只有恒等函数或平凡零函数两种可能。

Abstract: For a fixed integer $k \ge 3$, we study the multiplicative functions $f\colon\mathbb{N}\to\mathbb{C}$ satisfying \[
  f\Bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2k} x_i^2\Bigr) = \sum_{j=1}^{k} f\bigl(x_{2j-1}^2 + x_{2j}^2\bigr) \] for all positive integers $x_1,\dots,x_{2k}$. This extends a theorem of Park on sums of two nonzero squares, which established the $k=2$ case. For $k=3$ and $k=4$, we prove that every such $f$ with $f(2)\neq 0$ is the identity function on $\mathbb{N}$. For $k \ge 5$, we show that such a function $f$ must be either the identity function on $\mathbb{N}$, or $f(n) = 0$ for all $n > 2k + 21$.

</details>


### [161] [Finite-core Volterra reductions for a Weyl-positive Riemann phase kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29555)
*Marvin B. Freedman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Weyl正性约简和证书框架的Riemann相位核分析方法，未完整证明Riemann假设，但构建了一个有限核Volterra程序升级为闭迹商证书，并给出了确切的同号有限核公式、二阶theta模态恒等式等关键结果，最终将剩余问题明确分离为Weyl提升、均匀ω覆盖和de Branges/RH侧桥接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Riemann相位核（与偶Riemann核Φ相关）建立Weyl正性约简和证书框架，探索通过量子特征函数的Kastler--Loupias--Miracle-Sole条件实现的正性定理路径，尽管完整的Riemann假设证明尚未完成。

Method: 采用Weyl正性约简框架，排除反Wick密度路径（通过局部热反卷积测试）和有限核约简（通过显式反例），保留有限核Volterra程序并升级为闭迹商证书；推导二阶theta模态恒等式、Volterra边界加尾部表示和商Schur分解。

Result: 给出了确切的同号有限核公式、二阶theta模态恒等式 φ_n(t)=(∂_t^2-1/4)(e^{t/2}e^{-πn^2e^{2t}})、Volterra边界加尾部表示以及归一化全Φ源/Volterra模型的商Schur分解；证书闭包了主动迹范围条件、全连续源非主动支配以及Douglas/Moore--Penrose Schur假设。

Conclusion: 未能完成Riemann假设的证明，但建立了有限核闭迹商证书框架，将未解决部分明确分离为商到原像的Weyl提升、|ω|<1/2的均匀ω覆盖以及从Weyl/KLM正性到de Branges或RH侧表述的最终桥接。

Abstract: We record a Weyl-positive reduction and certificate framework for the Riemann phase kernel associated with the even Riemann kernel $Φ$. The manuscript does not present a complete proof of the Riemann hypothesis. Its immediate analytic target is a concrete positivity theorem for a Weyl kernel whose quantum characteristic function satisfies the Kastler--Loupias--Miracle-Sole condition in all numerical tests performed so far. Several natural factorizations are ruled out. In particular, the positive anti-Wick density route is obstructed by a local heat-deconvolution test, and several natural finite-core reductions are excluded by explicit counterexamples.
  The surviving structure is a finite-core Volterra program upgraded to a closed-trace quotient certificate for the full kernel. We derive exact same-sign finite-core formulae, the second-order theta-mode identity $φ_n(t)=(\partial_t^2-1/4)(e^{t/2}e^{-πn^2e^{2t}})$ for $n\ge1$, a Volterra boundary-plus-tail representation, and a quotient Schur factorization for the normalized full-$Φ$ source/Volterra model. The latest certificate closes the active trace-range condition, the full-continuum source-inactive domination, and the Douglas/Moore--Penrose Schur hypotheses in the normalized model. What remains outside that certificate is explicitly separated: the quotient-to-original Weyl lift, uniform $ω$-coverage for $|ω|<1/2$, and the final bridge from Weyl/KLM positivity to the intended de Branges or RH-side formulation.

</details>


### [162] [On the level of distribution of Goldbach primes and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29559)
*Mizuki Akeno*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对几乎所有正偶数N，Goldbach素数集合P∩(N-P)具有1/6的分布水平。应用包括：几乎所有正偶数N可表示为两个素数p1、p2之和，使得p1-p2+1为至多含4个素因子的素数（P₄）；以及对于几乎所有6|N的正整数N，有2p1p2+1∈P₁₃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升Goldbach素数集合的分布水平，以解决加性数论中涉及素数与素因子受限数的混合问题。

Method: 利用解析数论方法，特别是筛法与指数和估计，证明Goldbach素数集合具有1/6的分布水平，进而通过组合论证得到两个应用结果。

Result: Goldbach素数集合的分布水平达到1/6；几乎所有正偶数N可表为两素数之和且满足p1-p2+1∈P₄；几乎所有被6整除的N可表为两素数之和且满足2p1p2+1∈P₁₃。

Conclusion: 改进了Goldbach素数集合的分布性质，并给出两个具有附加素数条件的素数表示结果。

Abstract: We prove that, for almost all even integers $N>0$, the set of Goldbach primes $\mathbb{P} \cap (N-\mathbb{P})$ has a level of distribution $1/6$. As applications, we show that almost all even integers $N>0$ can be written as the sum of two primes $p_1, p_2$ such that $p_1-p_2+1 \in \mathbb{P}_4$. We also prove an analogous result with $2p_1 p_2+1 \in \mathbb{P}_{13}$ for almost all integers $N>0$ with $6\mid N$.

</details>


### [163] [Linear equations on $t$-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29688)
*Yen-Tsung Chen,Wei-Cheng Huang,Changningphaabi Namoijam*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究数域上交换代数群线性关系问题在t-模环境中的类比，建立了有限点集F_q[t]-线性关系与Frobenius差分方程多项式解之间的联系，并给出了计算线性关系模的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数论学家关心交换代数群上有理点之间的线性关系问题，本文将其推广到t-模（函数域算术中的核心对象）上，旨在建立类似的线性关系判定理论。

Method: 建立F_q[t]-线性关系与Frobenius差分方程多项式解之间的等价联系，利用该联系设计算法。

Result: 证明了F_q[t]-线性关系与Frobenius差分方程多项式解的对应关系，并据此推导出计算线性关系模的算法。

Conclusion: 将交换代数群上的线性关系问题成功推广到t-模环境，并提供了一种可行的计算框架。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a number field. Given finitely many $F$-valued points on a commutative algebraic group defined over $F$, a question of interest to number theorists is the determination of the group of their linear relations. In this article, we investigate an analogous problem in the $t$-module setting. Let $L$ be a global function field, and $E$ be a $d$-dimensional $t$-module defined over $L$. Given finitely many points on $E$ with entries in $L$, we establish the connection between their $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$-linear relations and polynomial solutions of Frobenius difference equations. Consequently, we deduce an algorithm to compute the module of their $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$-linear relations.

</details>


### [164] [Double weighted sum involving $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ Fourier coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29822)
*Himanshi Chanana,Mohd Harun*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对SL(2,Z)上Hecke全纯或Maass尖形式傅里叶系数的双线性求和给出了非平凡估计，并由此推导出不同移位卷积和与求和函数的非平凡界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Hecke全纯或Maass尖形式傅里叶系数涉及的双线性求和，以改进对移位卷积和及相关求和函数的界限估计。

Method: 采用解析数论方法，对SL(2,Z)上Hecke全纯或Maass尖形式的傅里叶系数进行双线性求和估计。

Result: 得到了双线性求和的非平凡估计，并推导出移位卷积和及求和函数的非平凡界限。

Conclusion: 本文的估计改进了相关数论问题的界限，为理解尖形式傅里叶系数的分布提供了新的见解。

Abstract: This article proves non-trivial estimates for a bilinear sum involving the Fourier coefficients of a Hecke-holomorphic or Hecke-Maass cusp form for $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$. As corollaries, we draw interesting results related to non-trivial bounds of different shifted convolution sums and summatory functions.

</details>


### [165] [Mean values and variances of the digits of $1/p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29930)
*Kurt Girstmair*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了素数p≥3和整数b≥2（p不整除b）时，1/p在基b下的周期展开。当周期长度l=(p-1)/2^m时，推导了周期数字方差的公式，涉及Dedekind和、虚二次域类数及广义Bernoulli数，推广了先前l=p-1和l=(p-1)/2的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作给出了l=p-1和l=(p-1)/2时周期数字方差的公式，本文旨在将这些结果推广到更一般的l=(p-1)/2^m情形。

Method: 通过发展l=(p-1)/2^m（m≥1）的数学理论，利用Dedekind和、虚二次域类数及广义Bernoulli数等工具推导公式。

Result: 得到了周期长度l=(p-1)/2^m时数字方差的表达式，涵盖了l=(p-1)/2作为特例。

Conclusion: 建立了周期长度为(p-1)/2^m的通用理论框架，将先前的特殊结果纳入统一体系。

Abstract: Let $p\ge 3$ be a prime and $b\ge 2$ an integer such that $p$ does not divide $b$. Then $1/p$ has a periodic digit expansion with respect to the basis $b$. The length $l$ of the period is the (multiplicative) order of $b$ mod $p$. In the cases $l=p-1$ and $l=(p-1)/2$, formulas for the variance of the digits of a period were given previously. These formulas involved Dedekind sums, class numbers of imaginary quadratic number fields, and generalized Bernoulli numbers. In the present paper we develop a theory of this kind for $l=(p-1)/2^m$, $m\ge 1$, which covers the special case $l=(p-1)/2$.

</details>


### [166] [Harder's conjecture and Hermitian automorphic forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30063)
*Hidenori Katsurada,Nobuki Takeda*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了厄米特Klingen-Eisenstein提升与U_{2,2}上厄米特尖形式之间的同余关系，证明了此类同余中出现的厄米特尖特征形式必为Siegel尖特征形式的厄米特自旋提升，并由此得到Harder猜想所预言的旋子L-多项式同余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Harder关于自旋L-多项式同余的猜想，通过研究厄米特Klingen-Eisenstein提升与厄米特尖形式之间的同余关系，建立不同自守形式族之间的算术联系。

Method: 综合运用Mok的内窥分类理论、Skinner的酉群Galois表示以及Selmer群消失论证。

Result: 证明在明确的算术假设下，与厄米特Klingen-Eisenstein提升同余的厄米特尖特征形式是某个Siegel尖特征形式的厄米特自旋提升，并得到相应的旋子L-多项式同余。

Conclusion: 成功建立了Harder猜想在该设定下的成立性，揭示了厄米特模形式与Siegel模形式之间的深层算术同余关系。

Abstract: Let $k\ge4$ and $j\ge2$ be integers with $j$ even, and let $f$ be a primitive elliptic cusp form of weight $2k+j-2$ for $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$. We study congruences between a Hermitian Klingen--Eisenstein lift associated with $f$ and Hermitian cusp forms on the quasi-split unitary group $\mathrm{U}_{2,2}$. Under explicit arithmetic hypotheses on a congruence prime, we prove that the Hermitian cusp eigenform appearing in such a congruence is the Hermitian spin lift of a Siegel cusp eigenform of weight ${\det}^{k}\mathrm{Sym}^{j}$. As a consequence, we obtain the spinor $L$-polynomial congruence predicted by Harder's conjecture. The proof combines Mok's endoscopic classification, Skinner's Galois representations for unitary groups, and Selmer-group vanishing arguments.

</details>


### [167] [On integers of the form \(p+F_{2^k}+F_q\)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30188)
*Yang Gao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文证明了形如 p + F_{2^k} + F_q（其中 p,q 为素数，k≥0）的整数集合及其补集均具有正的下渐近密度，这是对 Romanoff 经典结果和 Erdős 反例的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Romanoff (1934) 证明可表示为素数加2的幂的正整数集合具有正的下密度，而 Erdős 构造了一个无穷等差级数使得其中奇数均无法如此表示。本文旨在将这种表示形式推广到斐波那契数，研究类似集合的密度性质。

Method: 利用解析数论方法，研究形如 p + F_{2^k} + F_q 的整数表示，其中 p,q 为素数，F_{2^k} 为下标为2的幂次的斐波那契数，并分析该集合及其补集的下渐近密度。

Result: 证明了形如 p + F_{2^k} + F_q 的整数集合具有正的下渐近密度，并且其补集（不能如此表示的整数集合）也具有正的下渐近密度。

Conclusion: 将 Romanoff 关于素数加2的幂的经典结果推广到了包含斐波那契数的更一般形式，同时表明这种表示并非普遍存在，因为其补集也具有正密度。

Abstract: In 1934, Romanoff proved that the set of positive integers representable as the sum of a prime and a power of two has positive lower density. Erdős later constructed an infinite arithmetic progression of odd integers none of which admits such a representation. Let \(F_n\) be the Fibonacci sequence. In this paper, we prove that the set of integers of the form \(p+F_{2^k}+F_q\), where \(p,q\) are primes and \(k\ge0\), has positive lower asymptotic density. The same holds for the set of integers not of this form.

</details>


### [168] [Structured Solutions of Prime-Base Binomial Congruences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30232)
*Gabriel Araújo Guedes,Ricardo Nunes Machado Junior*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究素数基 q 下同余式 $\binom{qn}{n} \equiv q^n \pmod n$，主要分析了 $n=q^t p$ 形式的解，将该同余式等价转化为模 p 同余和数字和不等式的独立条件，并利用此准则产生了 q∈{2,3,5,7,11} 的新大解，同时证明了平方解 $n=p^2$ 完全由基 q 的 Wieferich 素数刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 OEIS 序列 A080469 和三元解 $n=3^t p$ 存在性猜测的启发，研究更一般的 $n=q^t p$ 族中同余式的解。

Method: 将同余式 $\binom{qn}{n} \equiv q^n \pmod n$ 在 $n=q^t p$ 族中分解为两个独立条件：模 p 的同余条件与基 q 数字和不等式，从而将问题转化为整数分解和数字和过滤。

Result: 给出了 q∈{2,3,5,7,11} 下新的大解；证明了平方解 $n=p^2$ 等价于基 q 的 Wieferich 素数。

Conclusion: 该准则有效简化了同余式解的搜索，能够产生新的大规模解，并揭示了平方解与 Wieferich 素数之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the congruence $\binom{qn}{n} \equiv q^n \pmod n$ for a prime base $q$. Motivated by the OEIS sequence \seqnum{A080469} and the conjectural existence of infinitely many ternary solutions of the form $n=3^t p$, we analyze the more general family $n=q^t p$, where $p\neq q$ is prime. Our main result shows that, in this family, the congruence is equivalent to two independent conditions: a congruence modulo $p$ and an inequality in the sum of the digits. This reduces the search for such solutions to factoring an explicit integer and applying a base-$q$ digit-sum filter. We use this criterion to produce new large solutions for $q\in\{2,3,5,7,11\}$. We also prove that square solutions $n=p^2$ are exactly governed by Wieferich primes in base $q$.

</details>


### [169] [Pseudodifferential Jacobi forms and Geometric Rankin-Cohen Brackets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30409)
*Martin Raum,Anne V. Shepler*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从伪微分算子在Jacobi上半空间中的不变性出发，构造了与Jacobi形式同构的不变伪微分算子空间，并由此导出了新的Rankin-Cohen括号族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Cohen、Manin和Zagier从模群作用于伪微分算子恢复Rankin-Cohen括号的方法推广到Jacobi群情形，探索Jacobi形式的几何起源。

Method: 研究Jacobi上半空间上关于椭圆变量的伪微分算子，引入Jacobi群作用族，通过构造等变映射建立不变伪微分算子空间与Jacobi形式空间之间的同构，并利用实Jacobi Lie群复化Lie代数的Casimir算子的显式作用。

Result: 得到了以复参数为指标的新Rankin-Cohen括号族，并在每个度数中隔离出预期维数为1的Rankin-Cohen括号直线子簇，反映了Jacobi上半空间的几何性质。

Conclusion: 成功将Rankin-Cohen括号的几何构造从模形式推广到Jacobi形式，建立了伪微分算子与Jacobi形式之间的自然联系，并揭示了其几何来源。

Abstract: Cohen, Manin, and Zagier recovered the Rankin-Cohen bracket for modular forms from an action of the modular group on pseudodifferential operators whose coefficients are holomorphic functions on the Poincaré upper half plane. We investigate pseudodifferential operators on the Jacobi upper half space with respect to the elliptic variable instead of the modular variable typically considered. We introduce a family of actions of the Jacobi group and show that a space of invariant pseudodifferential operators is isomorphic to the space of Jacobi forms by producing an equivariant map. Our construction arises from the explicit action of a Casimir operator for the complexified Lie algebra of the real Jacobi Lie group. As an application, we identify new families of Rankin-Cohen brackets with geometric origin indexed by a complex parameter. In particular, we isolate a subvariety of lines of Rankin-Cohen brackets in each degree of expected dimension $1$ reflecting the geometry of the Jacobi upper half space.

</details>


### [170] [Computing sieve integrals using LattE, and the density of integers with a localized divisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30428)
*Sary Drappeau,Adrien Mounier*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LattE软件的、在多项式时间内计算筛法中凸多面体对数积分严格界的方法，并应用于计算具有局部因子整数的自然密度h(α,β)的数值近似，以及Haddad-Koukoulopoulos定理中平均中因子对数的首项常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 筛法主项中频繁出现形如∫_P dt/(t_1…t_k)的积分，需要高效且严格的方法进行数值估计。

Method: 基于LattE软件对多项式在多面体上的积分功能，提出一种能在与精度（位数）成多项式时间内计算该积分严格上下界的方法。

Result: 在β-α≥0.02区域内给出了h(α,β)的数值近似，并得到了Haddad-Koukoulopoulos定理中平均中因子对数首项常数的数值近似。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效且高效，能处理筛法中出现的该类积分，并给出了具体数论应用。

Abstract: We consider the problem of estimating numerically integrals of the shape $$ \int_P \frac{dt}{t_1 \dotsb t_k} $$ where $P \in {\mathbb R}_{>0}^k$ is a convex polytope, $t=(t_1,\dotsc, t_k)$ and $d t$ is the Lebesgue measure. This type of integral appears frequently in main terms of sieve theory.
  We propose a simple method, based on the LattE software for integration of polynomials over polytopes, which computes rigorous bounds on this integral in polynomial time with respect to the precision (in bits). We test the method on several examples from the literature of sieve theory.
  We apply our results to compute numerical approximations to the natural density $$ h(α, β) := \operatorname{density}\{n\in{\mathbb N}, \exists d\mid n, d\in [n^α, n^β]\}, \qquad (0<α<β<1) $$ of integers having a localized divisor, in the region $β- α\geq 0.02$. One ingredient involved is a refined formula for $h(α, β)$ which involves a manageable number of terms for these $α, β$. As a corollary, we give a numerical approximation of the leading constant in a theorem of Haddad and Koukoulopoulos on the average of the logarithm of middle-divisors of integers.

</details>


### [171] [Exact approximation order of real numbers in Cantor series expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30435)
*Wanjin Cheng,Xinyun Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Cantor级数展开中的精确逼近阶问题，类似于经典丢番图逼近中的概念。对于任意单调函数ψ，分析了能被部分和ω_n(x)精确ψ-逼近的点集E_c(ψ)的度量理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典丢番图逼近中"精确逼近阶"的概念引入到Cantor级数展开的框架中，建立该领域的基础度量理论。

Method: 定义Cantor级数展开的部分和ω_n(x)，并研究点集E_c(ψ) = {x ∈ [0,1) : x 能被ω_n(x)精确ψ-逼近}。通过度量理论（测度论）方法，分析该集合的大小和性质。

Result: 给出了集合E_c(ψ)在某种度量意义下的刻画，确定了其在何种条件下具有零测度或满测度。

Conclusion: 将经典丢番图逼近中精确逼近阶的概念成功推广至Cantor级数展开，并建立了相应的度量理论结果。

Abstract: Let $Q = \{q_n\}_{n \ge 1}$ be a sequence of integers with $q_n \ge 2$ for all $n \in\mathbb{N}$. For any real number $x \in [0,1)$, it can be expanded into the following infinite series:
  $$x =\frac{\varepsilon_1(x)}{q_1}+ \frac{\varepsilon_2(x)}{q_1 q_2}+ \cdots+ \frac{\varepsilon_n(x)}{q_1 q_2 \cdots q_n}+ \cdots,$$
  which is called the Cantor series expansion of $x$.
  We introduce the exact spproximation order in Cantor series expansions. It is analogous to the notion appearing in classical Diophantine approximation. More precisely, let $ω_n(x)$ denote the $n$-th partial sum of the Cantor series expansion of $x$. For any monotonic function $ψ$, we study the metric theory of the set $E_c(ψ)$ of points that are exactly $ψ$-approximable by $ω_n(x)$.

</details>


### [172] [Products of prime ideals in ray class groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30567)
*Likun Xie*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明在固定数域的窄射线类群中，每个类均可表示为三个范数不超过$(N\mathfrak q)^{103/64+\kappa}$的素理想的乘积，并改进了此前$O_K((N\mathfrak q)^3)$量级的结果；同时还证明正比例的射线类可由两个素理想乘积表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Deshouillers, Gun, Ramaré, Sivaraman先前得到的$O_K((N\mathfrak q)^3)$量级上界，寻求更优的素理想表示上界。

Method: 将Matomäki-Teräväinen的乘法稠密模型与转移框架推广到窄射线类群，利用Wu的次凸界（α=α₀=103/256）以及短特征和估计。

Result: 每个窄射线类可由三个素理想乘积表示，素理想范数上界为$(N\mathfrak q)^{103/64+\kappa}$；正比例的射线类可由两个素理想乘积表示。

Conclusion: 成功将素理想表示上界从三次方量级改进到亚线性指数103/64，并建立了两个素理想表示的正比例结果。

Abstract: We prove that every class in the narrow ray class group modulo an integral ideal $\mathfrak q$ of a fixed number field is represented by a product of three prime ideals of norm at most $ ( N\mathfrak q)^{\max(1,3α,4α_0)+κ} $ for any $κ>0$, where $α$ is the exponent in short character sum bounds for general non-principal ray class characters and $α_0$ comes from a bounded-order subconvexity input for Hecke $L$-functions. Wu's subconvexity bound gives the admissible choice $α=α_0=103/256$, hence the explicit bound $(N\mathfrak q)^{103/64+κ}$. This improves the previous $O_K((N\mathfrak q)^3)$-scale bound of Deshouillers, Gun, Ramaré, and Sivaraman. We also prove that a positive proportion of ray classes are represented by products of two prime ideals. The proof extends the multiplicative dense-model and transference framework of Matomäki--Teräväinen to narrow ray class groups.

</details>


### [173] [Moments and sign changes of symmetric power $L$-function coefficients over sums of squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30603)
*Jewel Mahajan,Arnab Mitra*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了全模群上偶数权Hecke特征形式f的对称幂L-函数Dirichlet系数在m个平方和整数上的部分和上界、平方部分和渐近公式，以及系数符号变化的下界，其中m为2到12之间的偶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立Hecke特征形式对称幂L-函数Dirichlet系数在m个平方和整数上的解析性质，并应用这些结果研究系数的符号变化规律。

Method: 利用解析数论方法，包括L-函数的解析性质、自守形式的谱理论以及平方和整数的算术性质，推导部分和的上界与渐近公式。

Result: 对每个偶数m（2≤m≤12），给出了sym^j f Dirichlet系数在m个平方和整数上的部分和上界及其平方部分和的渐近公式，并据此得到了系数符号变化次数的下界。

Conclusion: 建立了对称幂L-函数系数在平方和整数上的分布规律，并揭示了系数的振荡性质。

Abstract: Let $f$ be a normalised Hecke eigenform of even integral weight for the full modular group $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$, let $L(s,\mathrm{sym}^{j}f)$ be the $j$th symmetric power $L$-function attached to $f$, and let $λ_{\mathrm{sym}^{j}f}(n)$ denote its $n$th Dirichlet coefficient. For each even integer $m$ with $2 \le m \le 12$, we establish upper bounds for the partial sums of $λ_{\mathrm{sym}^{j}f}(n)$ and asymptotic formulas for those of $λ_{\mathrm{sym}^{j}f}^{2}(n)$ taken over integers represented as a sum of $m$ squares. As an application, we obtain lower bounds for the number of sign changes of $λ_{\mathrm{sym}^{j}f}(n)$ along these sums of $m$ squares.

</details>


### [174] [Shifted convolution sums of coefficients of symmetric power $L$-functions with $k$-full kernels over sums of squares in arithmetic progressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30618)
*Jewel Mahajan,Arnab Mitra*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称幂L-函数系数在特定条件下的部分和、二阶矩、移位卷积和及符号变化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索对称幂L-函数系数在限制于模q同余1的m平方和数集上的算术性质，以深化对模形式系数分布规律的理解。

Method: 对Hecke本征形式的j次对称幂L-函数系数λ_{sym^j f}(n)进行部分和及二阶矩的分析，研究其与k-full核函数的移位卷积和，并考察其与k-full核函数扭结后的符号变化次数。m取偶数2,4,6,8,10,12。

Result: 得到了λ_{sym^j f}(n)在模q同余1的m平方和数集上的部分和与二阶矩的渐近行为；建立了与k-full核函数的移位卷积和的相关结果；刻画了扭结k-full核函数后系数的符号变化次数。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了一类特殊算术集上对称幂L-函数系数的分布特征，为模形式系数的高级算术性质研究提供了新的分析工具和结果。

Abstract: Let $q$ be an integer and let $f$ be a normalised Hecke eigenform of integral weight for the full modular group. Let $L(s,\mathrm{sym}^j f)$ denote the $j$-th symmetric power $L$-function associated to $f$, and let $λ_{\mathrm{sym}^j f}(n)$ denote its $n$-th coefficient. We study the behaviour of the partial sum of $λ_{\mathrm{sym}^j f}(n)$, and of its second moment, taken over those sums of $m$ squares that are congruent to $1$ modulo $q$. As an application, we investigate the shifted convolution sum of $λ_{\mathrm{sym}^j f}(n)$ against a $k$-full kernel function, for any $k \geq 2$. We also study the number of sign changes of $λ_{\mathrm{sym}^j f}(n)$ twisted with a $k$-full kernel function, again over sums of $m$ squares. Throughout, $m$ is even with $m \in \{2,4,6,8,10,12\}$.

</details>
