### [175] [A New $L2-1_σ$-Interior Penalty Method for Variable-Order Time-Fractional Subdiffusion Interface Problem with Curved Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28443)
*Hongying Huang,Chanchan Hao,Changmu Yu,Huili Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有曲界面不连续系数的变阶时间分数次扩散问题，采用L2-1_σ时间步进（基于渐变网格）和对称内部惩罚有限元法（基于体适网格），建立了离散时间L^2范数下的稳定性和最优先验误差估计，实现了二阶时间精度。通过放宽对α_n的不等式约束简化了参数选择，数值实验验证了时间阶min{2,rδ}、空间阶min{s,k+1}以及对超收敛点和界面几何的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变阶时间分数次扩散方程在描述非均匀介质中的反常扩散现象时，常遇到系数在曲界面处不连续的问题，现有方法在处理此类问题时存在精度不足或参数选择复杂的局限，因此需要发展一种同时保证时间二阶精度、空间高阶精度且参数选取简便的数值格式。

Method: 采用L2-1_σ时间步进法在渐变网格上离散时间分数阶导数，并利用对称内部惩罚有限元法在体适网格上处理空间离散；通过放宽对α_n的约束条件（即允许不满足α_n≤α(t_{n-α_n/2})）简化参数选择，从而无需求解非线性方程即可从多个可行值中选取α_n。

Result: 建立了离散时间L^2范数下的稳定性和最优先验误差估计，证明时间方向达到二阶精度；数值实验验证了时间收敛阶为min{2,rδ}、空间收敛阶为min{s,k+1}，并展示了方法对超收敛点和界面几何形状的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提数值格式在理论上保证了变阶分数次扩散问题的高精度求解，同时通过简化α_n的选择过程提升了实际应用中的便利性，数值结果充分支持了理论分析和方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper treats variable-order time-fractional subdiffusion with discontinuous coefficients across a curved interface using $L2\!-\!1_σ$ time stepping on graded meshes and a symmetric interior penalty FEM on body-fitted meshes. Stability and optimal a priori error estimates in a discrete-in-time $L^2$ norm are established, yielding second-order temporal accuracy. While analysis typically assumes $α_n$ at $t_{n-σ_n}$ lies in the range of $α(t)$ on $[t_{n-1},t_n]$ and $α_n\le α(t_{n-α_n/2})$, experiments indicate the second inequality can be relaxed or omitted, enabling straightforward selection of $α_n$ from many admissible values without solving a nonlinear equation. Numerical results verify temporal rates $\min\{2,rδ\}$, spatial order $\min\{s,k+1\}$, and robustness to superconvergent points and interface geometry.

</details>


### [176] [Constrained Kolmogorov widths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28587)
*Ronald DeVore,Guergana Petrova,Jonathan W. Siegel,Przemysław Wojtaszczyk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了有约束逼近问题，探讨在近似过程中施加额外约束（如光滑性、单调性、凸性）对近似效率的影响，提出了有约束Kolmogorov宽度等概念，并证明在经典光滑性约束下，施加约束通常不会损失近似效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中，近似函数g需要保留原函数f的已知性质（如光滑性、几何性质等），这些额外要求称为约束。传统逼近理论研究的是无约束情况，而约束的存在可能限制逼近效率，因此需要系统理解约束对逼近能力的影响。

Method: 在有限维线性空间V的线性逼近框架下，引入并研究了多种有约束宽度（包括有约束Kolmogorov宽度），并对不同约束宽度进行了比较。论文第一部分定义和比较了这些宽度概念，第二部分将结果应用于经典的光滑性约束场景。

Result: 在经典光滑性约束的设置下，施加额外约束通常不会损失逼近效率，即有约束逼近与无约束逼近的最优逼近阶相同。

Conclusion: 本文为有约束逼近提供了系统性的理论基础，建立了有约束宽度的概念框架，并证明了在光滑性约束下，约束可以以零代价施加。

Abstract: The main theme of approximation theory is to understand how well a general function $f$ can be approximated by a simpler function $g$ such as a polynomial or spline. In many applications, one wants $g$ to retain known properties of $f$ such as its inherent smoothness or a geometrical property such as monotonicity or convexity. Additional requirements on $g$ of this type are known as constraints. In this paper, we do a systematic study of constrained approximation to understand how the imposition of such constraints limits the efficiency of the approximation. We study constrained approximation in the setting of linear approximation where $g$ is to be taken from a finite dimensional linear space $V$ of a fixed dimension $n$. Kolmogorov widths describe how well one can approximate when using such linear spaces $V$. The first part of this paper introduces and studies several types of constrained widths, including the constrained Kolmogorov widths, and gives comparisons between them. The second part of the paper is restricted to classical settings where the constraint imposes a smoothness requirement on $g$. In this case, our results prove that the additional constraint can typically be imposed with no loss in the efficiency of the approximation.

</details>


### [177] [An Adaptive Fast Algorithm for Periodic Coulomb Lattice Sums in Arbitrary Unit Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28608)
*Xuanzhao Gao,Leslie Greengard,Shidong Jiang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种快速算法，用于评估条件收敛的库仑晶格求和，该算法扩展了双空间多级核分裂（DMK）框架，通过五步EWALD求和流程（包括FFT和插值）处理周期性边界条件下的任意单位晶胞和粒子分布，在固定晶胞形状下复杂度为O(N)，2D算法比周期性快速多极子方法快约一个数量级，3D中在非立方晶胞下也能达到与自由空间DMK相当的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要高效处理周期性边界条件下库仑晶格求和的收敛问题，特别是针对任意倾斜/三斜晶胞和非均匀粒子分布，现有方法效率不足。

Method: 扩展双空间多级核分裂（DMK）框架，自适应树根节点采用包含内块和环绕镜像块矩形网格，光滑顶层周期核通过五步EWALD流程（扩展-FFT-对角缩放-逆FFT-插值）计算。

Result: 固定晶胞形状下复杂度O(N)；2D中比周期性快速多极子方法快约一个数量级；3D中即使边比约17的三斜晶胞，速度也与自由空间DMK相当。

Conclusion: 该方法在条件收敛库仑晶格求和上实现了高效计算，尤其在高度非均匀分布和极端晶胞形状下具有显著优势。

Abstract: We present a fast algorithm for evaluating conditionally convergent Coulomb lattice sums, governed by the Laplace equation with periodic boundary conditions on arbitrary unit cells (oblique in 2D, triclinic in 3D) and arbitrary particle distributions. The algorithm extends the dual-space multilevel kernel-splitting (DMK) framework to this context. The root of the adaptive tree is now a rectangular grid of cubes consisting of an inner block covering the unit cell and a surrounding halo of image cubes, rather than a single cube, and the smooth top-level periodic kernel -- the only term that requires the consideration of conditional convergence issues -- is evaluated by the ``five-step procedure" used in fast Ewald summation: spreading, fast Fourier transform (FFT), diagonal scaling, inverse FFT, and interpolation. The resulting complexity is $O(N)$ for fixed cell shape. Benchmarked against the periodic fast multipole method on highly nonuniform source distributions, our 2D algorithm is roughly an order of magnitude faster across particle counts and target precisions; in three dimensions, it is often as fast as the free-space DMK on the same sources, even for triclinic cells with edge-length ratios up to roughly $17$.

</details>


### [178] [A Trace-Based Interface Reduction Method for Highly Conducting Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28700)
*So-Hsiang Chou,Do Young Kwak*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对高导电性界面椭圆界面问题，发展了一种约化界面公式。通过采用公共界面迹而非解跳跃作为界面未知量，推导了基于迹的Schur补形式，将独立扩展问题与Dirichlet-to-Neumann跳跃算子和表面刚度算子结合的约化界面方程相结合。数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高导电性界面问题中，传统界面处理方法面临界面条件复杂（法向通量跳跃与界面迹的表面Laplacian相关）的挑战，需要发展更高效的约化界面公式以降低计算复杂度并提高精度。

Method: 采用公共界面迹作为界面未知量替代传统解跳跃，推导基于迹的Schur补形式；将问题分解为独立子域扩展问题，构建包含Dirichlet-to-Neumann跳跃算子和表面刚度算子的约化界面方程；采用有限维迹逼近产生仅定义在界面上的紧凑约化系统。

Result: 通过圆形、光滑非圆形和心形界面的数值实验，验证了所提方法的有效性，并展示了界面模式富集的作用。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于公共界面迹的约化界面公式能有效处理高导电性界面椭圆界面问题，通过将问题约化到界面上显著降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: We develop a reduced interface formulation for elliptic interface problems with highly conducting interfaces. The interface condition consists of continuity of the primal variable together with a jump in the normal flux proportional to the surface Laplacian of the interface trace. Instead of using the solution jump as the interface unknown, we employ the common interface trace and derive a trace-based Schur complement formulation. For prescribed interface trace data, independent extension problems are solved in the two subdomains, leading to a reduced interface equation involving the Dirichlet-to-Neumann jump operator and a surface stiffness operator. Finite-dimensional trace approximations produce compact reduced systems posed only on the interface. Numerical experiments for circular, smooth noncircular, and heart-shaped interfaces illustrate the effectiveness of the method and the role of interface-mode enrichment.

</details>


### [179] [Schrödingerization based quantum algorithms for regularized Wasserstein proximal operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28752)
*Shi Jin,Nana Liu,Yue Yu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子算法求解正则化Wasserstein proximal算子，通过Schrödingerization方法将Fokker-Planck方程和粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程转化为热方程求解，实现了对经典方法指数级的加速，查询复杂度为O(d N_x T log²(1/ε))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化Wasserstein proximal算子是最优传输和平均场博弈中的基本工具，但经典方法计算代价随空间维度指数增长(N_x^d)，亟需高效量子算法突破维度灾难。

Method: 通过Cole-Hopf变换将Fokker-Planck方程和粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程转化为前向热方程；利用Schrödingerization方法求解热方程；通过Hadamard除法和乘积操作处理方程间的耦合；使用矩阵-向量乘积表示实现这些操作。

Result: 算法以O(d N_x T log²(1/ε))的查询复杂度制备ε-近似的终端密度态，复杂度对空间维度d和网格点数N_x呈线性依赖，实现了对经典方法(N_x^d/时间步)的指数级加速，数值实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该量子算法为高维最优传输和平均场博弈问题提供了指数级加速的可行方案，拓展了Schrödingerization方法在PDE求解中的应用。

Abstract: We develop a quantum algorithm for the regularized Wasserstein proximal operator, which is a fundamental tool in optimal transport and mean-field games. The regularization introduces a small diffusive term into the continuity equation of the Benamou-Brenier formulation, which results in a forward-backward PDE system consisting of a Fokker-Planck equation and a viscous Hamilton-Jacobi equation with a quadratic Hamiltonian. Through the Cole-Hopf transformation, both equations are converted to forward heat equations, whose coupling requires a Hadamard division to prepare the initial data for the second heat equation and a Hadamard product to recover the terminal density. We solve these heat equations via the Schrödingerization method and implement the Hadamard division and product operations using simple matrix-vector multiplication representations. The complete quantum algorithm prepares an $\varepsilon$-approximation of the terminal density state with $\mathcal{O}(d N_x T \log^2(1/\varepsilon))$ query complexity, up to constants depending on the potential and initial density, where $d$ is the spatial dimension, $N_x$ is the number of grid points per spatial dimension and $T$ is the evolution time. The complexity depends only {\it linearly} on $d N_x$, yielding an {\it exponential} speedup over classical methods, whose cost scales as $N_x^d$ per time step. Numerical experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [180] [Viscosity in error upper bound for a consistent splitting scheme of the Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28800)
*M Nader Alhomsi,Jiahong Wu,Xiaoming Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Huang 和 Shen [5] 提出的 Navier-Stokes 方程一致分裂格式中粘度对误差上界的影响。将粘度保持为符号参数扩展原证明方法，得到包含粘度负幂次的 H1 速度误差界，表明格式在粘度趋零时非鲁棒。数值实验证实了该预测：高雷诺数下格式发散，对比隐式 Newton 求解器和时间依赖 Stokes 格式发现失败源于对流项的显式处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究粘度（或高雷诺数）对 Navier-Stokes 方程分裂格式误差上界的影响，验证格式在粘度趋零时的鲁棒性。

Method: 扩展 Huang 和 Shen [5] 的证明方法，将粘度作为符号参数保留；改进文献[8]中 Stokes 压力估计常数定理；设计基于 Kovasznay 流扰动的数值实验进行验证。

Result: 获得了含粘度负幂次的 H1 速度误差上界，证明格式在粘度趋零时非鲁棒；数值实验显示高雷诺数下格式发散，失败原因是对流项的显式处理。

Conclusion: Huang-Shen 分裂格式在高雷诺数（小粘度）条件下不鲁棒，原因在于对流项的显式离散处理，需改进以增强对粘度的稳健性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the role of viscosity in the error upper bounds of a consistent splitting scheme for the Navier-Stokes equations proposed by Huang and Shen [5]. In their original analysis the viscosity is fixed to unity. By following and extending their proof methodology while keeping the viscosity symbolic, we obtain an H1 velocity error bound that contains negative powers of viscosity, indicating that the scheme is not robust as viscosity tends zero. To establish this bound we refine a theorem in [8] on the constant in the Stokes pressure estimate, which is crucial to the error analysis. A targeted numerical experiment based on a perturbation of the Kovasznay flow corroborates this analytical prediction: the scheme of [5] blows up at high Reynolds number, and a comparison with a fully implicit Newton solver and with the time-dependent Stokes counterpart of the same scheme localizes the failure to the explicit treatment of the convection term.

</details>


### [181] [Fast unified evaluation of layer and volume potentials for the 2D modified Helmholtz equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28865)
*Edith Frisk Gärtner,Fredrik Fryklund,Anna-Karin Tornberg*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于势理论的快速精确方法，用于求解二维修正亥姆霍兹方程。该方法通过将自由空间格林函数分解为短程局部部分和光滑长程部分，分别采用非均匀快速傅里叶变换(NUFFT)和渐近展开处理；层势通过二进精化层的 telescoping sum 实现平滑快速衰减核的评估；体积势在三角切割网格上评估，对网格质量具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的势理论方法在处理二维修正亥姆霍兹方程时，涉及奇异和近奇异层势评估以及体积势，缺乏一个统一、高效且对网格质量鲁棒的计算框架。

Method: 1) 将自由空间格林函数分解为短程局部部分和光滑长程部分；2) 长程部分用NUFFT高效评估，局部部分用渐近展开处理；3) 层势引入二进精化层的telescoping sum，使差核平滑快速衰减，无需特殊求积规则；4) 体积势在三角切割网格上评估，网格仅作为光滑数据的求积规则。

Result: 数值实验展示了预期的收敛速度、势评估的高通量以及对网格质量的鲁棒性，无需稳定化或单元合并技术。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个统一、高效且鲁棒的框架，能够处理二维修正亥姆霍兹方程中的奇异/近奇异层势和体积势，对微小和扭曲的网格单元具有天然韧性。

Abstract: We present a fast and accurate potential theory-based method for the two-dimensional modified Helmholtz equation, treating the involved singular and nearly singular layer evaluations together with volume potentials within a single computational framework. The method is based on a decomposition of the free-space Green's function into a short-range local part and a smooth long-range part. The long-range contribution is evaluated efficiently using the non-uniform fast Fourier transform (NUFFT), while the local contribution is treated by asymptotic expansions. For the layer potentials, an intermediate telescoping sum over dyadic refinement levels is added, where the resulting difference kernels are smooth and rapidly decaying, allowing the dyadic levels to be evaluated without specialized quadrature rules. The volume potential is evaluated on triangular cut-cell meshes, where the mesh only enters the scheme as quadrature rule for smooth data. This makes the method robust with respect to small and distorted mesh cells, without the need for stabilization or cell-merging techniques. Numerical experiments demonstrate the expected convergence rates, high throughput of the potential evaluations, and robustness with respect to mesh quality.

</details>


### [182] [Splitting schemes for problems with memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28883)
*Petr N. Vabishchevich*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对有限维希尔伯特空间中带记忆的一阶积分-微分方程的柯西问题，通过用指数和近似记忆核将非局部问题转化为局部问题，提出并研究了加权算子差分格式和分裂格式的稳定性与构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服带记忆的积分-微分方程数值求解中需要存储和计算所有历史时间层解的主要计算困难。

Method: 采用指数和近似记忆核，将原非局部问题约化为局部问题（弱耦合演化方程组附加辅助函数的常微分方程）；在希尔伯特空间直和上给出向量形式表述；证明两层加权算子差分格式的无条件稳定性；提出并研究分离局部算子和积分算子的分裂格式。

Result: 证明了在标准限制下加权算子差分格式的无条件稳定性；给出了分裂格式的构造与分析；指出该方法可推广至其他非局部问题（如含时间导数记忆的方程）。

Conclusion: 指数和近似记忆核结合分裂格式的方法能有效解决带记忆积分-微分方程的计算困难，且具有推广至更广泛非局部问题的潜力。

Abstract: The paper considers the Cauchy problem for a first-order integro-differential equation with memory in a finite-dimensional Hilbert space. The main computational difficulty of such problems is the need to store and process the solution at all previous time levels. To overcome this difficulty, an approach is used that approximates the memory kernel by a sum of exponentials, which reduces the original nonlocal problem to a local one -- a system of weakly coupled evolution equations with additional ordinary differential equations for auxiliary functions. The problem is formulated in vector form on the direct sum of Hilbert spaces. Unconditional stability of two-level operator-difference schemes with weights is proved under standard restrictions. Splitting schemes are proposed and investigated by separating the local and integral operators of the problem. Possibilities for constructing similar schemes for other nonlocal problems, in particular for the equation with memory of the time derivative of the solution, are noted.

</details>


### [183] [Pressure-robust ALE space-time DG method for the Stokes equations on moving domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28914)
*L. Beirão da Veiga,S. Gómez,K. B. Haile*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对移动域上的不可压缩Stokes方程，在任意拉格朗日-欧拉(ALE)框架下提出并分析了一种时空间断伽辽金方法。通过使用逆变Piola映射定义离散速度空间，在离散层面上保持了逐点无散性质。证明了该方法在空间网格和时间分区无约束条件下的inf-sup稳定性，并建立了能量范数下关于空间和时间任意逼近阶的先验误差估计。对于时间上的分片常数和分片线性逼近，证明了该方法在低黏性 regime 下的鲁棒性，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动域上不可压缩Stokes方程的数值求解面临几何变形和不可压缩性约束的双重挑战，现有方法往往在处理点态无散性质或任意阶时空近似时存在局限性，亟需一种既保持离散无散性又具备高精度和稳定性的数值方法。

Method: 在ALE框架下构建时空间断伽辽金方法，采用逆变Piola映射定义离散速度空间以保持逐点无散性；通过理论分析证明inf-sup稳定性；建立能量范数下的先验误差估计；分析低黏性 regime下的鲁棒性。

Result: 证明了方法在无网格或时间分区约束条件下的inf-sup稳定性；建立了任意空间和时间逼近阶的能量范数误差估计；证明了低黏性 regime下时间分片常数和分片线性近似的鲁棒性；数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了移动域上不可压缩Stokes方程的高精度数值求解问题，在离散无散性、稳定性和鲁棒性方面具有优势，为移动边界/界面问题提供了可靠的数值工具。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a space-time discontinuous Galerkin method for the incompressible Stokes equations on moving domains within the arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian setting. We use a contravariant Piola map in the definition of the discrete velocity space to preserve the pointwise divergence-free property on the discrete level. We show that the method is inf-sup stable, with no constraints on the spatial mesh or the time partition. We also establish a priori error estimates in the energy norm for arbitrary degrees of approximation in space and time. For piecewise-constant and piecewise-linear approximations in time, we show that the method is also robust at low viscosity regimes, and provide numerical evidence suggesting that this property extends to high-order cases as well. We present several numerical experiments to validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [184] [Gregory Nested Picard Iteration Schemes for Open Quantum Systems Governed by the Lindblad Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29002)
*Jiuhua Hu,Daniel Appelo,Yingda Cheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出Gregory型嵌套Picard迭代（NPI）格式，用于Lindblad方程的数值模拟。该方法在保持高阶精度和完全正性保迹（CPTP）结构的前提下，通过使用等距节点上的Gregory型求积取代高斯求积，显著降低了计算成本，阶数可达九阶，并通过数值实验验证了收敛性和低秩近似的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算硬件和开放量子系统的数值模拟面临密度矩阵高维度和保持物理性质（如完全正性和保迹）的双重挑战，现有方法计算成本高昂，亟需开发兼具高阶精度、结构保持性和低计算成本的数值格式。

Method: 在前期任意阶低秩CPTP的NPI方法基础上，采用Gregory型求积公式替代高斯求积，在等距节点上构造阶数最高至九的Gregory NPI方案；通过物理基测试方程分析稳定性，并引入低秩逼近技术降低计算复杂度。

Result: Gregory NPI方案在保持高阶精度和CPTP结构的同时，相比高斯求积NPI方案大幅降低了计算成本；数值实验验证了收敛性，低秩近似有效；对CNOT门在闭系统和开量子系统中的性能进行了分析。

Conclusion: Gregory NPI方案为Lindblad方程的高效、高精度、结构保持数值模拟提供了可行的新途径，尤其适用于量子计算硬件的仿真。

Abstract: Numerical simulation of quantum computing hardware and open quantum systems governed by the Lindblad equation is challenging due to the high dimensionality of the density matrix and the need to preserve fundamental physical properties. In our previous work, we developed an arbitrary-order, low-rank, completely positive and trace preserving (CPTP) method for the Lindblad equation with time-dependent Hamiltonians by nested Picard iteration (NPI). In this work, we develop Gregory NPI schemes, which are CPTP schemes constructed by Gregory-type quadrature on equispaced nodes. The methods, which are of order up to nine, substantially reduce the computational cost compared to our previously proposed NPI schemes with Gaussian quadrature rules, while retaining high-order accuracy and structure preservation. We analyze the stability of the resulting scheme for a physics-based test equation. Numerical experiments verify the convergence of the method and demonstrate the effectiveness of the low-rank approximation. We study the performance of a previously constructed CNOT gate for both closed and open quantum systems.

</details>


### [185] [A structure-preserving Chebyshev-filtered subspace iteration for the Bethe-Salpeter eigenvalue problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29025)
*Blanca Mellado-Pinto,Fernando Alvarruiz,Jose E. Roman*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Bethe-Salpeter方程求解中出现的结构化矩阵特征值问题，提出了一种保持Bethe-Salpeter结构的Chebyshev多项式滤波策略与子空间迭代方法，能够高效提取指定区间内的特征值近似，并以实际应用矩阵验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bethe-Salpeter方程在理论物理和应用物理中有广泛用途，其求解归结为具有丰富代数结构的矩阵特征值问题。现有结构保持方法已有部分工作，但需要更高效的算法来提取指定区间内的特征值。

Method: 开发了一种保持Bethe-Salpeter结构的Chebyshev多项式级数作为滤波器，并结合专门设计的保持Bethe-Salpeter结构的子空间迭代方法，确保算法每一步都维持原有结构。

Result: 给出了鲁棒实现所需的所有细节，并通过实际应用中的矩阵算例展示了算法的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的多项式滤波策略能够有效提取指定区间内的特征值近似，且在整个计算过程中保持了Bethe-Salpeter结构。

Abstract: The Bethe-Salpeter equation, which has many applications in both theoretical and applied physics, is generally solved via a matrix eigenvalue problem with a rich algebraic structure. The numerical solution of such structured eigenproblem calls for specific algorithms that are able to preserve the structure throughout the computation. Several structure-preserving methods have already been proposed in the literature. In this paper, we develop a polynomial filter strategy that is able to extract approximations of eigenvalues located inside a specified interval. For this, we have devised a structure-preserving Chebyshev polynomial series, along with a specialized subspace iteration method that preserves the Bethe-Salpeter structure at every step of the algorithm. All necessary details required for a robust implementation are incorporated, and the performance is illustrated with matrices arising from real applications.

</details>


### [186] [A Mass, Momentum, and Energy Conserving Semi-Lagrangian Adaptive-Rank (SLAR) Method for the Vlasov-Poisson System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29027)
*Nanyi Zheng,William A. Sands,Jing-Mei Qiu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半拉格朗日自适应秩（SLAR）方法，结合了半拉格朗日格式的大时间步长能力与自适应秩张量表示的效率，同时强制执行质量、动量和能量的局部守恒律。该方法通过隐式局部宏观守恒（LoMaC）修正技术扩展到高维Vlasov-Poisson系统，并提出了一种统一的自适应权重投影技术，无需手动调参。数值实验证明了方法的精度、效率及守恒性质保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维Vlasov-Poisson系统在长时间模拟中因低秩张量近似导致的守恒性质丧失问题，同时克服传统LoMaC方法需要人工调参的局限性。

Method: 采用半拉格朗日自适应秩（SLAR）框架，结合隐式局部宏观守恒（LoMaC）修正技术，使用向后微分公式离散隐式宏观系统并通过Jacobian-free Newton-Krylov方法求解。提出统一自适应权重投影技术，从解的低秩速度基中构建权重，消除手动调参需求。

Result: 成功实现了2D-2V设置下的Vlasov-Poisson系统隐式LoMaC方法，数值实验验证了方法的精度、效率以及守恒律保持能力。

Conclusion: 首次成功将隐式LoMaC方法应用于高维Vlasov-Poisson系统，所提统一自适应权重投影技术有效提升了方法的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: We propose a semi-Lagrangian adaptive-rank (SLAR) method that combines the large time-step capability of semi-Lagrangian schemes with the efficiency of adaptive-rank tensor representations while simultaneously enforcing local conservation laws for mass, momentum, and energy. The method builds on the high-dimensional SLAR framework introduced in our previous work and achieves high-order accuracy in both space and time. To address the loss of conservation in long-time simulations, we extend the implicit local macroscopic conservative (LoMaC) correction technique for the BGK equation to the high-dimensional Vlasov--Poisson (VP) system. The implicit macroscopic system is discretized using backward differentiation formulas and solved with a Jacobian-free Newton-Krylov method. This approach enables a consistent coupling with semi-Lagrangian methods which are capable of taking large time steps. A novel component of the proposed method is a unified adaptive-weight projection technique that eliminates the ad hoc parameter tuning required by previous LoMaC approaches. These weights capture problem-dependent velocity space structures and are constructed from the low-rank velocity bases of the solution. The local semi-Lagrangian method used in this work reconstructs the solution at the feet of the characteristics using efficient tensor contractions. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first successful implementation of an implicit LoMaC method for the VP system up to the 2D--2V setting. Numerical experiments on several classical benchmark problems demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the proposed method, as well as its ability to preserve conservation laws in VP simulations.

</details>


### [187] [Residual-Guided Dictionary Learning for Spectrally Accurate Koopman Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29083)
*George Coote,Matthew J. Colbrook*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过最小化残差动态模式分解（Residual DMD）误差和优化数据矩阵条件数的神经网络字典学习方法，使学习到的Koopman谱具有可靠性和可验证性，显著减少了谱污染并提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman理论承诺非线性动力系统中的线性结构，但数值计算的Koopman谱容易获得却难以信任——有限EDMD矩阵总有特征值，但许多特征值与无限维算子无关。因此需要让谱可靠性成为字典学习的目标。

Method: 训练神经网络字典时，不仅预测下一快照，还最小化Residual DMD残差（算子级别的后验误差，用于检验特征值和模态是否为真正的Koopman谱对象），同时惩罚提升数据矩阵的条件数，从而耦合小残差与良态表示两个要求。

Result: 在保守和耗散基准系统上，该方法显著减少了谱污染，改善了残差伪谱包含性，降低了相对于标准固定字典的预测误差。在海表温度数据上，从含噪观测中获得了更干净的Koopman诊断和更好的单步预测。

Conclusion: 神经Koopman学习不应仅以预测能力评判，而应以其谱声明的可验证性来评判。残差提供了证书，条件数使证书可计算。

Abstract: Koopman theory promises linear structure in nonlinear dynamics, but numerical Koopman spectra are easy to compute and hard to trust. A finite EDMD matrix always has eigenvalues; the problem is that many of them may have nothing to do with the infinite-dimensional operator. In this paper we make spectral reliability the objective of dictionary learning. We train neural-network dictionaries not merely to predict the next snapshot, but to minimize Residual Dynamic Mode Decomposition residuals: operator-level a posteriori errors that test whether computed eigenvalues and modes are genuine Koopman spectral objects. To keep the learned observables from collapsing into an unstable coordinate system, the loss also penalizes the condition number of the lifted data matrix. Thus the method couples two requirements that should not be separated: small Koopman residuals and a well-conditioned representation. The result is a learned dictionary that is expressive, numerically stable, and spectrally disciplined. Across conservative and dissipative benchmark systems, the method sharply reduces spectral pollution, improves residual pseudospectral inclusion, and lowers forecast error relative to standard fixed dictionaries. On sea-surface temperature data, it gives cleaner Koopman diagnostics and substantially better one-step forecasts from noisy observations with no governing equations. The message is simple: neural Koopman learning should be judged not by prediction alone, but by whether its spectral claims can be certified. Residuals provide the certificate; conditioning makes it computable.

</details>


### [188] [Consistent CutPINNs for Convection-Diffusion Equations on Curved Level-Set Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29147)
*Maneesh Kumar Singh*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对稳态对流扩散方程（含弯曲水平集域），提出了相容损失PINNs的先验误差分析。标准均方内损失在对流主导区域失效，因边界层内残差增长至O(ε⁻¹)，导致损失被少数点主导而无法解析光滑主体。通过引入离散L^γ范数（γ=1+1/log(1/ε)）作为H^{-1}稳定项的可计算替代，并结合离散H^{1/2}迹范数一致处理边界条件，在Besov正则性假设下证明了对所有γ∈(1,2]有效的单一先验H^1误差界，恢复率受弯曲几何特有的cut-cell下限1/(2γ)控制。矩形与圆盘上的数值实验（ε=2^{-s}, s=2,4,6）证实：随层变陡，L^2内损失变得种子敏感，而L^γ内损失训练可靠，内范数是收敛性的决定性因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINNs的均方内损失在对流主导区域失效：解形成O(ε)边界层，点态残差增长至O(ε⁻¹)，导致损失被层内少数配置点主导，无法解析光滑主体区域。

Method: 采用相容损失PINNs：用离散L^γ范数（γ=1+1/log(1/ε)，作为H^{-1}稳定项的可计算替代）惩罚内部残差，用离散H^{1/2}迹范数统一处理平坦和弯曲几何的边界条件。在Besov正则性假设下推导先验H^1误差界。

Result: 证明了对所有γ∈(1,2]有效的单一先验H^1误差界，恢复率受弯曲几何特有的cut-cell下限1/(2γ)控制。数值实验（矩形和圆盘，ε=2^{-s}, s=2,4,6）验证：L^γ内损失训练可靠且收敛决定性因素，而L^2内损失种子敏感。

Conclusion: 所提相容损失PINNs有效解决了对流主导区域标准损失失效问题，内范数选择是收敛性的决定性因素。

Abstract: We present an a priori error analysis of consistent-loss PINNs for stationary convection-diffusion equations on curved level-set domains. The standard mean-squared interior loss fails in the convection-dominated regime: the solution develops an $O(\eps)$ boundary layer in which the pointwise residual grows like $\eps^{-1}$, so the loss is dominated by the few collocation points inside the layer and leaves the smooth bulk unresolved. We remove this mismatch by penalising the interior residual in a discrete $\Lpγ$ norm with $γ= 1 + 1/\log\mtil$, a computable surrogate for the $\Hminusone$ stability term, and imposing the boundary condition through a discrete $\HhalfBdry$ trace norm, which treats flat and curved geometries uniformly. Under Besov regularity assumptions we prove a single a priori $\Hone$ error bound, valid for all interior exponents $γ\in (1,2]$, with an optimal recovery rate governed by a cut-cell floor $1/(2γ)$ specific to the curved geometry. Numerical experiments on a rectangle and a disk at $\eps = 2^{-s}$, $s \in \{2,4,6\}$, confirm the analysis: as the layer sharpens, the $\Lp{2}$ interior loss becomes seed-fragile while the $\Lpγ$ interior trains reliably, the interior norm being the decisive factor in convergence.

</details>


### [189] [Second-Order Area/Volume-Preserving PFEMs for Surface Diffusion via Simpson--Boole Geometric Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29195)
*Zhiqing Pan,Jiwei Jia,Lian Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于二维封闭曲线和三维封闭曲面表面扩散的二阶时间参数有限元方法，基于精确几何变分恒等式和Simpson/Boole数值积分，实现了无需拉格朗日乘子的面积/体积精确守恒，并在BGN预测辅助几何上组装，数值实验验证了二阶精度、守恒性和良好网格质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表面扩散数值方法大多仅有一阶时间精度，或需要引入辅助拉格朗日乘子来强制几何约束。本文旨在构造无需拉格朗日乘子、自然满足面积/体积守恒的二阶时间格式，同时与已有BGN方法兼容。

Method: 基于二次时间插值路径上的精确几何变分恒等式，二维情形采用Simpson法则精确计算面积变分，三维情形采用Boole法则精确计算体积变分；全离散格式在BGN预测的辅助几何上进行组装，无需额外拉格朗日乘子。

Result: 数值实验显示该方法在曲线和曲面演化中均达到二阶时间收敛阶，精确保持封闭面积/体积，且网格质量良好。

Conclusion: 成功设计了兼具二阶时间精度、精确面积/体积守恒、良好网格质量且与BGN格式兼容的表面扩散数值格式。

Abstract: We propose second-order-in-time parametric finite element methods for surface diffusion of closed curves in two dimensions and closed surfaces in three dimensions. The construction is based on exact geometric variation identities along a quadratic temporal interpolation path. The induced area variation in 2D is evaluated exactly by Simpson's rule, while the induced volume variation in 3D is evaluated exactly by Boole's rule. The resulting fully discrete schemes preserve the enclosed area or volume exactly, without introducing an auxiliary Lagrange multiplier for the geometric constraint. They can be assembled on BGN-predicted auxiliary geometries and are therefore compatible with existing second-order BGN-type implementations. Numerical experiments demonstrate the expected second-order behavior, area/volume conservation, and good mesh quality for both curve and surface evolutions.

</details>


### [190] [A Multi-Level Machine Learning Framework for Inverse Scattering Problems with Multi-Frequency Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29368)
*Yi Liu,Yanzhao Cao,Junshan Lin,Yimin Zhong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多层级机器学习框架，通过沿频率轴逐层构建神经网络，利用多频数据求解逆散射问题。该框架在每层频率上更新重建结果，逐步恢复成像目标的高阶傅里叶模态，并将整体学习问题分解为一系列单频率的简单局部任务，显著降低优化复杂度并避免陷入不良局部最小值。数值实验和神经正切核理论分析验证了该方法的有效性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆散射问题具有高度非线性和病态性，传统方法在处理多频数据时面临计算复杂度高和优化困难的问题。现有方法难以有效利用频率维度信息，且容易收敛到不理想的局部最小值，迫切需要一种能够渐进学习多频信息、降低优化难度的鲁棒框架。

Method: 提出多层级机器学习框架，沿频率轴逐层构建神经网络：在每个固定频率上增加新层级网络更新重建结果。整体学习问题被分解为一系列单频率的局部任务，通过频率逐层推进，网络深度增长时逐步获取更高阶傅里叶模态。

Result: 针对逆源散射问题和逆介质散射问题的多组数值实验验证了该框架的有效性和鲁棒性；神经正切核理论分析表明多层级架构能够渐进恢复成像目标的高阶傅里叶分量。

Conclusion: 所提出的多频多层级神经网络框架通过频率逐层递增的策略，有效降低了逆散射问题的优化复杂度，避免了不良局部最小值，并能渐进学习目标的高频信息，为求解逆散射问题提供了一种鲁棒且可靠的机器学习方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a multi-level machine learning framework for solving inverse scattering problems with multi-frequency data. The multi-level neural network is built along the frequency axis of the scattering problem, wherein at each fixed frequency, a new level of network is added to the existing architecture to update the reconstruction. By marching through the frequency levels, the proposed multi-level computational framework is able to obtain higher-order Fourier modes of the imaging target as the depth of the neural network grows and higher-frequency data are used. Furthermore, the overall learning problem is decomposed into a sequence of simpler local tasks, each associated with a single frequency. This decomposition significantly reduces the complexity of the optimization problem and mitigates the risk of convergence to undesirable local minima, resulting in a robust and reliable training procedure for solving inverse scattering problems. We conduct various numerical experiments for the inverse source scattering problem and the inverse medium scattering problem to illustrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed machine learning framework. In addition, theoretical analysis in the neural tangent kernel regime shows that the proposed multi-level architecture progressively recovers the higher-order Fourier components of the imaging target.

</details>


### [191] [McMg: A Learned Phase-Space Multi-channel Multigrid Preconditioner for Helmholtz Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30495)
*Jiwei Jia,Xinliang Liu,Juntao Wang,Jinchao Xu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出多通道多重网格（McMg），一种基于学习相空间的多重网格预条件器，用于求解高波数异质亥姆霍兹方程。其核心思想是在粗化物理空间的同时，在通道维度保留未分辨的局部波信息（振幅、相位、方向、散射系数），结合线性多通道传输算子、局部自适应模板、神经PDE算子和介质相关平滑器，在固定介质下实现线性V-cycle迭代，并支持跨尺度泛化与逐层渐进微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高波数异质亥姆霍兹方程求解面临三大挑战：离散化算子不定性、污染效应降低相位精度、标量粗网格校正丢失振荡误差携带的局部相位和传播方向信息。现有方法难以兼顾效率和精度。

Method: 提出多通道多重网格（McMg）预条件器：每个粗网格节点携带学习的波包（振幅、相位、方向、散射系数）而非单一标量未知量；架构结合线性多通道传输算子、局部自适应模板、神经PDE算子和介质相关平滑器；固定介质下V-cycle关于残差线性；采用逐层渐进微调（LLPF）策略扩展格林算子支持范围。

Result: 在高频、高对比度、大规模三维问题的数值实验中，McMg相比强经典基线方法需要显著更少的迭代次数和更短的墙钟时间，且持续优于现有神经预条件器。

Conclusion: McMg通过相空间多通道粗化策略有效保留了振荡误差的局部波信息，结合线性化V-cycle和跨尺度泛化能力，为高波数异质亥姆霍兹方程提供了高效、可扩展的求解方案。

Abstract: Solving heterogeneous Helmholtz equations at high wavenumbers remains challenging because the discretized operator is indefinite, pollution degrades phase accuracy, and scalar coarse-grid correction can discard the local phase and propagation-direction information carried by oscillatory errors. We propose Multi-channel Multigrid (McMg), a learned phase-space multigrid preconditioner for heterogeneous Helmholtz equations. Rather than predicting the solution directly, McMg maps residuals to corrections within an iterative framework. Its central idea is to coarsen physical space while retaining unresolved local wave information in the channel dimension: each coarse node carries a learned packet of amplitude, phase, direction, and scattering coefficients rather than a single scalar unknown. The architecture combines linear multi-channel transfer operators with locally adaptive stencils, neural PDE operators, and medium-dependent smoothers whose coefficients are generated from the wave speed. For a fixed medium, the V-cycle is linear in the residual; nonlinear physical features are computed once in a setup phase and cached, so each online iteration reduces to convolutions with fixed coefficients. We further study generalization across scales. Models trained on small domains transfer directly to larger domains and higher effective wavenumbers, and a Layer-by-Layer Progressive Finetuning (LLPF) strategy extends the support of the learned Green's operator by adding and finetuning only new coarse levels. Numerical experiments on high-frequency, high-contrast, and large-scale three-dimensional problems demonstrate that McMg requires substantially fewer iterations and less wall-clock time than strong classical baselines, while consistently outperforming existing neural preconditioners.

</details>


### [192] [Fourier Neural Operators with Least-Squares Readout Refit for Learning Random Obstacle-to-Solution Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29436)
*Chenhui Zhu,Fei Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对随机障碍物-解映射的算子学习问题，提出了一种后训练最小二乘读出层修正方法（FNO-LS），在固定网格上直接学习随机障碍物样本到解的映射，有效提升了接触集恢复和障碍物违反等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆变分不等式中的随机障碍物-解映射学习具有挑战性，因为解不仅依赖于障碍场本身，还受诱导接触集和自由边界几何的影响。现有方法（如DeepONet、标准FNO）在复杂接触几何下表现有限。

Method: 在标准FNO端到端训练完成后，冻结其非线性骨干网络，通过求解所有训练样本和网格点上诱导的线性最小二乘问题重新计算最后的仿射读出层，得到经验平方误差最优的读出权重。

Result: 在两种不同振幅水平的障碍物系综中，FNO-LS在平均场精度、接触集恢复和障碍物违反指标上均优于vanilla DeepONet、POD-DeepONet、两阶段DeepONet和标准FNO，尤其在高振幅、复杂接触几何场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: 最小二乘读出层修正是一种简单有效的后训练增强方法，特别适用于FNO骨干网络信息丰富但尚未完全收敛的场景。

Abstract: We study operator learning for random obstacle-to-solution maps arising from elliptic variational inequalities with finite-band self-affine random obstacle fields. Instead of introducing an explicit truncated stochastic parametrization of the random input, we learn the map directly from sampled obstacle realizations on a fixed grid. This problem is challenging because the solution is governed not only by the obstacle field itself, but also by the induced contact set and free-boundary geometry. We introduce a post-training least-squares readout refit for the Fourier neural operator (FNO). After the FNO is trained end to end, its nonlinear backbone is frozen and the final affine readout is recomputed by solving the induced linear least-squares problem over all training samples and grid points. The refit yields the empirical squared-error optimal readout for the learned frozen features while leaving the nonlinear representation unchanged. We compare vanilla DeepONet, POD-DeepONet, a two-stage DeepONet baseline, FNO, and FNO with least-squares readout refit (FNO-LS) on two obstacle ensembles with different amplitude levels. Numerical results show that FNO-LS achieves the strongest overall performance among the tested models, particularly for higher-amplitude obstacles with more complex contact geometry. The method improves average field accuracy, contact-set recovery, and obstacle-violation metrics at low additional cost, especially when the FNO backbone is informative but not fully converged. These results suggest that least-squares readout refit is a simple and effective post-training enhancement for learning random obstacle-to-solution maps.

</details>


### [193] [Fundamental weak convergence theorem for stochastic Volterra integral equations and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29458)
*Xinjie Dai,Qijiao Yin,Diancong Jin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机Volterra积分方程（SVIEs）数值近似的弱收敛速率。通过结合马尔可夫提升技巧、多米诺论证、泰勒展开和路径泛函的Fréchet微分演算，建立了一个基础的弱收敛定理，为多种数值近似方法提供了统一的弱误差分析框架。作为应用，推导了随机theta方法和Wong-Zakai近似的一阶弱收敛率，放宽了现有假设，并首次给出了SVIEs的Wong-Zakai近似弱收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVIEs是一类非马尔可夫模型，在随机波动率建模等领域有重要应用，但其非马尔可夫本性导致经典弱误差技术无法直接应用，因此需要发展新的分析方法。

Method: 将马尔可夫提升技巧与多米诺论证、泰勒展开以及路径泛函的Fréchet微分演算相结合，建立统一的弱收敛定理。

Result: 对于非奇异SVIEs，建立了基础的弱收敛定理；针对随机theta方法和Wong-Zakai近似推导了一阶弱收敛率；去除了扩散系数有界性的要求；首次获得SVIEs的Wong-Zakai近似弱收敛结果。

Conclusion: 所提方法为SVIEs数值近似的弱误差分析提供了统一框架，数值实验验证了理论收敛率。

Abstract: We study weak convergence rates of numerical approximations for stochastic Volterra integral equations (SVIEs), a class of non-Markovian models that arises naturally in stochastic volatility modeling and other fields. The intrinsic non-Markovian nature prevents the direct application of classical weak error techniques developed for finite-dimensional Markov processes. To overcome this difficulty, we combine a Markovian lifting technique with a domino argument, Taylor expansions, and Fréchet differential calculus for path-dependent functionals, and establish a fundamental weak convergence theorem for nonsingular SVIEs, providing a unified approach to the weak error analysis for a broad class of numerical approximations. As applications, we derive the first-order weak convergence rate for the stochastic theta method and the Wong--Zakai approximation. Our results relax existing assumptions for Euler-type schemes by removing the boundedness requirement on the diffusion coefficient. Furthermore, to the best of our knowledge, this work provides the first weak convergence result for Wong--Zakai approximations of SVIEs. Numerical experiments for a stochastic volatility model corroborate the theoretical convergence rate.

</details>


### [194] [Stable Positive Integral Deferred Correction Methods for Positive Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29523)
*Pezzella Mario*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类Stable Positive Integral Deferred Correction (SPIDeC)方法，用于正动力系统的数值积分，通过将延迟校正机制嵌入指数型Volterra重整化公式中，实现了无条件保持正性、平衡态、L-稳定性和任意高阶精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数值积分方法在处理正动力系统时，往往无法同时保证正性保持、平衡态保持和高阶精度，且稳定性受步长限制。SPIDeC旨在设计一种无条件保持系统关键结构性质（正性、平衡态）且能实现任意高阶精度的数值方法。

Method: 将延迟校正(Deferred Correction)机制嵌入到指数型Volterra重整化公式中，构造乘性结构以保证正性和平衡态的无条件保持；通过显式逐次校正(explicit-in-sweep corrections)从低阶基近似逐步提升至任意高阶精度；采用Gauss-Radau求积节点时，离散流渐近收敛为对数收缩映射以保证稳定性。

Result: SPIDeC方法无条件保持正性和平衡态（与步长无关）；实现L-稳定；精确再现对角线性算子生成的连续半群；采用Gauss-Radau节点时，随校正次数增加离散流渐近趋于对数收缩映射，确保稳定性；数值实验验证了理论与实际性能。

Conclusion: SPIDeC为一类兼具正性保持、平衡态保持、L-稳定性和任意高阶精度的数值积分方法，特别适用于正动力系统的长期数值积分。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the class of Stable Positive Integral Deferred Correction (SPIDeC) methods for the numerical integration of positive dynamical systems. The proposed framework embeds a deferred correction mechanism within an exponential-type Volterra reformulation of the underlying differential problem. The resulting multiplicative structure guarantees the unconditional preservation of both positivity and equilibria, independently of the integration stepsize. Arbitrarily high-order accuracy is systematically achieved through successive explicit-in-sweep corrections applied to a low-order base approximation. From a stability viewpoint, the SPIDeC integrators are L-stable and exactly reproduce the continuous semigroup generated by diagonal linear operators. Furthermore, when Gauss--Radau quadrature nodes are employed, the associated discrete flow asymptotically approaches a logarithmically contractive map as the number of sweeps increases, ensuring stability. Numerical experiments are provided to validate the theoretical analysis and illustrate the practical performance of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [195] [Hypocoercivity-preserving space-time Galerkin methods for kinetic Fokker-Planck equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29621)
*Zhaonan Dong,Emmanuil H. Georgoulis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文设计并分析了一类保余摩擦性的全离散Galerkin方法，用于求解非齐次动力学Fokker-Planck方程，该方法通过指数加权范数实现强制双线性形式，结合C0内罚空间离散和hp型DG时间离散，证明了指数收敛到平衡态，并保留了总质量守恒特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学Fokker-Planck方程是一类具有退化扩散的演化偏微分方程，其长期模拟面临数值方法难以保持指数收敛到平衡态的挑战。现有方法缺乏对余摩擦性（hypocoercivity）的系统保持，需要设计能够精确反映该结构的数值格式。

Method: 基于Villani的增强二次型框架，在指数加权范数下构造强制双线性形式；空间离散采用连续分片多项式有限元（单纯形/箱型/半无限棱柱网格），通过C0内罚方法处理非协调性；时间离散采用hp版本间断Galerkin时域方法，任意阶精度；新证明了一类指数权重下的多项式逆迹不等式。

Result: 建立了Galerkin方法的强制性与指数收敛性（通过指数加权Poincaré不等式）；全离散格式保持总质量守恒并实现指数收敛；数值实验验证了理论结果与收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该方法系统性地保持kFP方程的余摩擦性结构，为长时间动力学模拟提供了可靠的数值框架，新证明的逆迹不等式具有独立理论价值。

Abstract: We design and analyse a family of hypocoercivity-preserving fully discrete Galerkin methods for the (inhomogeneous) kinetic Fokker--Planck (kFP) equations, a class of evolution PDEs with degenerate diffusion. The proposed methods mimic Villani's framework of enhanced quadratic forms [23], yielding a coercive bilinear form in an exponentially weighted norm that admits a spectral gap/Poincaré inequality despite the degeneracy. The problem is formulated as a fourth-order-in-space evolution PDE on the whole space $\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^d$. The spatial discretisation employs continuous piecewise polynomial finite element spaces on simplicial and/or box-type meshes comprising both finite and ``infinite'' elements, while nonconformity is handled by numerical fluxes in the spirit of $C^0$ interior penalty ($C^0$-IP) methods. The analysis requires new polynomial inverse trace inequalities in exponentially weighted norms for simplicial, box-type, and semi-infinite prismatic elements, which are proved for a broad class of exponential weights and are of independent interest. Coercivity of the Galerkin method then leads to exponential convergence to equilibrium via an exponentially weighted Poincaré inequality. We further develop a fully discrete scheme by coupling the spatial discretisation with an $hp$-version discontinuous Galerkin time-stepping method of arbitrary order and establish the same exponential convergence. The proposed methods preserve the total mass and exhibit \emph{provably} exponential convergence to equilibrium, making them well suited for long-time kFP simulations. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical results and demonstrate the convergence behaviour of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [196] [Fourier--Hankel Moment Methods for Topological Counting and Phase-Center Recovery in Acoustic Inverse Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29678)
*Xiaomei Yang,Zhiliang Deng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fourier-Hankel矩框架的方法，从全孔径声学远场数据中提取拓扑计数信息。该方法利用分离局部组件在角向Fourier数据中产生不同相位中心这一观察，通过Bessel-Fourier矩恒等式将适当缩放的Fourier系数转化为有限指数矩序列，其Hankel矩阵的秩等于分离连通组件的数量，Hankel束可恢复相位中心位置。论文证明了精确Hankel秩公式，建立了扰动定理，并将框架扩展到可检测空腔（引入符号相位中心模型），同时揭示了同心环等退化情形。数值实验验证了该理论在理想矩序列、Born远场数据、相位中心恢复、空腔计数以及精确Helmholtz远场数据等多个层面上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从全孔径声学远场数据中提取拓扑计数信息（如分离连通组件的数量和空腔数量），提供一种数据层面的代数方法，避免传统成像方法中复杂的正向模型求解。

Method: 1. 建立Fourier-Hankel矩框架，利用角向Fourier系数构造指数矩序列；2. 构建Hankel矩阵，其秩等于分离连通组件数量；3. 利用Hankel束恢复相位中心位置；4. 引入符号相位中心模型扩展至空腔检测，通过超出正组件计数的超额秩来计数可检测空腔。

Result: 推导了精确Hankel秩公式，建立了扰动定理（奇异值间隙条件下稳定计数），揭示了空腔检测的退化情形（同心环等），并通过多个层级的数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Fourier-Hankel秩机制提供了一种数据层面的代数方法，用于组件计数和可检测空腔计数，同时明确了其稳定性条件和失效模式。

Abstract: We develop a Fourier--Hankel moment framework for extracting topological counting information from full-aperture acoustic far-field data. The method is based on the observation that separated localized components generate distinct phase centers in angular Fourier data. Under the Born approximation, a Bessel--Fourier moment identity shows that suitably scaled row Fourier coefficients form, to leading order, a finite exponential moment sequence. The associated Hankel matrix has rank equal to the number of separated connected components, and the corresponding Hankel pencil recovers their phase-center locations. We prove the exact Hankel rank formula in the phase-center model and establish a perturbation theorem showing stable component counting under a singular-gap condition. We further extend the framework to detectable cavities by introducing a signed phase-center model. In this model, material components and cavities contribute with opposite signs to the moment sequence. The signed Hankel rank counts distinct signed phase centers, and the detectable cavity count is obtained from the excess rank beyond the positive component count. This formulation also identifies an intrinsic degeneracy: cavities whose phase centers coincide with material phase centers, such as perfectly concentric annuli, do not increase the leading signed rank and therefore cannot be detected by the leading phase-center mechanism alone. Numerical experiments validate the proposed theory at several levels: ideal moment sequences, Born far-field data with finite-size components, phase-center location recovery, signed cavity counting, and exact Helmholtz far-field data. The results show that the Fourier--Hankel rank mechanism provides a data-level algebraic approach to component counting and detectable cavity counting, while also making explicit its stability conditions and failure modes.

</details>


### [197] [Implementation of Hyperelastic Physics-Augmented Neural Networks in the Explicit Finite Element Codes Simcenter Radioss and OpenRadioss with Applications to Impact Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29874)
*Lukas Maurer,Sascha Eisenträger,Marian Bulla,Daniel Juhre*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将物理增强神经网络（PANNs）集成到显式有限元求解器（Simcenter Radioss/OpenRadioss）中的方法，通过自动化生成Fortran用户材料子程序，实现基于机器学习的本构模型在工程仿真中的应用，并重点优化了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统材料模型难以捕捉复杂本构行为，而数据驱动方法虽能突破这一限制，但需要可靠的物理约束。PANNs通过嵌入物理约束兼具灵活性与可靠性，如何将其高效集成到现有工业级显式有限元求解器中是关键挑战。

Method: 构建通用框架，将预训练的PANNs（PyTorch/TensorFlow）及其参数迁移至独立的Fortran用户材料子程序；使用SQuarePlus激活函数替代SoftPlus以降低计算成本；开发自动化GitHub代码库生成用户材料子程序。

Result: 生成的PANN用户材料能够再现超弹性材料在大应变下的非线性行为；SQuarePlus激活函数相比SoftPlus降低了计算开销；网络架构对仿真性能有显著影响。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条将机器学习本构模型应用于显式有限元仿真的实用路径，无需专用求解器，兼顾精度与效率，可推广至其他机器学习框架。

Abstract: Data-driven material modeling techniques have gained significant attention due to their ability to capture complex constitutive behaviors beyond the limitations of classical material models. Physics-augmented neural networks (PANNs), which embed physical constraints directly into their architecture, combine the flexibility of machine learning with the reliability required for engineering simulations. This work presents an approach to integrate such network architectures into the explicit finite element solvers Simcenter Radioss and OpenRadioss (Siemens). A framework for transferring pretrained network architectures and their parameters to a standalone user material routine is developed. Networks are trained using PyTorch, though the procedure can be adapted to other frameworks such as TensorFlow, enabling the use of PANNs within existing finite element technology without requiring specialized solvers. Particular emphasis is placed on computational efficiency. The influence of network architecture on simulation performance is investigated, and strategies for reducing evaluation costs while preserving accuracy are discussed. Specifically, replacing the SoftPlus activation function with SQuarePlus is shown to reduce computational cost. A publicly available GitHub repository automates the generation of Fortran user material routines, requiring only the specification of the network architecture and trained parameters. An example impact simulation demonstrates that the generated PANN user material reproduces the nonlinear behavior characteristic of hyperelastic materials under large strains, providing a practical route toward machine-learning-based constitutive models in explicit finite element simulations.

</details>


### [198] [Dirichlet-Neumann waveform relaxation for heterogeneous heat equations: continuous and time discrete L2 analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29987)
*Niklas Kotarsky,Philipp Birken,Martin J. Gander,Lu-di Lu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同空间域上通过低维界面耦合的两个线性热方程（共轭热传递），采用Dirichlet-Neumann波形松弛法进行求解，推导了连续和离散时间设置下的新误差估计，识别了保证超线性收敛的优化松弛参数，并发现大对比度下收敛速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是开发更高效的分区耦合方法，需要可靠的误差估计来指导算法设计。

Method: 采用指数加权傅里叶技术，在连续和离散时间设置下推导L2误差估计，分析优化松弛参数，并进行数值实验验证。

Result: 连续估计：大T时线性收敛，小T时超线性收敛；离散估计：大T时与连续估计一致，小T时超线性收敛需小时间步长；大对比度（如空气-钢）下选择合适的传输条件可加速收敛。

Conclusion: Dirichlet-Neumann波形松弛法在共轭热传递问题中可实现超线性收敛，优化松弛参数和合理选择传输条件可显著提升效率。

Abstract: We consider two coupled linear heat equations on different spatial domains that interact through a lower dimensional interface. This models conjugate heat transfer. The problem is solved using Dirichlet-Neumann waveform relaxation. This allows us to couple separate codes for the subproblems, a so-called partitioned approach. Our overall goal is to develop more efficient partitioned methods, and to this end, we want reliable error estimates.
  We use an exponentially weighted Fourier technique to derive new error estimates in L2 for finite time T in both continuous and time discrete settings. We identify an optimized relaxation parameter that guarantees superlinear convergence. Our new continuous estimate predicts linear convergence when T is large, and superlinear when T is small. For large T, our new time discrete estimate closely mirrors its continuous counterpart, whereas for small T, superlinear convergence in the time discrete case requires small time step dt. We also show that convergence is fast when the contrast is large, provided that the small physical parameter domain (e.g. air) is using the Dirichlet transmission condition, and the large physical parameter domain (e.g. steel) is using the Neumann transmission condition in the Dirichlet-Neumann waveform relaxation method. Our numerical experiments confirm all these findings.

</details>


### [199] [A polynomial moment approach to a rank condition for continuous-stage Runge--Kutta methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30122)
*Yuto Miyatake*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了多项式连续级龙格-库塔方法中，在标准一致性条件下矩阵Φ^CSRK具有满行秩，从而验证了Miyatake和Butcher在2016年提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Miyatake和Butcher (2016) 提出的猜想：在一致的多项式连续级龙格-库塔方法中，矩阵Φ^CSRK是否总是具有满行秩。该猜想的成立与否直接影响能量守恒方法的充分必要条件。

Method: 利用Pakovich和Muzychuk (2009) 解决的多项式矩问题，直接证明在标准一致性条件下矩阵Φ^CSRK具有满行秩。

Result: 证明了在标准一致性条件下，矩阵Φ^CSRK具有满行秩，即Miyatake和Butcher提出的猜想成立。

Conclusion: 该结果完善了能量守恒多项式连续级龙格-库塔方法的理论基础，确认了对所有一致的方法，对称性既是充分条件也是必要条件。

Abstract: In the study of energy-preserving methods for Hamiltonian systems, polynomial continuous-stage Runge--Kutta methods play an important role. Necessary and sufficient conditions for such methods to be energy-preserving have already been established. They are energy-preserving if the matrix $M\in \mathbb{R}^{s\times s}$ defining the method is symmetric, and the converse holds under the assumption that a certain $s\times \infty$ matrix $Φ^\mathrm{CSRK}$ has full row rank. It was conjectured in Remark 3 in Miyatake and Butcher (SIAM J. Numer. Anal., 2016) that the full-rank assumption should always hold for every consistent polynomial continuous-stage Runge--Kutta method. In this paper, we prove the conjecture by showing that the matrix $Φ^\mathrm{CSRK}$ has full row rank under the standard consistency condition. The proof is a direct application of the polynomial moment problem solved by Pakovich and Muzychuk (Proc. Lond. Math. Soc., 2009).

</details>


### [200] [Low-Rank Tensor Completion using Tensor Train Decomposition via Riemannian Optimization on the Quotient Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30173)
*Zhenlong Hu,Zhongming Chen,Defeng Sun,Liping Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文利用张量列（TT）分解的左正交性构造了一个新的商流形并引入一族可接受黎曼度量，基于此提出了两种兼容商结构的回缩（基于递归极分解和QR分解），进而推导出黎曼梯度下降和共轭梯度方法用于张量补全。理论上，该方法将每块未知数从TT秩的二次依赖降至近半，提升了计算效率；实验表明重建精度与最先进的TT几何方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于TT-format的低秩张量补全方法在商流形框架下计算效率仍存在提升空间，尤其是每块未知数对TT秩的二次依赖导致计算开销较大。本文旨在通过构造新的商流形和兼容回缩来降低计算复杂度。

Method: 1) 利用TT分解的左正交性质构造新的商流形并引入一族可接受黎曼度量；2) 提出基于递归极分解和QR分解的两种新回缩，兼容商结构；3) 基于该几何框架推导黎曼梯度下降和共轭梯度方法求解张量补全问题。

Result: 理论上，每块未知数从对TT秩的二次依赖降至近半，显著提升了计算效率。数值实验表明，该算法在重建精度上与最先进的TT几何方法相当。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于新商流形和递归回缩的黎曼优化方法有效降低了TT张量补全的计算复杂度，同时保持了竞争性的重建精度，为张量补全提供了更高效的几何优化框架。

Abstract: Owing to the effectiveness of Tensor Train (TT) decomposition in managing high-order tensors, low-rank tensor completion within the TT-format has emerged as a prominent research focus. In this paper, we leverage the left-orthogonal property of the TT-decomposition to construct a novel quotient manifold and introduce a family of admissible Riemannian metrics. Within this geometric framework, we propose a new approach to constructing retractions compatible with the quotient structure, realized via two novel retractions based on recursive polar and QR decompositions that respect the recursive orthogonalization structure of the TT format. We then derive Riemannian gradient descent and conjugate gradient methods to solve the tensor completion problem. Theoretically, our approach streamlines the horizontal projection by reducing the number of unknowns per block from a quadratic dependence on the TT-ranks to a near-half scaling, thereby enhancing computational efficiency over conventional quotient-based methods. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithms achieve reconstruction accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art TT-based geometric methods.

</details>


### [201] [On modified anti-Gaussian rules for Jacobi weight functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30305)
*Eleonora Denich,Paolo Novati,Alvise Sommariva*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究针对 Jacobi 权函数的反高斯公式不内蕴问题，提出通过引入依赖节点数 n 的参数 θ(n) 构造修正反高斯公式，并理论分析修正平均高斯公式的渐近收敛速率，最后通过数值实验验证方法有效性，所有 Matlab 代码均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准反高斯公式应用于 Jacobi 权函数时无法保证所有节点落在积分区间内（即不内蕴），这限制了其在实际计算中的应用。本文旨在解决这一内蕴性问题，使反高斯公式和平均高斯公式能更广泛地应用于 Jacobi 权函数情形。

Method: 引入依赖于高斯节点数 n 的可调参数 θ(n)，构造所谓的修正反高斯公式（modified anti-Gaussian rule），通过调节 θ 使得求积节点全部落在积分区间内部。

Result: 理论推导了修正平均高斯公式的渐近收敛速率，并通过数值实验展示了该方法相比标准公式在误差估计中的优势。

Conclusion: 修正反高斯公式有效克服了 Jacobi 权函数下节点不内蕴的问题，为高斯求积公式的动态误差估计提供了更可靠的数值工具，且开源 Matlab 代码便于复现和应用。

Abstract: Anti-Gaussian formulas represent an efficient tool for a dynamical estimation of the error of the underlying Gaussian rule. When applied to the Jacobi weight function it is known that such formulas are not always internal. In this work we show how to overcome this problem by using the so called modified anti-Gaussian rule with suitable parameter θ = θ(n), that depends on the number n of quadrature points of the Gaussian formula. Next we study theoretically the asymptotic rate of convergence of the corresponding modified averaged Gaussian formulas. We conclude by showing the benefits of this approach via numerical experiments. All the Matlab codes used in this work are available as open-source software.

</details>


### [202] [Adjoint-Based Bayesian Uncertainty Quantification for PDE-Constrained Inverse Problems with Application to Semiconductor Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30340)
*Hassan Yazdanian,Leila Taghizadeh,Babak Maboudi Afkham*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯框架，用于从边界通量测量中重建pn结半导体器件的掺杂分布。通过将未知掺杂场建模为具有未知界面和两个平台浓度的分段常数函数，引入通过Sigmoid变换映射潜在高斯场的推送前验，并结合No-U-Turn采样器（NUTS）进行后验采样。数值实验表明，该方法相比pCN采样器在有效样本量上提升1-2个数量级，并能提供空间分辨的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pn结半导体器件的掺杂分布重建是一个由泊松-玻尔兹曼型方程控制的非线性不适定逆问题，现有方法难以同时处理分段常数结构、界面不确定性以及高效的后验采样，亟需一种能够实现可靠不确定性量化的贝叶斯框架。

Method: 将掺杂场建模为分段常数函数（未知界面+两个平台浓度）；引入推送前验：通过Sigmoid变换映射具有Matérn型协方差的潜在高斯场，用截断Karhunen-Loève展开参数化潜在场，用标量参数表示平台浓度；采用No-U-Turn Sampler（NUTS）结合伴随法梯度进行后验采样；证明了前向映射的Lipschitz连续性和后验的Hellinger稳定性。

Result: 所提方法与pCN采样器相比，有效样本量提升1-2个数量级；在已知平台浓度设定下，成功重建了平面和弯曲界面，并提供了空间分辨的不确定性量化；在联合推断界面几何与平台浓度时，后验相关性揭示了结构不可识别性。

Conclusion: 将推送前验与伴随梯度采样相结合，能够在具有尖锐界面的非线性PDE约束逆问题中实现可靠的不确定性量化，验证了该方法在半导体器件掺杂重建中的有效性。

Abstract: We formulate a Bayesian framework for reconstructing doping profiles in pn-junction semiconductor devices from boundary flux measurements. The unknown doping field is modeled as a piecewise-constant function characterized by an unknown interface and two plateau concentrations, leading to a nonlinear ill-posed inverse problem governed by a Poisson-Boltzmann-type equation. To represent this structure while enabling efficient gradient-based inference, we introduce a pushforward prior constructed by mapping a latent Gaussian field with Matérn-type covariance through a sigmoid transformation. The latent field is parameterized by a truncated Karhunen-Loève expansion, while the two piecewise-constant levels are represented by scalar plateau parameters. The prior yields differentiable approximations of piecewise-constant fields with controllable interface sharpness. We establish well-posedness of the Bayesian formulation by proving Lipschitz continuity of the forward map and Hellinger stability of the posterior. We then sample the posterior using the No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS) with gradients computed by the adjoint method. Numerical experiments show that the combination of the proposed prior and NUTS provides more efficient posterior exploration than the dimension-robust preconditioned Crank-Nicolson (pCN) sampler, yielding one to two orders of magnitude larger effective sample sizes. In the known-plateau setting, the method reconstructs both planar and curved interfaces and provides spatially resolved uncertainty quantification (UQ). When the interface geometry and plateau concentrations are inferred jointly, posterior correlations reveal structural non-identifiability. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining pushforward priors with adjoint-gradient-based sampling for reliable UQ in nonlinear partial differential equation-constrained inverse problems with sharp interfaces.

</details>


### [203] [A Quantum Spectral Solver for Periodic Incompressible Stokes Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30447)
*Juan M. Gimenez,Fehmi Cirak,Michael Ortiz*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子傅里叶变换的二维不可压缩稳态斯托克斯方程的量子谱求解器，通过Helmholtz投影实现散度自由约束，数值验证了稳态涡、周期力偶极子和类Kolmogorov涨落基准问题，并展示了多尺度有限元架构中的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子计算方法拓展至不可压缩流动问题，实现压力-速度显式分裂和无散投影的量子谱求解，并兼容多尺度有限元中同时更新所有代表体元(RVE)状态的量子并行性。

Method: 使用量子傅里叶变换进行相干基变换，拉普拉斯算子对角化；通过模依赖的笛卡尔速度到纵-横坐标旋转实现二维Helmholtz投影，再结合分量条件化的逆拉普拉斯缩放；速度和压力场编码为量子态，谱因子通过多项式编码的幅度块实现。

Result: 在稳态涡、正则化周期力偶极子和RVE启发的类Kolmogorov涨落基准上进行了数值验证，其中后者展示了无需重建完整速度场即可恢复均匀化动能观测量的能力。在有效态准备和观测估计的标准假设下，电路复杂度对网格分辨率呈多对数依赖。

Conclusion: 该量子谱求解器有效实现了不可压缩斯托克斯流动的显式压力-速度分裂和无散投影，具有多对数级的网格分辨率依赖性，适用于多尺度流场计算中的平均量恢复场景。

Abstract: We present a quantum spectral solver for the steady incompressible Stokes equations on a two-dimensional periodic domain. The method uses the Quantum Fourier Transform as a coherent change of basis and exploits the resulting spectral structure of the Stokes operator: the Laplacian becomes diagonal, while incompressibility is enforced mode by mode through a Helmholtz projection. In two dimensions, this projection is realized by a mode-dependent rotation from Cartesian velocity components to longitudinal--transverse coordinates, followed by component-conditioned inverse-Laplacian scaling. The velocity and pressure fields are encoded as quantum states over Fourier modes and physical components, and the corresponding spectral factors are implemented through polynomially encoded amplitude blocks. The construction extends recent quantum spectral methods in computational mechanics to an incompressible flow operator with explicit pressure--velocity splitting and divergence-free projection. The approach is also compatible with multiscale finite-element architectures in which quantum parallelism can simultaneously update all representative volume element (RVE) states. Numerical verification includes a steady vortex, a regularized periodic force-dipole benchmark, and an RVE-inspired Kolmogorov-like fluctuation benchmark. The latter illustrates how the circuit can recover a homogenized kinetic-energy observable without reconstructing the full velocity field, consistent with the role of averaged quantities in multiscale flow calculations. Under the standard assumptions of efficient state preparation and observable estimation, the circuit has polylogarithmic dependence on the grid resolution, with the polynomial degree and tile count appearing as explicit approximation and implementation parameters.

</details>


### [204] [Iterated Tikhonov regularization of large linear problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30448)
*Davide Furchì,Lothar Reichel*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于部分Golub-Kahan双对角化的迭代Tikhonov正则化方法，利用Golub-Kahan双对角化与Gauss求积之间的联系，在不计算多个近似解的情况下确定正则化参数，从而减少计算量，并满足偏差原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性离散不适定问题中，数据含有误差时需用Tikhonov正则化求解。迭代Tikhonov正则化通常比标准Tikhonov正则化得到更高精度的近似解。当已知数据误差范数的估计时，希望应用迭代Tikhonov正则化得到满足偏差原理的近似解，这需要选择合适的正则化参数。传统方法需计算多个不同参数下的解，计算成本高，因此需要更高效的方法。

Method: 基于部分Golub-Kahan双对角化的迭代Tikhonov正则化方法，利用Golub-Kahan双对角化与Gauss求积之间的理论联系，在不计算多个候选解的情况下直接确定满足偏差原理的正则化参数。

Result: 该方法能有效减少计算所需近似解的计算量，同时满足偏差原理的要求。

Conclusion: 利用Golub-Kahan双对角化与Gauss求积的联系，可以在迭代Tikhonov正则化中高效地确定正则化参数，避免传统方法中计算多个解的繁琐过程，降低了计算开销。

Abstract: Many solution methods for linear discrete ill-posed problems with error-contaminated data (right-hand side) apply Tikhonov regularization to compute a meaningful approximate solution. This solution depends on a regularization parameter. It is well known that iterated Tikhonov regularization often determines an approximate solution of higher quality than (standard) Tikhonov regularization. We consider the situation when an estimate of the norm of the error in the data is known and would like to apply iterative Tikhonov regularization to determine an approximate solution that satisfies the discrepancy principle. This requires a suitable choice of a regularization parameter. The standard approach to determine this parameter is to compute solutions for several values of the regularization parameter and choose a computed approximate solution that satisfies the discrepancy principle. This paper discusses iterated Tikhonov regularization based on partial Golub-Kahan bidiagonalization and describes how the regularization parameter can be determined without computing several approximate solutions by using the connection between Golub-Kahan bidiagonalization and Gauss quadrature. This approach reduces the computational effort required to compute a desired solution.

</details>
