### [205] [The BEG model at the FAD triple point on the square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28821)
*Estevão F. Borel,Aldo Procacci,Rémy Sanchis,Benedetto Scoppola*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明二维Blume-Emery-Griffiths模型在FAD三相点处任意温度下存在唯一Gibbs测度，即无相变发生。证明基于随机簇表示与Bernoulli渗流耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究BEG模型在FAD点这一奇异点（具有无穷多基态）处的相变行为，旨在建立该点无相变的理论保证。

Method: 采用依赖构型的随机簇表示方法，并将其与参数为1/2的Bernoulli位点渗流耦合。

Result: 证明了任意温度下Gibbs测度的唯一性，即不存在相变。

Conclusion: 在FAD三相点处，尽管存在无穷多基态，模型仍无相变，Gibbs测度唯一。

Abstract: In this note, we prove that the two-dimensional Blume-Emery-Griffiths model at the triple point Ferromagnetic-Antiquadrupolar-Disordered (FAD) has a unique Gibbs measure at any temperature, thereby establishing the absence of phase transitions. The FAD point lies at the intersection of lines separating three regions of the phase diagram, and it is a singular point where the model exhibits infinitely many ground states. Our proof is based on a random-cluster type representation with configuration-dependent cluster weights and a coupling with Bernoulli site percolation with parameter $1/2$.

</details>


### [206] [Metric Congruence in Finite-Dimensional Non-Hermitian Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28868)
*Ramirez Romina,Reboiro Marta*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限维非厄米量子力学中的度量表示，强调非厄米系统的物理描述可以在不同但同构的希尔伯特空间中表述。通过Krein空间形式，将具有不定Krein度量的向量空间与标准希尔伯特空间通过同构联系，并指出物理态、度量和算符必须通过相应的希尔伯特空间同构进行变换。通过两能级非厄米自旋模型和Robertson不确定关系验证，表明仅改变度量而固定算符和态会导致表象不匹配，而非不确定性原理的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明非厄米量子力学中不同但同构的希尔伯特空间表示之间的数学关系，解决因度量选择不当导致的物理不一致性。

Method: 采用Krein空间形式，建立不定Krein度量与标准希尔伯特空间之间的显式同构关系；通过两能级非厄米自旋模型进行理论验证；利用Robertson不确定关系作为一致性检验工具。

Result: 物理态、度量和算符必须通过希尔伯特空间同构一同变换才能保持物理内容一致；仅改变度量而固定算符和态会导致表象不匹配，产生所谓的不确定性原理"违反"。

Conclusion: 非厄米系统的等价格表示必须通过希尔伯特空间同构整体变换，否则会导致物理不一致性；Robertson不确定关系的表观违反实为表象失配的反映，而非基本原理的失效。

Abstract: We study metric representations in finite-dimensional non-Hermitian quantum mechanics. The main purpose of this work is to emphasize that the physical description of a non-Hermitian system may be formulated in different, but isomorphic, Hilbert spaces. In particular, within the Krein-space formalism, we show that the vector space endowed with an indefinite Krein metric can be explicitly related to the standard Hilbert space through a suitable isomorphism. This observation is essential for a consistent description of non-Hermitian Hamiltonians. Physical states, metrics, and operators must be transported through the corresponding Hilbert-space isomorphism. In this way, equivalent representations of the same system can be used without changing the physical content of the theory. We illustrate these theoretical aspects by studying a two-level non-Hermitian spin model. We use the Robertson uncertainty relation as a consistency test. Apparent violations can arise when operators and states are kept fixed while the metric is changed, and therefore reflect a mismatch of representations rather than a failure of the uncertainty principle.

</details>


### [207] [How to Untwist Twisted Gauge Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28888)
*Serge Lazzarini,Thierry Masson,Louis Usala*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一种从主丛 $\mathcal{P}$ 上扭曲规范场到关联主丛 $\mathcal{Q}$ 上标准规范场的同构，该同构可延拓至底流形上的局域场，使得扭曲场可被用于标准规范理论（如Yang-Mills理论）。构造通过一个更大的主丛 $\mathcal{S}$ 实现，其以 $\mathcal{P}$ 和 $\mathcal{Q}$ 为商丛，且 $\mathcal{S}$ 的同构类与上循环的等价类一一对应。此外，本文还给出了（完整）dressing场作为主丛动态（或活性）截面的新诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将扭曲规范场转化为标准规范场，从而在标准规范理论框架下处理具有两种对称群的系统，并统一扭曲场与标准场的描述。

Method: 构造一个更大的主丛 $\mathcal{S}$，使得 $\mathcal{P}$ 和 $\mathcal{Q}$ 为其商丛；建立 $\mathcal{P}$ 上扭曲规范场与 $\mathcal{Q}$ 上标准规范场之间的同构映射，该同构可延拓至底流形上的局域场；通过上循环与上边界分类 $\mathcal{S}$ 的同构类。

Result: 成功建立了扭曲规范场与标准规范场之间的同构，该同构可延拓至局域场；$\mathcal{S}$ 的同构类与上循环等价类一一对应；给出了dressing场的新诠释。

Conclusion: 该同构使得扭曲场可被纳入标准规范理论框架，同时处理来自 $\mathcal{P}$ 和 $\mathcal{Q}$ 的两种对称群；dressing场被重新解释为主丛的动态截面。

Abstract: This paper provides an isomorphism between the space of twisted gauge fields on a principal bundle $\mathcal{P}$ and the space of standard gauge fields on a different principal bundle $\mathcal{Q}$ associated to $\mathcal{P}$. This isomorphism extends to local fields on the base manifold, which enables the use of local twisted fields in standard gauge theories (e.g. Yang-Mills-like theories). This allows one to deal with two symmetry groups, coming from $\mathcal{P}$ and $\mathcal{Q}$, respectively. The construction makes use of a larger principal bundle $\mathcal{S}$ which has $\mathcal{P}$ and $\mathcal{Q}$ as quotient bundles. The gauge structure on $\mathcal{S}$ encodes both standard and twisted gauge structures on $\mathcal{P}$. In addition, the isomorphism classes of bundles $\mathcal{S}$ are in 1:1 correspondence with the equivalence classes of cocycles (up to a coboundary). This paper also provides a new interpretation of (full) dressing fields as dynamic (or active) sections of a principal bundle.

</details>


### [208] [The heat-kernel master field on $\mathbb{Z}^d$ at strong coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28945)
*Thibaut Lemoine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了$\mathbb{Z}^d$（$d\geq2$）上强耦合大$N$ Yang-Mills理论，使用热核作用量和$\mathrm{U}(N)$结构群，证明了Wilson圈期望的大$N$极限存在、因子化、具有$1/N$展开，并给出了面积律上界。证明基于根植热核主圈方程和强耦合截断轨迹展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大$N$ Yang-Mills理论在强耦合区域、高维晶格上的极限行为，特别是Wilson圈期望的因子化性质和主场的刻画。

Method: 提出根植热核主圈方程（rooted heat-kernel master loop equation），该方程不在Wilson圈可观测量上闭合，而是在与紧支撑面片装饰耦合的扩展回路可观测量空间上闭合。结合伴侣论文的有限$N$对偶公式和大$N$热核估计，发展强耦合截断根植轨迹展开，识别其主导项为主场。

Result: 证明了归一化Wilson圈期望在无限体积大$N$极限下存在、因子化（前导阶），允许全阶$1/N$展开（系数指数局部化），前导序刻画主场。还证明了热核主场的面积律上界及其更强的系数版本。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套处理高维晶格上强耦合大$N$ Yang-Mills理论的系统方法，克服了传统Wilson作用量方程或二维Makeenko-Migdal方程无法闭合的困难，为理解非微扰大$N$极限行为提供了新途径。

Abstract: We solve large-$N$ Yang--Mills theory on $\mathbb{Z}^d$, for every $d\geq2$, at strong coupling, for structure group $\mathrm{U}(N)$ and for the heat-kernel action. More precisely, we prove that normalized Wilson loop expectations have infinite-volume large-$N$ limits, factorize at leading order, and admit an all-order $1/N$-expansion with exponentially local coefficients, whose leading order characterizes the master field. We also prove an area-law upper bound for the heat-kernel master field, with a stronger coefficientwise version.
  The proof is based on a rooted heat-kernel master loop equation. Unlike the Wilson-action equation or the two-dimensional Makeenko--Migdal equation, this equation does not close on Wilson loop observables alone; it closes on an extended space of loop observables coupled to compactly supported plaquette decorations. We prove a strong-coupling, order-truncated rooted trajectory expansion and then identify its leading term with the master field. The main inputs are the universal finite-$N$ duality formulas developed in the companion paper \cite{Lem26a} and large-$N$ heat-kernel estimates from \cite{LemMai25,LM2}.

</details>


### [209] [Cohomological beta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28975)
*Oleksandr Gamayun,Maxim Gritskov,Andrey Losev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种上同调方法来计算共形反常，以二维共形场论中的流-流形变为例，通过将微扰β函数主导项的Cardy公式重新解释为态空间上Virasoro模结构形变障碍的余圈系数，并预期该方法能高效计算高阶微扰β函数系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为共形反常提供一种新的概念性视角，并发展一种能够高效计算微扰β函数高阶系数的工具。

Method: 采用上同调方法，将共形反常视为态空间上Virasoro模结构形变时的障碍，通过余圈系数来刻画该障碍，从而重新推导出Cardy公式。

Result: 成功复现了二维CFT中流-流形变下微扰β函数主导项的Cardy公式，并将其解释为上同调意义下的余圈系数。

Conclusion: 所提出的上同调框架不仅为共形反常提供了新的概念理解，而且有望成为计算微扰β函数高阶系数的有效工具。

Abstract: We propose a cohomological approach to computing the conformal anomaly. Using the example of current-current deformations of two-dimensional conformal field theories, we reproduce the well-known Cardy formula for the leading contribution to the perturbative beta function as the coefficient of the cocycle that realizes the obstruction to deforming the Virasoro module structure on the state space. In addition to offering a novel conceptual perspective on the conformal anomaly, the proposed approach is anticipated to provide an efficient tool for computing higher-order coefficients of perturbative beta functions.

</details>


### [210] [A Unified Geometric Framework for BPS Flows: Split Attractor, Hessian, and Spectral Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29190)
*Qiang Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的几何框架，将N=2超对称规范理论中BPS中心荷的三种自然结构（分裂吸引子流、Hessian流和谱网络）联系起来，通过严格的证明和丰富的例子展示了其计算能力，并给出了新结果如Argyres-Douglas H1理论的BPS谱闭式解和SU(N)规范理论的圆盘计数生成函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统性地建立BPS中心荷相关的三种几何结构（分裂吸引子流、Hessian流和谱网络）之间的严格关系，弥补此前这些结构之间缺乏统一几何框架的空白。

Method: (i) 利用Kähler结构简洁证明了分裂吸引子流与梯度Hessian流之间的正交性；(ii) 建立精确的升-射对偶，表明谱网络投影到Hitchin基上的特征Hessian流；(iii) 通过在分裂吸引子流树深度上归纳，完整证明了Kontsevich-Soibelman等变性，并由特征Hessian流提供几何排序。

Result: 给出了多个非平凡例子：SU(2)纯规范及N_f=4（含更高味荷的新BPS指标）、SU(3)纯规范（完整BPS谱重构）、SU(4)、Kronecker 3-quiver；推导了Argyres-Douglas H1理论的闭式BPS谱Ω(nα_1+mα_2)=C(n+m,n)；在热带极限下得到了SU(N)规范理论的圆盘计数显式生成函数。

Conclusion: 该统一框架具有强大的计算能力，能够推导新结果并提供可验证的预测，为N=2超对称规范理论的BPS谱分析提供了系统性的几何工具。

Abstract: We provide a systematic and rigorous geometric framework that relates three structures naturally associated to BPS central charges in $\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetric gauge theories: the split attractor flow (SAF) of $|Z|$, the Hessian flow (HF) of $\operatorname{Im}(e^{-i\vartheta}Z)$, and the spectral network (SN) on the base curve of the Hitchin fibration. Our main contributions are: (i) a concise proof of orthogonality between SAF and gradient Hessian flow using only the Kahler structure; (ii) a precise lift-projection duality showing that the spectral network projects to the *characteristic Hessian flow* (the Hamiltonian flow of $\operatorname{Im}(e^{-i\vartheta}Z)$) on the Hitchin base, clarifying a crucial distinction; (iii) a complete proof of the Kontsevich-Soibelman (KS) equivariance by induction on the SAF tree depth, with the geometric ordering provided by the characteristic Hessian flow. We illustrate the framework with detailed and nontrivial examples: $SU(2)$ pure and $N_f=4$ (including new BPS indices for higher flavour charges), $SU(3)$ pure (full BPS spectrum reconstruction), $SU(4)$, the Kronecker $3$-quiver, and we apply the induction to derive a closed-form BPS spectrum for the Argyres-Douglas $H_1$ theory, $Ω(nα_1+mα_2)=\binom{n+m}{n}$, which is a new result. In the tropical limit we obtain an explicit generating function for disk counts in $SU(N)$ gauge theories, $Z_{\mathrm{disk}}^{SU(N)} = \prod_{α\inΦ_+}\prod_{k\ge1}(1-e^{-k\langleα,y\rangle})^{-\binom{k+\mathrm{ht}(α)-1}{\mathrm{ht}(α)-1}}$, which follows directly from our recursion. These results demonstrate the computational power of the unified framework and provide new, verifiable predictions.

</details>


### [211] [Probability density functions as solutions of heterogeneous Cattaneo-Vernotte diffusion equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29234)
*K. Górska,Ž. Tomovski,A. Horzela,M. Magdziarz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用拉普拉斯变换方法，求解了带有指数型扩散系数的非齐次Cattaneo-Vernotte方程，得到了两类以修正贝塞尔函数比表示的确切解析解，并通过完全单调函数理论证明了解为概率密度函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非齐次Cattaneo-Vernotte方程在特定扩散系数下的解析解，并验证其概率性质。

Method: 采用拉普拉斯变换方法求解，将解表示为修正贝塞尔函数的比值形式，并利用完全单调函数理论进行概率密度函数验证。

Result: 获得了两种形式的精确解析解，证明了解是概率密度函数。

Conclusion: 所求解具有明确的概率解释，为非齐次热传导或扩散过程提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we considered a heterogeneous Cattaneo-Vernotte equation with an exponential type of diffusion coefficient under the fundamental initial and boundary conditions stating that the solution vanishes at $+/-$ infinity. Owing to the Laplace transform method we obtain two forms of exact analytical solutions which are presented in terms of the ratio of modified Bessel functions. Using the theory of complete monotone functions, we show that the obtained solutions are probability density functions.

</details>


### [212] [PDE-constrained optimization for virtual sensing in structural dynamics: Full-field displacement and force recovery from sparse sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29349)
*Minjae Kim,Jaehwan Jeong,Jaemin Kim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将虚拟传感问题建模为PDE约束优化（PDE-CO）问题，通过反向模式自动微分和L-BFGS优化同时恢复全场位移和力分布，在三个复杂算例上比模态扩展法误差降低2~17倍，GPU加速达64.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏传感器测量中恢复全场结构响应（虚拟传感）是结构健康监测中的基本挑战，现有方法如模态扩展法存在力需从截断模态反算且未联合优化的局限性。

Method: 将虚拟传感建模为PDE约束优化问题，以弹性动力学方程为等式约束，施加力分布为优化变量，联合恢复全场位移和力；通过反向模式自动微分计算Tikhonov正则化目标的梯度，采用L-BFGS迭代优化；框架耦合离线FEniCSx有限元组装与在线GPU加速JAX计算。

Result: 在悬臂板、90°弯管和核反应堆压力容器三个算例上，PDE-CO方法位移误差比模态扩展法降低2~17倍，均达到亚百分比精度；GPU加速相比CPU可达64.8倍。

Conclusion: PDE-CO方法通过PDE约束同时优化位移和力，克服了模态扩展法需分别反算力的缺陷，尽管计算成本更高，但GPU加速有效弥补了性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Virtual sensing -- recovering full-field structural response from sparse sensor measurements -- is a fundamental challenge in structural health monitoring. This study formulates virtual sensing as a PDE-constrained optimization (PDE-CO) problem, where the governing elastodynamic equation serves as an equality constraint, the applied force distribution is the optimization variable, and the full-field displacement and force are jointly recovered. Gradients of the Tikhonov-regularized objective are computed via reverse-mode automatic differentiation through the forward PDE solver, and L-BFGS iteratively finds the optimal state. The framework couples an offline FEniCSx stage for finite element assembly with an online GPU-accelerated JAX stage, and is verified on three examples of increasing complexity: a cantilever plate, a $90^\circ$ elbow pipe, and a reactor pressure vessel (RPV) representative of a 300~MW pressurized nuclear reactor. PDE-CO consistently outperforms modal expansion across all three cases, reducing displacement errors by factors of $2$ to $17\times$ with sub-percent accuracy on every example. Unlike modal expansion, where force is derived by back-calculation from truncated modal coordinates and is not jointly optimized, PDE-CO recovers displacement and force simultaneously through the PDE constraint; the increased computational cost is offset by GPU acceleration delivering up to $64.8\times$ speedup over CPU.

</details>


### [213] [A Multi-Body Dobrushin-Sokal Criterion -- Part II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29404)
*Jan Philipp Neumann*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了适用于具有复数多体相互作用的晶格气体的Mayer簇展开绝对收敛的充分条件，通过超图划分方案处理强多体相互作用，并可与Gruber-Kunz条件结合以扩展聚合物展开的收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为具有复数多体相互作用的晶格气体建立Mayer簇展开绝对收敛的充分条件，并处理更强的多体相互作用（包括超图独立多项式中的高阶硬核排斥）。

Method: 提出超图划分方案（spanning hypergraphs partition scheme）作为核心工具，结合Gruber-Kunz条件扩展收敛结果。

Result: 该充分条件涵盖了多个经典结果，在超图独立多项式场景下优于传统方法，且与Nguyen和Fernández (2024)的近期结果相媲美。

Conclusion: 本文的方法在复数多体相互作用和强多体排斥场景下具有显著的推广性和实用性，为聚合物展开理论提供了更广泛的收敛保证。

Abstract: We prove a sufficient condition for the absolute convergence of Mayer cluster expansions of log-partition and correlation functions applicable to lattice gases with possibly complex-valued multi-body interactions. Not only are several classical results subsumed but a partition scheme for spanning hypergraphs also makes our methods well-suited for treating stronger multi-body interactions, including higher-order hard-core repulsion in the context of hypergraph independence polynomials. Furthermore, our approach is easily combined with the Gruber--Kunz condition to produce extended convergence results for the polymer expansion of lattice gases, rivalling those obtained not too long ago by Nguyen and Fernández (2024).

</details>


### [214] [Existence and absence of Bose-Einstein condensation in the interacting random Kac-Luttinger model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30277)
*C. Boccato,J. Kerner,M. Pechmann,W. Spitzer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Kac-Luttinger随机连续模型中零温相互作用玻色子的凝聚类型转变，证明弱相互作用下存在类型-I BEC（单粒子基态凝聚），强相互作用下不存在局域态凝聚，并证明存在类型-III BEC（广义凝聚，无单个态宏观占据），首次在更高维随机连续模型中严格证明凝聚类型的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索随机连续模型中相互作用玻色子的凝聚类型转变，特别是从类型-I BEC到类型-III BEC的转换，填补该方向在更高维随机连续模型中严格结果的空白。

Method: 在Kac-Luttinger引入的随机连续模型框架下，分别分析弱相互作用和强相互作用情形，利用单粒子哈密顿量的本征态性质，证明不同类型凝聚的存在性和不存在性。

Result: 弱相互作用时存在类型-I BEC（最低本征态凝聚）；强相互作用时局域态凝聚不可能发生；存在类型-III BEC（广义凝聚，一族本征态被宏观占据但无一单独宏观占据）。

Conclusion: 首次在更高维随机连续模型中严格证明了凝聚类型从类型-I到类型-III的转变。

Abstract: In this paper, we study interacting bosons at zero temperature in a random and higher-dimensional continuum model introduced by Kac and Luttinger. For weak interactions we prove that there is condensation in the lowest eigenstate of the one-particle Hamiltonian (type-I BEC). For strong interactions, however, we show that condensation in a localized state cannot occur. We also prove generalized condensation, where a family of eigenstates of the one-particle Hamiltonian is macroscopically occupied as a whole. Combining these results yields a scenario where there is generalized condensation into a family of eigenstates of the one-particle Hamiltonian, but none of them is macroscopically occupied itself (type-III BEC). This proves a transition in the type of condensation. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first rigorous result in this direction for a random continuum model in higher dimensions.

</details>


### [215] [Generalized Hermite Polynomials and Spectral Degeneracies of a Singular Sextic Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30357)
*Davide Guzzetti,Dmitrii Rachenkov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究准可解奇异六次振子及其代数谱，证明在特定参数范围内判别式可分解为三个因子，其中一个因子是广义Hermite多项式$H_{mn}$的平方，其零点对应第四Painlevé方程有理解的极点。谱简并（能级交叉点）与广义Hermite多项式的零点一一对应，提供了Painlevé IV版本的Shapiro-Tater渐近对应。此外，还刻画了六次振子同时存在两个指数行为相反的拟多项式本征函数的参数条件，并给出了$H_{mn}$的新行列式表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立六次振子代数谱的判别式分解理论，揭示其谱简并与Painlevé IV方程广义Hermite多项式零点之间的精确对应关系，推广Shapiro-Tater渐近对应从Painlevé II到Painlevé IV情形。

Method: 通过分析准可解奇异六次振子的矩阵表示，研究其特征多项式的判别式分解；利用代数谱与Painlevé IV方程有理解的联系，将谱简并与广义Hermite多项式零点对应；通过分析两个互补块的特征多项式的结式，得到广义Hermite多项式的新表示。

Result: 证明了判别式自然分解为三个因子，其中一个是广义Hermite多项式$H_{mn}$的平方；谱简并与$H_{mn}$零点一一对应；刻画了同时存在两个指数行为相反的拟多项式本征函数的参数条件；给出了$H_{mn}$的新行列式表示（两个互补块特征多项式的结式）。

Conclusion: 建立了六次振子与Painlevé IV方程之间的精确代数联系，为Shapiro-Tater对应提供了Painlevé IV版本，同时得到了广义Hermite多项式的新表示。

Abstract: We study a quasi-exactly solvable singular sextic oscillator and its algebraic spectrum. For a distinguished range of parameters, we prove that the discriminant of the characteristic polynomial of the matrix determining the algebraic spectrum admits a natural factorization into three factors. One of these factors is the square of a generalized Hermite polynomial $H_{mn}$, whose zeros are poles of a rational solution of the fourth Painlevé equation. Hence, the spectral degeneracies (level crossing points) corresponding to a component of the discriminant locus are in exact correspondence with the zeros of generalized Hermite polynomials, providing an exact Painlevé IV analogue of the Shapiro--Tater asymptotic correspondence originally conjectured for the quartic oscillator and Painlevé II. We also characterize the values of the parameters for which the sextic oscillator admits simultaneously two quasi-polynomial eigenfunctions with opposite exponential behaviour at infinity, and show that this phenomenon is also governed by generalized Hermite polynomials. Our result also yields a new determinantal representation of $H_{mn}$ as the resultant of the characteristic polynomials of two complementary blocks of the matrix determining the algebraic spectrum.

</details>


### [216] [A Kac system interacting with two heat reservoirs: the shearing case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30529)
*Federico Bonetto,Matthew Powell*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究了一个由M个粒子与两个热库（每个热库含N个粒子，N>>M）组成的系统，通过随机碰撞演化。证明在短于√N/M的时间尺度内，两个热库可近似为两个具有剪切作用的动态麦克斯韦恒温器，并在温度相等、动量相同条件下获得时间均匀逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限粒子数热库对系统动力学的影响，并建立从有限N热库到理想化无穷大热库（恒温器）的严格逼近理论。

Method: 采用Kac型主方程描述系统与两个非中心麦克斯韦分布热库（温度T±、平均动量p±）的随机碰撞演化，通过分析有限N与N→∞极限之间的误差界，推导时间尺度依赖的逼近结果。

Result: 对于时间t < √N/M，有限N热库的动力学可被两个剪切动态麦克斯韦恒温器有效近似；当T+=T-且p+=p-时，逼近在时间上均匀成立。

Conclusion: 建立了有限粒子数热库与无穷大热库（恒温器）之间在特定时间尺度下的等价性，为理解有限尺寸效应对非平衡统计力学的影响提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: We study a system formed by $M$ particles moving in 3 dimensions and interacting with two heat reservoirs, each with $N\gg M$ particles. The system and the reservoirs interact via random collisions and thus evolve via a Kac-type master equation. The initial state of the reservoirs is given by two non-centered Maxwellian distributions; they have temperature $T_+$ and $T_-$ and have average velocity $\vec p_+$ and $\vec p_-$, respectively. We prove that, for times shorter than $\sqrt{N}/M$, the interaction with the two reservoirs is well-approximated by the interaction with two shearing {\it dynamic} Maxwellian thermostats (i.e. heat reservoirs with $N=\infty$). As a byproduct of our analysis, we obtain a uniform in time approximation when $T_+=T_-$ and $\vec p_+=\vec p_-$.

</details>

### [217] [Stochastic Variability of Binary Accretion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28499)
*Akhil Nair,Jonathan Zrake*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过超高分辨率二维流体动力学模拟，测量了不等质量（q=0.2）双星系统周围环双星气体盘中吸积速率时间序列的功率谱密度（PSD）。研究发现连续PSD呈现为断裂幂律形式，从平坦（白噪声）过渡到斜率为-4，断裂频率约为双星轨道频率的5倍。这种形式源于气体输运的阻尼随机游走和微盘的低通滤波效应。研究还指出，过大"汇"区会导致伪影。这种PSD特征与普通活动星系核（AGN）的随机变率相似，暗示典型AGN变率可能源于广泛的双星系统，但脉冲星计时实验可能排除这一可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于连续PSD的形状识别超大质量黑洞双星在活动星系核中的新标志，以补充PSD中已被充分研究的周期线特征。

Method: 使用超高分辨率二维流体动力学模拟，测量不等质量（q=0.2）双星系统周围环双星气体盘中吸积速率时间序列的功率谱密度。

Result: 连续PSD呈现断裂幂律，从白噪声过渡到斜率-4，断裂频率约为双星轨道频率的5倍。发现该形式源于阻尼随机游走和微盘低通滤波的双重机制。

Conclusion: AGN连续PSD特征与双星吸积模型一致，暗示典型AGN变率可能源于双星系统，但脉冲星计时实验结果可能排除这种可能性。

Abstract: We measure the power spectral density (PSD) of the accretion rate time series in an unequal mass (q = 0.2) binary surrounded by a circumbinary gas disk, using very high-resolution 2D hydrodynamics simulations. Our aim is to identify new signposts of supermassive black hole (SMBH) binaries in active galactic nuclei (AGN), based on the shape of the continuum PSD, to complement well-studied line features in the PSD (periodicities). We find that the continuum PSD is a broken power-law, transitioning from flat (white noise) to a slope of -4 at a break frequency generically ~5 times the binary orbital frequency. This form is expected when (a) delivery of gas from the circumbinary disk to the individual "minidisks" is a damped random walk with correlation time equal to binary orbital period and (b) the minidisks function as low-pass filters acting at the Kepler frequency of the outer edge of the smaller black hole's minidisk; we show numerical evidence for both. The broken power-law PSD is attained in a limit where the secondary black hole is much smaller than its minidisk, realized numerically by a sufficiently small "sink" region; larger sinks lead to excess high-frequency noise seen as accretion rate spikes, and we argue these should be regarded as artificial when the black holes themselves are smaller than the sink regions. The broken power-law PSD is reminiscent of stochastic variability in ordinary AGN, inviting the conjecture that canonical AGN variability could result from widespread binarity, however pulsar timing experiments may exclude this possibility.

</details>


### [218] [Twin Peaks: Resolving Features in the Binary Black Hole Mass Function with COSMIC-METISSE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28506)
*Duncan B. Maclean,Poojan Agrawal,Katelyn Breivik,and Alexandra G. Guerrero,Michael Zevin,Mathieu Renzo,Carl L. Rodriguez*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用MESA恒星演化代码生成新网格，结合METISSE和COSMIC双星群体合成代码，研究恒星物理参数（风驱动质量损失和对流边界混合）对并合双黑洞（BBH）群体性质的影响。发现在~10M☉处主质量谱存在峰值，由~8M☉和~13M☉两个子群构成，其中高质量子群与质量比反转（MRR）相关。改变恒星物理参数可导致两个子群合并为~9M☉单峰，并造成~6倍的并合率差异，主要源于对公共包层形成通道的调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波观测为研究大质量恒星演化提供了新视角，但群体合成模型的预测能力受限于恒星模型及其相关不确定性难以充分变化的问题。本文旨在通过系统变化恒星物理参数，评估这些不确定性对并合双黑洞群体性质的影响。

Method: 使用MESA开源恒星演化代码计算覆盖10⁻³≤Z/Z☉≤7金属丰度的新恒星演化网格，变化风驱动质量损失和对流边界混合（CBM）两个恒星物理参数；结合METISSE插值方法和COSMIC双星群体合成代码，获得本地宇宙中并合双黑洞的合成群体。

Result: 主质量谱在~10M☉处存在峰值，大多数模型变化中由~8M☉和~13M☉两个子群构成，高质量子群主导于经历质量比反转（MRR）的BBH；MRR群体暗示主质量与质量比之间的反相关，m₁≳10M☉的BBH偏好q≈0.7。改变风和CBM处理可使两个子群合并为~9M☉单峰；恒星演化模型变化（尤其CBM）导致~6倍的并合率差异，主要源于对公共包层形成通道的调制。

Conclusion: 双黑洞群体性质对恒星物理参数（尤其是对流边界混合和风驱动质量损失）敏感，这些不确定性显著影响并合率预测和主质量谱结构；未来需更精确的恒星物理模型以提高引力波源群体预测的可靠性。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from inspiraling binary black holes (BBHs) provide insights into the lives and deaths of massive stars. Population synthesis allows us to model these binaries through isolated binary evolution, but its predictive power is limited by difficulties in varying the stellar models and their associated uncertainties. We present a new grid of stellar tracks computed with the open-source stellar evolution code MESA, spanning metallicities $10^{-3} \le Z/Z_{\odot} \le 7$. We vary two stellar physics parameters: wind-driven mass loss and the convective boundary mixing (CBM) mechanism. We pair these models with the Method of Interpolation for Single Stellar Evolution (METISSE) and binary population synthesis code COSMIC to obtain synthetic populations of merging BBHs in the local Universe. We find a maximum in the primary mass spectrum near $10M_\odot$ which in most model variations is composed of two sub-populations at $\approx8M_{\odot}$ and $\approx13 M_\odot$, with the higher-mass population dominated by BBHs whose progenitors underwent a mass ratio reversal (MRR). This population also suggests an anticorrelation between higher primary masses and mass ratio, as BBHs with $m_1\gtrapprox10M_\odot$ preferentially undergo MRR and prefer a final mass ratio of $q\approx0.7$. However, the location and relative strength of these two sub-populations is sensitive to our assumed stellar physics: varying both the wind and CBM treatments can merge the MRR and non-MRR populations into a single peak near $9M_\odot$. Variations in our stellar tracks, especially CBM, lead to a factor of $\approx6$ difference in the rate, primarily due to modulation of the common envelope formation channel.

</details>


### [219] [Dual AGN and Multiple SMBH Systems in the Era of SKAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28512)
*Q. D'Amato,L. Cui,R. Deane,S. Komossa,C. Pillay,A. Tripathi,P. Kharb,H. Guo,S. Nandi,K. Rubinur,S. Anton,T. An,S. Bonoli,N. Chang,R. Dave,A. De Rosa,M. Habouzit,F. Mannucci,I. Prandoni,P. Severgnini,M. Scialpi,C. Spingola,C. Vignali,W. Xu,X. Yan,Y. Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用射电干涉测量（特别是VLBI和SKAO）探测和表征双活动星系核及超大质量黑洞双星的最新策略，强调了射电观测在识别多吸积SMBH、区分AGN与恒星形成发射、以及连接电磁与引力波观测方面的独特优势，并展望了SKAO将如何实现对双SMBH系统的首次全面射电普查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双活动星系核和超大质量黑洞双星是研究星系合并、黑洞增长及引力波发射的关键天体，但现有观测受限于灵敏度和分辨率；本文旨在综述当前探测策略并展望SKAO如何突破这些限制。

Method: 采用综述性方法，梳理了基于射电干涉测量（VLBI、SKAO）的DAGN/SMBHB探测策略，包括高分辨率成像、光学/红外选源的射电后随、盲选射电样本、致密束缚SMBHB研究等，并讨论了与多波段设施及引力波探测器（LISA、脉冲星计时阵列）的协同观测。

Result: 指出了当前观测在灵敏度-分辨率联合覆盖及天区面积上的局限性；阐明了SKAO凭借其灵敏度、巡天速度、成像保真度和角分辨率的空前组合，将从近邻宇宙到再电离纪元实现DAGN和SMBHB的发现与表征；提出了多项具体科学案例。

Conclusion: SKAO将实现首个全面的双SMBH射电普查，填补电磁与引力波观测之间的鸿沟，为理解SMBH配对、吸积及合并驱动的反馈提供统计显著的宇宙学视角。

Abstract: We present a radio-oriented review of current strategies for the detection and characterization of dual active galactic nuclei (DAGN) and supermassive black hole binaries (SMBHBs), emphasizing the crucial role of radio interferometry in advancing this field. We discuss how high-resolution radio imaging - particularly through very long baseline interferometry (VLBI) - provides a unique, dust-unbiased tool to identify multiple accreting SMBHs, disentangle AGN-related emission from star formation, and trace components from tens of kpc to sub-parsec scales. We summarize current observational limitations, such as insufficient sensitivity-resolution combination and area coverage. We then outline how the SKAO will overcome these constraints through its unprecedented combination of sensitivity, survey speed, imaging fidelity and angular resolution, enabling the discovery and characterization of dual and binary SMBHs from the nearby Universe to the epoch of reionization. Several science cases are presented, including radio follow-ups of optical/infrared-selected DAGN, direct blind radio selection of DAGN, studies of compact bound SMBHBs, and the link between SMBHB orbital evolution and low-frequency gravitational wave emission. We further emphasize the synergy between SKAO observations and modern and upcoming facilities such as the James Webb and Euclid space telescopes, Rubin Observatory, and gravitational wave detectors including the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna and pulsar timing arrays. These combined capabilities will allow SKAO to enable the first comprehensive radio census of dual and binary SMBH systems, bridge the gap between electromagnetic and gravitational wave observations, and provide a statistically significant view of SMBH pairing, accretion, and merger-driven feedback throughout cosmic history.

</details>


### [220] [Search for Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background in the Full KamLAND Dataset with Neural-Network-Based Event Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29381)
*D. Chernyak,T. Eda,M. Eizuka,R. Endo,A. Gando,Y. Gando,T. Hachiya,F. Haneishi,K. Hata,T. Hirai,K. Hosokawa,K. Ichimura,H. Ikeda,K. Inoue,K. Ishidoshiro,Y. Kamei,N. Kawada,Y. Kishimoto,M. Koga,K. Mikami,H. Miyake,K. Mizukoshi,D. Morita,K. Nakamura,R. Nakamura,J. Nakane,Y. Nakano,N. Obata,Y. Ota,K. Saito,I. Sakaki,I. Shimizu,J. Shirai,A. Suzuki,K. Tachibana,A. Takeuchi,K. Tamae,H. Watanabe,Z. Xu,S. Yoshida,S. Umehara,K. Fushimi,K. Kotera,Y. Urano,S. Kurosawa,H. Ozaki,B. E. Berger,B. K. Fujikawa,J. G. Learned,J. Maricic,Z. Li,L. A. Winslow,Y. Efremenko,H. J. Karwowski,D. M. Markoff,W. Tornow,S. Dell'Oro,T. O'Donnell,J. A. Detwiler,S. Enomoto,M. P. Decowski,K. M. Weerman,C. Grant,O. Penek,H. Song,A. Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: KamLAND利用深度神经网络事件分类技术，在8.3-30.8 MeV能量范围内搜索弥散超新星中微子背景（DSNB），观测到7个候选事件（背景期望16.2±9.4），未发现显著超出，设定90%置信水平上限（通量38-43 cm⁻²s⁻¹），并展示了神经网络在液闪探测器抑制中子相关本底方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测弥散超新星中微子背景（DSNB），这是来自宇宙历史上所有超新星累积发射的电子反中微子，其探测对理解超新星物理和中微子特性具有重要意义。

Method: 利用KamLAND液闪探测器，通过逆β衰变（IBD）过程探测电子反中微子，能量范围8.3-30.8 MeV，采用基于深度神经网络的新型事件分类技术来抑制本底，对能量和径向分布进行谱分析。

Result: 在9.02 kton-yr（8.3-9.3 MeV）和9.42 kton-yr（9.3-30.8 MeV）曝光量下观测到7个候选事件，背景期望16.2±9.4（含神经网络选择系统不确定度）。未发现显著DSNB信号，设定90% CL通量上限38-43 cm⁻²s⁻¹（依赖模型），在13.3 MeV以下给出模型无关的电子反中微子通量上限。

Conclusion: 未探测到DSNB信号，获得了该能量段最严格的通量限制之一；同时证明深度神经网络事件分类是抑制液闪探测器中子相关本底的有效方法。

Abstract: We report a search for the diffuse supernova neutrino background (DSNB) with the KamLAND detector, targeting electron antineutrinos via inverse beta decay in the neutrino energy range of 8.3 to 30.8 MeV. Using liquid-scintillator exposures of 9.02 kton-year for 8.3 to 9.3 MeV and 9.42 kton-year for 9.3 to 30.8 MeV, we observe seven candidate events after applying a new deep-neural-network-based event classification technique. This result is consistent with the background-only expectation of 16.2 plus or minus 9.4 events, including systematic uncertainties associated with the neural-network selection. A spectral analysis of the energy and radial distributions finds no significant excess attributable to the DSNB. We therefore set 90 percent confidence-level upper limits on the DSNB flux of 38 to 43 per square centimeter per second, depending on the assumed DSNB model. We also derive model-independent 90 percent confidence-level upper limits on the electron-antineutrino flux, obtaining some of the most stringent constraints below 13.3 MeV. Beyond the DSNB search itself, this work demonstrates neural-network-based event classification as a promising approach for suppressing neutron-associated backgrounds in liquid-scintillator neutrino detectors.

</details>


### [221] [Lower Your Rates: On Claims of a Binary Black Hole Merger-Rate Crisis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28515)
*Floor S. Broekgaarden*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对57项孤立双星演化研究中的1490个双黑洞并合率子模型进行综合分析，发现约80%的子模型预测高于LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA观测值，但仍有相当一部分子模型与观测一致。通过2543对子模型对比，识别出影响并合率的关键物理假设，并指出低并合率并非唯一与强natal kicks或低金属丰度星形成关联。研究揭示了"模拟孤岛"现象，表明声称的BBH并合率危机具有强烈模型依赖性，强调在得出孤立双星演化与引力波观测存在矛盾的结论前，需充分探索跨多个种群合成框架的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，孤立双星演化模拟普遍高估了观测到的本地双黑洞并合率，这被解读为需要对双星恒星演化模型进行重大修订。本文旨在重新审视这些论断，评估模型依赖性，并澄清是否存在真正的"BBH并合率危机"。

Method: 汇编57项孤立双星演化研究中的1490个BBH并合率子模型，与LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA GWTC-5观测约束进行比较。通过构造2543对子模型变体（每对仅改变单一物理假设），系统量化各物理假设（如natal kicks、共有包层演化、质量转移、角动量损失、遗迹形成、恒星风、初始条件、星形成历史）对并合率的独立影响。分析"模拟孤岛"现象，即同一代码框架内的一致性可能不具普适性。

Result: 约80%的子模型预测率高于GWTC-5区间，但相当一部分子模型可复现或低估观测率。文献结果跨度达数个数量级，对多种物理假设高度敏感。低并合率并非唯一与强natal kicks或低金属丰度星形成相关，多种物理动机明确的假设均可独立将模拟率降至与观测一致。模拟率呈现"模拟孤岛"聚类特征，表明代码内共识不具跨框架普适性。

Conclusion: 声称的BBH并合率危机具有强烈模型依赖性。在得出孤立双星演化与引力波观测存在根本性矛盾的结论前，必须跨多个种群合成框架充分探索完整参数空间。本研究强调系统不确定性量化的重要性，并警示基于单一模拟框架的论断可能具有误导性。

Abstract: Recent studies have argued that isolated binary evolution simulations generically overestimate the observed local binary black hole (BBH) merger rate, even after adopting observationally motivated variations in the metallicity-dependent cosmic star-formation history, and have interpreted this as motivation for drastic revisions to binary stellar evolution models. We revisit these claims using a compilation of 1490 simulated BBH merger rates from 57 isolated binary-evolution studies, compared to constraints from the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA Collaboration through GWTC-5. While $\sim$80% of compiled submodels find rates above the GWTC-5 interval, a substantial subset reproduces or underestimates the observed rate. The literature spans several orders of magnitude, reflecting strong sensitivity to assumptions about natal kicks, common-envelope evolution, mass transfer, angular-momentum loss, remnant formation, stellar winds, initial conditions, and star-formation history. Using 2543 pairwise BBH submodel variations constructed to isolate single physical assumptions, we identify which choices most strongly impact the simulated BBH merger rate. Low BBH merger rates are not uniquely associated with strong natal kicks or reduced low-metallicity star formation. Multiple physically motivated assumptions can independently reduce simulated rates to values consistent with observations. We further show that simulated rates cluster into `simulation silos': frameworks producing apparent consensus within a code that does not generalize beyond it. Our results indicate that claims of a universal BBH merger-rate crisis are strongly model dependent, and underscore the importance of exploring the full parameter space across multiple population-synthesis frameworks before concluding that isolated binary evolution is in tension with gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [222] [JWST observations of SN 2024abup: First Detection of CO in a broad-lined Type Ic Supernova and Constraints on r-process Nucleosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28561)
*Manisha Shrestha,Lindsey A. Kwok,David J. Sand,Stan Bartmentloo,Collin Christy,Anders Jerkstrand,K. Azalee Bostroem,Jennifer E. Andrews,Kate D. Alexander,Yize Dong,Carl E.
