Ransome,Aravind P. Ravi,Mathieu Renzo,Nicolás Meza Retamal,Bhagya Subrayan,Nathan Smith,Stefano Valenti,Sergiy Vasylyev,Giacomo Ricigliano,Peter J. Brown,Moira Andrews,Joseph Farah,D. Andrew Howell,Curtis McCully,Megan Newsome,Kathryn Wynn,Ryan Chornock,Natalie LeBaro,Raffaella Margutti,Melissa Shahbandeh,Chris Ashall,Peter Hoeflich*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了SN 2024abup（一颗邻近的宽谱线Ic型超新星）的JWST近红外到中红外观测结果，在爆炸后+54天首次捕捉到SN Ic-bl的MIR数据，识别出C、O、Mg和CO分子信号（迄今最早在核坍缩超新星中检测到分子），发现>1.5微米的连续谱可能来自尘埃（既有、新生或两者结合），但未找到r-process元素的明确红外证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNe Ic-bl被认为是r-process核合成和早期宇宙尘埃产生的可能场所，但此前缺乏JWST中红外波段的直接观测数据来验证这些理论。

Method: 利用JWST的NIR+MIR观测（1-14微米），结合射电和光学数据，使用光谱合成代码SUMO识别谱线特征，分析超新星抛射物的元素组成、分子形成和尘埃贡献。

Result: 识别出C、O、Mg和CO分子特征；CO是迄今在核坍缩超新星中最早检测到的分子；>1.5微米的连续谱可用尘埃（既有或新生）解释；未发现r-process元素的明确红外信号。

Conclusion: SNe Ic-bl可能是早期宇宙尘埃生产的贡献者；如果r-process元素确实产生，从光谱中揭示它们的存在需要极高数据质量和模型来解混重叠特征。

Abstract: SN 2024abup is a nearby broad-lined Type Ic supernova (SN Ic-bl) in NGC 0681 at a distance of 23.3 \pm 1.6 Mpc. As energetic explosions of massive stars, SNe Ic-bl are considered a plausible site for rapid-neutron capture nucleosynthesis (r-process) and chemical enrichment from short-lived progenitors. They may also contribute to dust production in the early Universe. We present JWST near- to mid-infrared (NIR+MIR) observations (1-14 micron) of SN Ic-bl 2024abup at +41 days after the V band maximum (+54 days after explosion), the first-ever JWST+MIR observation of a SN Ic-bl along with radio and optical data. Using the spectral synthesis code SUMO, we identify the observed broad IR line features in SN 2024abup and find significant contributions from C, O, Mg, and carbon monoxide (CO) -- the earliest detection of molecules in a core-collapse SN so far. The spectrum shows continuum emission at wavelengths greater than 1.5 micron, which could be explained by dust -- preexisting, newly formed, or a combination-heated by the SN. We do not find compelling evidence for infrared signatures of r-process elements, though our search is hampered by the presence of many broad and blended features from the non-r-process elements. These new observations indicate that SNe Ic-bl could be a contributor to early-universe dust production, and suggest that if r-process elements are produced, revealing their presence from spectra requires very high-quality data and models to disentangle blends.

</details>


### [223] [The NANOGrav 15 yr Data Set: Customized Chromatic Noise Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28571)
*Bjorn Larsen,Jeremy G. Baier,Daniel J. Oliver,Kalista Wayt,Yu-Ting Chang,Jeffrey S. Hazboun,Chiara M. F. Mingarelli,Joseph Simon,Matthew T. Miles,Gabriella Agazie,Akash Anumarlapudi,Anne M. Archibald,Zaven Arzoumanian,Paul T. Baker,Paul R. Brook,H. Thankful Cromartie,Kathryn Crowter,Megan E. DeCesar,Paul B. Demorest,Timothy Dolch,Elizabeth C. Ferrara,William Fiore,Emmanuel Fonseca,Gabriel E. Freedman,Nate Garver-Daniels,Peter A. Gentile,Joseph Glaser,Deborah C. Good,Ross J. Jennings,Megan L. Jones,David L. Kaplan,Matthew Kerr,Michael T. Lam,Duncan R. Lorimer,Jing Luo,Ryan S. Lynch,Alexander McEwen,Maura A. McLaughlin,Natasha McMann,Bradley W. Meyers,Cherry Ng,David J. Nice,Timothy T. Pennucci,Benetge B. P. Perera,Nihan S. Pol,Henri A. Radovan,Scott M. Ransom,Paul S. Ray,Ann Schmiedekamp,Carl Schmiedekamp,Brent J. Shapiro-Albert,Ingrid H. Stairs,Kevin Stovall,Abhimanyu Susobhanan,Joseph K. Swiggum,Haley M. Wahl*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文为NANOGrav 15年数据集中的67颗脉冲星定制了色散噪声模型，使用高斯过程同时表征多种色散延迟，并发现了21颗脉冲星的非色散性色度延迟，以及19颗脉冲星的无色噪声推断受到显著影响，部分无色噪声可被色散过程部分或完全解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列进行低频引力波探测时，星际传播效应（色散、散射等）引入的色度延迟会降低引力波灵敏度并导致推断偏差，且这些延迟强烈依赖于每颗脉冲星的视线方向特性，因此需要针对每颗脉冲星定制色散噪声模型。

Method: 从扩展的高斯过程套件中为67颗NANOGrav 15年数据集脉冲星选择定制化色散噪声模型，同时表征多种类型的色度延迟；利用这些模型推断太阳风电子密度在约1.5个太阳周期内的变化；并识别非色散性色度延迟。

Result: 在21颗脉冲星中发现非色散性色度延迟证据；应用色散模型后，19颗脉冲星的无色噪声推断受到显著影响，其中多颗脉冲星此前显著的无色噪声过程可被部分或完全解释为色散过程；同时利用模型推断了太阳风电子密度变化。

Conclusion: 精细化的色散噪声建模对于提升脉冲星计时阵列低频引力波搜索的灵敏度和准确性至关重要。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays conduct low-frequency gravitational wave searches, which require comprehensive accounting of various noise sources to achieve robust results. Interstellar propagation effects (e.g., dispersion and scattering) are especially complex noise sources, introducing chromatic delays that can reduce sensitivity to gravitational waves and bias their inference if left unmodeled. These delays also strongly depend on the line of sight properties to each individual pulsar. To address this, we present customized chromatic noise models for 67 pulsars in the NANOGrav 15 yr dataset. These models are selected from an expanded suite of Gaussian processes to simultaneously characterize multiple types of chromatic delays and are tailored to each pulsar's dataset. Alongside probing the interstellar medium, we use these models to infer the solar wind electron density over the course of $\sim 1.5$ solar cycles. We also find evidence for non-dispersive chromatic delays in 21 out of 67 NANOGrav pulsars. After applying our chromatic models, we observe significant impacts on the inference of achromatic noise in 19 out of 67 pulsars, finding in several cases that a previously significant achromatic noise process can be partially or entirely described as chromatic. These results demonstrate that refined noise modeling is essential to enhance the sensitivity and accuracy of low-frequency gravitational wave searches with pulsar timing arrays.

</details>


### [224] [Identifying Observational Signatures of Flux Eruption Events in Supermassive Black Hole Accretion Flows with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28603)
*Angelo Ricarte,Erandi Chavez,Franc O,Pavlos Protopapas*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习（CNN、随机森林、逻辑回归）识别强磁场黑洞吸积流中"磁通量爆发事件"（FEEs）在事件视界望远镜（EHT）可观测图像中的特征，发现FEEs期间毫米波图像呈现更弥散、更高线偏振、更低总流量，且Q-U环旋转速率降低，但多数特征较弱，随机森林准确率约80%，表明CNN学到了传统统计量未捕捉的FEE结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁场黑洞吸积流模拟中常出现磁通量爆发事件（FEEs），这些现象可能被事件视界望远镜（EHT）观测到。本文旨在利用机器学习方法，识别FEEs在EHT可观测图像中的特征信号，为未来观测提供诊断工具。

Method: 首先训练卷积神经网络（CNN）从无噪声模拟图像中学习FEE的任务相关表征；利用该网络标记更大规模图像集后，训练可解释模型（随机森林和逻辑回归）确定观测特征。分析FEE期间图像弥散度、线偏振、总流量、Q-U环旋转速率等统计量。

Result: FEE期间毫米波图像趋向更弥散、更高线偏振、更低总流量，但多数FEE的这些特征相对于通常的时间变异性较弱；Q-U环旋转速率在FEE期间降低，这与将FEE同时导致毫米波Q-U环和耀斑的图景相矛盾；随机森林在观测摘要统计量上达到约80%的类加权准确率，表明CNN学到了传统统计量未完全映射的FEE结构。

Conclusion: 图像尺寸和偏振分数可用于标记候选FEE，但高分辨率、高动态范围的图像对于确认FEE并检验吸积流中这一现象仍然至关重要。

Abstract: Simulated black hole accretion flows with strong magnetic fields often exhibit "flux eruption events" (FEEs), transient and localized expulsions of matter near the event horizon due to magnetic reconnection. It may now be possible to image them with the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT), a global network of millimeter-wave observatories that images black holes. Here we use machine learning as an interpretable inference tool to identify observational signatures of FEEs that could be accessible to the EHT. First, we train a convolutional neural network to learn task-relevant representations of FEEs in uncorrupted simulated images. After using this network to label a larger set of images, we then train interpretable models (random forest and logistic regression) to determine observational signatures. We find that during a FEE, images in the millimeter tend toward more diffuse emission, higher linear polarization, and lower total fluxes, but these signatures are weak for most FEEs compared to the usual time variability of these features. Moreover, the Q-U loop rotation rate decreases during FEEs, contrary to a picture in which FEEs could jointly cause both millimeter Q-U loops and flares. Our random forest trained on observable summary statistics achieves ~80% class-weighted accuracy, suggesting that the CNN learns FEE structure not fully mapped onto these traditional summary statistics. Our results imply that image size and polarization fraction can be used to flag candidate FEEs, but high-resolution, high-dynamic range images will still be important to confirm FEEs and test accretion flows for this phenomenon.

</details>


### [225] [Tracing the Orbital Motion of the Accreting White Dwarf in EX~Hydrae with XRISM/Resolve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28915)
*Yuken Ohshiro,Yukikatsu Terada,Taichi Ichikawa,Yugo Motogami,Manabu Ishida,Koji Mukai,Masayoshi Nobukawa,Takayuki Hayashi,Mariko Kimura,Mai Takeo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用XRISM/Resolve的高分辨X射线光谱，首次在吸积白矮星EX Hydrae中探测到Fe XXV Kα线的轨道调制，测得K1=58.1±8.5 km/s，结合文献轨道参数导出白矮星质量M1=0.79±0.04 M⊙，验证了X射线谱线可作为吸积白矮星轨道运动示踪物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量吸积白矮星质量对理解其演化和吸积物理至关重要，高分辨X射线光谱可通过多普勒位移追踪白矮星运动，但此前尚未在单个Fe K壳层谱线中探测到轨道调制信号。

Method: 利用XRISM/Resolve对中介偏振星EX Hydrae进行83 ks观测，通过频繁在轨增益校准获取高分辨X射线光谱，分析Fe XXV Kα各分量谱线质心的轨道相位调制，拟合得到速度半振幅K1，并结合文献轨道参数推导白矮星质量。

Result: 首次在吸积白矮星系统中探测到单个Fe K壳层谱线的轨道调制，Fe XXV Kα分量呈现一致性轨道调制，K1=58.1±8.5 km/s，与光学/UV测量结果一致，导出白矮星质量M1=0.79±0.04 M⊙。

Conclusion: 高分辨X射线光谱可利用单个Fe K壳层谱线质心示踪吸积白矮星的轨道运动，XRISM/Resolve的高谱分辨率和在轨增益校准能力是实现这一突破的关键。

Abstract: Measuring the masses of accreting white dwarfs (WDs) is crucial for understanding their evolution and the physics of accretion. High-resolution X-ray spectroscopy can trace the WD motion through Doppler shifts of emission lines formed close to the WD. We report an 83~ks XRISM/Resolve observation of the intermediate polar EX~Hydrae and measure the orbital modulation of individual Fe K-shell line centroids. The Fe~{\sc xxv} K$α$ components show coherent orbital modulation, yielding $K_1 = 58.1 \pm 8.5\ \mathrm{km\ s^{-1}}$. This is the first detection of orbital modulation in individual Fe K-shell lines from an accreting WD, made possible by the high spectral resolution of Resolve and its frequent in-orbit gain calibration. The measured $K_1$ is consistent with optical/UV $K_1$ measurements, providing a cross-check that these distinct tracers follow the WD orbital motion. Combining this X-ray measurement with literature orbital parameters, we derive a WD mass of $M_1 = 0.79 \pm 0.04\ M_\odot$. These results demonstrate that high-resolution X-ray spectroscopy can use individual Fe K-shell line centroids to trace WD orbital motion in accreting WDs.

</details>


### [226] [Late-time evolution of the interacting stripped-envelope supernova 2017dio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28918)
*C. Humina,C. P. Gutiérrez,H. Kuncarayakti,T. Nagao,S. González-Gaitán,T. Kangas,T. Kravtsov,K. Maeda,S. Mattila,T. M. Reynolds,L. Tartaglia,S. Benetti,M. Fraser,C. Gall,P. Lundqvist,A. Morales-Garoffolo,M. Stritzinger*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过晚期观测和建模，研究了与致密星周介质相互作用的Ic型超新星SN 2017dio的质量损失机制和前身星通道，发现其CSM峰值质量损失率约0.2 M☉/yr，CSM形成于爆发前4至65年，红外超源于尘埃辐射，并推断氢-rich CSM来源于伴星而非前身星本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现与致密星周介质相互作用的剥离包层超新星挑战了现有的大质量恒星演化理论，因此需要深入研究这类超新星的质量损失机制和前身星通道。

Method: 分析晚期光谱和光变曲线；对抛射物-CSM相互作用和尘埃红外回波进行辐射建模；研究光谱特征演化，重点分析Hα发射线。

Result: CSM峰值质量损失率~0.2 M☉/yr，典型值~0.06 M☉/yr；CSM形成于爆发前4至65年，半径起始于~1.3×10¹⁵ cm；红外超与碳尘埃（质量~0.001至0.005 M☉）或硅酸盐尘埃（~0.005至0.02 M☉）辐射一致；红外回波建模给出尘埃质量上限4×10⁻⁵ M☉，对应前身星质量损失率2.4×10⁻⁵ M☉/yr。

Conclusion: SN 2017dio的前身星虽已失去氦包层，但与爆发前不久形成的氢-rich CSM相互作用，表明该物质来自伴星而非前身星本身；质量损失率在爆发前急剧增加。

Abstract: The discovery of stripped-envelope supernovae (SNe) interacting with dense circumstellar medium (CSM) challenges our current understanding of massive star evolution. We present late-time observations of the interacting Type Ic SN 2017dio and investigate its mass-loss mechanism and progenitor channel. We analysed late-time spectra and light curves (LCs) that are dominated by ejecta-CSM interaction. We examined the CSM and the source of the infrared (IR) excess by modelling the radiation produced by the ejecta-CSM interaction and the IR echo from circumstellar dust. In addition, we studied the evolution of spectral features, with a particular emphasis on the Halpha emission line. From the combined analysis of the LCs and spectral properties, we infer that the peak mass-loss rate for the CSM reaches ~0.2 $M_{\odot}/yr$ and that the typical value over most epochs is ~0.06 $M_{\odot}/yr$. The nearby CSM was formed over a period of 4 to 65 years before the explosion. The CSM radius begins at ~$1.3\cdot10^{15}$ cm. The IR excess identified in the LCs is consistent with the radiation from dust with a mass increasing from ~0.001 to ~0.005 $M_{\odot}$ in the case of carbon dust or ~0.005 to ~0.02 $M_{\odot}$ in the case of silicate dust. From IR echo modelling, we estimate an upper limit on the dust mass of $4\cdot10^{-5} M_{\odot}$, which implies an SN progenitor mass-loss rate of $2.4\cdot10^{-5} M_{\odot}/yr$ at the dust evaporation radius determined by the SN peak luminosity (0.017 pc for carbon dust, corresponding to mass loss ~170 years before the explosion). This implies a very rapid increase in the mass-loss rate ahead of the explosion. Although the progenitor of SN 2017dio has lost its helium envelope, it interacted with a hydrogen-rich CSM formed shortly before the explosion, suggesting that this material originated from a companion star rather than the progenitor itself. [Abridged]

</details>


### [227] [Neutron Star Mass across Binary Pulsar Subpopulations: Mass-Spin Correlation, Mass Distributions, and Moment of Inertia Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29146)
*Debatri Chattopadhyay*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对约50颗银河系射电脉冲双星进行分层贝叶斯分析，发现中子星质量与自旋周期在再循环种群中呈中等程度反相关（ρ=-0.26），双中子星系统则呈现正相关趋势，并确认了双中子星系统中伴星质量与轨道偏心率的相关性。结果支持吸积驱动的再循环与双通道形成图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测对中子星质量、自旋与轨道性质的联合统计特征认识不足，不同双星类型（PSR-WD与DNS）的形成通道尚未在群体层面得到严格的贝叶斯推断验证。

Method: 对约50个银河系射电脉冲双星（含PSR-WD和DNS系统）进行分层贝叶斯分析，联合建模中子星质量、自旋周期、轨道周期、倾角、偏心率等参数，并考虑了候选DNS的归属不确定性及射电可探测性选择效应校正。

Result: 再循环种群中质量与自旋周期呈中等反相关（ρ=-0.26，90%可信区间排除零）；DNS系统呈正相关趋势（ρ=+0.13）但统计不显著；质量与轨道周期、倾角无显著相关，与偏心率弱相关；氦白矮星伴星的中子星质量略高于CO/O-Ne白矮星伴星（Δ≈0.06 M⊙）；确认DNS中伴星质量与轨道偏心率强相关（ρ=+0.82）。

Conclusion: 观测结果支持吸积驱动的再循环图像，但无法在统计上区分吸积起源与转动惯量驱动的自旋加速机制；整体上支持PSR-WD（吸积增长）与DNS（初始质量主导）的双通道形成图像。

Abstract: We present a hierarchical Bayesian analysis of the joint mass, spin, and orbital properties of $\sim 50$ Galactic binary radio pulsars with measured neutron star masses, classified by binary type into pulsar-white dwarf (PSR-WD) and double neutron star (DNS) systems. We find moderate evidence for an anti-correlation between neutron star mass and spin period in the pooled recycled population (correlation coefficient $ρ= -0.26$, with $96\%$ of the posterior probability at $ρ<0$; the $90\%$ credible interval excludes zero), robust to the treatment of candidate DNSs and to a radio-detectability selection correction. Although consistent with accretion-driven recycling, the correlation cannot statistically distinguish an accretion origin from a moment of inertia-driven spin-up mechanism, because the neutron star moment of inertia is nearly linear in mass over the observed range. The DNS systems alone instead lean to the positive side expected from the moment-of-inertia mechanism ($ρ=+0.13$), though with only ten systems this is not statistically conclusive. Mass shows no significant correlation with orbital period or inclination, and only a weak one with eccentricity. As a secondary result, neutron stars with helium white dwarf companions are marginally more massive than those with carbon-oxygen/oxygen-neon white dwarf companions ($Δ\simeq 0.06\,M_\odot$), consistent with more extensive accretion in the helium white dwarf channel. We confirm, in a hierarchical framework, the previously reported correlation between companion mass and orbital eccentricity in double neutron stars ($ρ=+0.82$). We interpret these results within a two-channel picture -- accretion-grown PSR-WD versus birth-mass-dominated DNS.

</details>


### [228] [SKA-VLBI Probes of High-energy Emission Processes in Relativistic Jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29365)
*M. Kadler,E. K. Bempong-Manful,P. G. Edwards,F. Eppel,C. M. Fromm,M. Giroletti,J. Heßdörfer,T. Hovatta,Y. Y. Kovalev,K. Mannheim,R. Ojha,F. Rösch,E. Ros*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了活动星系核中的相对论性喷流，特别是耀变体（blazars）作为高能X射线和伽马射线源的特征。指出高同步峰BL Lac天体是高能中微子的可能来源，但存在种子光子来源和多普勒危机等未解问题。SKA-VLBI有望通过高灵敏度、高角分辨率的成像和偏振测量，为研究微弱耀变体喷流结构提供关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高能光子与中微子发射过程中的未解问题，包括光-π介子产生的种子光子来源以及TeV发射BL Lac天体的多普勒危机。

Method: 利用SKA-VLBI结合相控阵SKA-Mid与全球VLBI阵列，实现对毫角秒分辨率下的高灵敏度成像和偏振测量。

Result: 对微弱耀变体实现高灵敏度、高角分辨率的成像与偏振探测，获得明亮源中结构化喷流的高保真空间分辨参数化。

Conclusion: SKA-VLBI的高分辨率探测能力将为约束AGN喷流中高能光子和粒子发射的物理模型提供关键见解。

Abstract: Relativistic jets in the nuclei of active galaxies are ubiquitous sources of high-energy emission. In particular, blazars represent the most luminous persistent X-ray and gamma-ray sources, whose defining characteristics are small jet inclination angles to the line of sight. Blazars can be detected in many cases up to TeV energies and the largest class of TeV emitting extragalactic AGN is represented by high-synchrotron peaked (HSP) BL Lac objects, which are generally comparably faint radio sources.
  Moreover, evidence has also been accumulated that high-energy cosmic neutrinos detected by IceCube can be associated with blazars. There is an increasing number of suggested blazar-neutrino associations, along with many cases of coincident flaring radio emission, but in a majority of cases, faint blazars on the level of millijanskies or below have to be considered.
  These high-energy photon and neutrino emission processes hold many unanswered questions including the unknown source of seed-photons for photo-pion production and the infamous Doppler crisis of TeV-emitting BL Lac objects. SKA-VLBI offers the opportunity to achieve superior sensitivity at milliarcsecond resolutions, provided by the combination of the phased SKA-Mid and global VLBI arrays. This opens the possibility to perform high-sensitivity and high-angular resolution imaging and polarimetric probes of faint blazars. The resulting high-fidelity spatially resolved parameterizations of structured jets in bright sources will yield key insights to constrain physical models of high-energy photon and particle emission in AGN jets.

</details>


### [229] [Modelling the delayed shock-breakout emission following jet-launching binary neutron star mergers via relativistic MHD simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29515)
*Matteo Pais,Riccardo Ciolfi,Andrea Pavan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过相对论磁流体动力学模拟，研究了双中子星并合后喷流驱动的激波突破发射，发现引力波-电磁信号时延对观测视角和抛射物不透明度依赖较弱，可作为喷流模型的可靠诊断，并与GRB 170817A观测对比表明，显著受阻喷流模型最符合观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星并合中相对论性喷流激波突破抛射物时会产生电磁信号，该信号与引力波信号之间的时间延迟是探测喷流发射条件和早期传播物理的强有力探针。

Method: 将前期BNS并合模拟结果直接导入作为后并合环境，进行相对论MHD模拟追踪喷流传播；构建早期动力学抛射物至其自然前沿；在3D下追踪至0.6秒后转为轴对称高分辨率模拟；考虑不同喷流发射时间和光度，选取三种代表模型（从早期突破到喷流受阻）；追踪喷流驱动前向激波至光球层，计算角分辨的bolometric SBO光度，考虑非径向光子传播、相对论多普勒效应和光行时效应，并考虑相差10倍的两个不透明度值。

Result: GW-EM时延对观测视角和抛射物不透明度的依赖均较弱，使其成为约束喷流模型的稳健诊断；与GRB 170817A对比，显著受阻喷流模型在峰值bolometric光度、GW-EM时延和信号持续时间上最为吻合。

Conclusion: GW-EM时延可作为BNS并合喷流模型的稳健诊断工具；GRB 170817A的观测特征支持喷流在抛射物中显著受阻的物理图像。

Abstract: In binary neutron star (BNS) mergers launching a relativistic jet, an electromagnetic (EM) signal is produced when the jet-driven shock breaks out of the merger ejecta. The observed time delay of this shock-breakout (SBO) emission with respect to the gravitational-wave (GW) signal from the merger provides a powerful probe of the physical conditions governing jet launching and early-time jet propagation. Considering different models of jet propagation in realistic post-merger environments, we investigate the SBO emission and corresponding GW-EM delay that would be observed depending on the viewing angle and the assumed ejecta opacity. We perform relativistic MHD simulations of jets propagating through a post-merger environment directly imported from the outcome of a previous BNS merger simulation. We also introduce a specific procedure to faithfully reconstruct the early dynamical ejecta up to their natural front. The evolution is followed in 3D up to 0.6 s and then continued imposing axisymmetry and higher resolution. Varying jet launching time and luminosity, we identify three representative models spanning regimes from early breakout to extended jet choking. For each case, we track the jet-driven forward shock up to the photosphere and compute the angle-dependent bolometric SBO luminosity, assuming full conversion of the thermal energy within the shocked material into radiation, and taking into account non-radial photon propagation, relativistic Doppler shifts, and light-travel-time effects. We consider two opacity values spanning a factor of 10. We find that the GW-EM delay depends weakly on both viewing angle and ejecta opacity, making it a robust diagnostic for constraining models. Comparing with GRB 170817A, the model resulting in a substantially choked jet provides the most plausible peak bolometric luminosity and the closest match to the observed GW-EM delay and signal duration.

</details>


### [230] [General Relativistic Shock Wave Solutions with Black Hole Formation: The Singular Isothermal Sphere Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29607)
*Chien-Ting J. Chen,Michael J. Cai,Fabio Pacucci*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在完全广义相对论框架下，解析求解了奇异等温球塌缩为黑洞过程中伴随激波的相似解，发现中心吸积率比光滑膨胀波解低约5-7倍，激波释放能量可达封闭静质量的~10%，为直接塌缩黑洞形成提供了能量预算，并对JWST发现的高红移超大质量黑洞、小红点及伽马射线暴等瞬变现象具有重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST在z≳6发现了大量超大质量黑洞（SMBH）和估计质量>10¹⁰M☉的类星体，亟需高效的早期增长路径。奇异等温球塌缩已有解析解，但伴随塌缩的激波尚未在广义相对论框架下得到处理。

Method: 扩展Cai & Shu (2005)的框架至非连续流，推导等温流体的广义相对论激波跃变条件，将内部塌缩解与外部静态、膨胀或塌缩包层连接；利用零速度面的坐标匹配技术桥接Schwarzschild和共动自相似描述。

Result: 获得了传播速度可达~40%光速的丰富激波族；中心吸积率相比光滑膨胀波解被抑制约5-7倍；激波释放能量达到封闭静质量的~10%，接近Schwarzschild吸积辐射效率（5.7%）的两倍。

Conclusion: 为直接塌缩黑洞形成提供了解析能量预算，对SMBH种子形成、高红移黑洞周围的稠密茧层、JWST发现的小红点及伽马射线暴等相对论性瞬变现象具有重要启示。

Abstract: The rapid emergence at $z\gtrsim 6$ of ubiquitous populations of supermassive black holes (SMBHs) revealed by JWST and of quasars with estimated masses $M_\bullet > 10^{10} M_\odot$ demands efficient pathways for early growth. The smooth collapse of a singular isothermal sphere (SIS) has been solved analytically in full general relativity, but the shock waves that inevitably accompany such collapse have not. Here, we derive general-relativistic self-similar shock-wave solutions for the collapse of an SIS to a black hole, extending the framework of Cai \& Shu (2005) to discontinuous flows. We obtain the general relativistic jump conditions for an isothermal fluid and show that they connect interior collapse solutions to exterior envelopes that may be static, expanding, or collapsing, yielding a rich family of shocks propagating at up to $\sim$40\% the speed of light; the available exterior types narrow with increasing sound speed. A coordinate-matching technique that uses the zero-velocity surface uniquely bridges the Schwarzschild and comoving self-similar descriptions, completing the characterization of the growing black hole. The central accretion rate is set by the interior collapse alone and is suppressed by a factor of $\sim$5--7 relative to the smooth expansion-wave solution, while the energy released at the shock reaches $\sim$10\% of the enclosed rest mass -- nearly twice the 5.7\% radiative efficiency of Schwarzschild accretion. These results provide an analytical energy budget for direct-collapse black hole formation, with implications for SMBH seed assembly, the dense cocoons around nascent high-redshift black holes, the recently discovered JWST's Little Red Dots, and relativistic transients such as gamma-ray bursts.

</details>


### [231] [Pulsed Infrared Emission from Magnetar 4U 0142+61 Detected by JWST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29653)
*Jeremy Hare,George G. Pavlov,Bettina Posselt,George Younes,Oleg Kargaltsev*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用JWST NIRCam在4.08μm波段观测磁星4U 0142+61，测得其流量密度22.9±0.6μJy，检测到与自转周期一致的115.059±0.035mHz脉冲信号，脉冲轮廓为单峰结构（受限于时间分辨率），脉冲分数下限约10%。对比X射线脉冲轮廓发现红外峰值与硬X射线峰值重叠，暗示脉冲红外辐射起源于磁层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星的红外脉冲辐射机制尚不明确，需要通过高灵敏度中红外观测确认其脉冲特征并探究辐射起源。

Method: 使用JWST NIRCam在F410M滤镜（中心波长4.08μm）以2.5秒时间分辨率对磁星4U 0142+61进行33分钟时序模式观测，分析流量密度、脉冲频率和脉冲轮廓，并与NICER/NuSTAR的X射线观测进行对比。

Result: 测得流量密度22.9±0.6μJy，检测到115.059±0.035mHz脉冲（与自转周期一致），脉冲轮廓为单峰，脉冲分数下限约10%。红外脉冲峰与硬X射线峰重叠。

Conclusion: 磁星4U 0142+61在4μm波段存在显著的脉冲辐射，其红外脉冲峰与硬X射线峰重合表明脉冲红外发射可能来自磁层，而非来自星体表面或周围尘埃的回响。

Abstract: We report on a JWST observation of the magnetar 4U 0142+61 on 2024 August 18 with the Near-Infrared Camera (NIRCam). NIRCam observed the magnetar for 33~min in timing mode, providing a time resolution of 2.5~s. In the F410M filter (pivot wavelength 4.08 $μ$m), we measured the flux density $f_ν= 22.9\pm0.6$ $μ$Jy and detected pulsations at a frequency of $115.059\pm0.035$ mHz, in agreement with the magnetar's spin period at the epoch of the JWST observation. The observed pulse profile has one peak per period (although this may be due to the poor time resolution), with a lower limit on the pulsed fraction of about 10\%. We compare the IR pulse profile to the NICER and NuSTAR X-ray pulse profiles and find that the IR peak overlaps with the hard X-ray peak, suggesting a magnetospheric origin for the pulsed IR emission.

</details>


### [232] [VLBI Astrometry of Magnetars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29671)
*Takuya Akahori,Sujin Eie,Kei Amada,Sangita Kumari,Kohei Kurahara,Hiroto Masaoka,Hao Ding,Teruaki Enoto,Shota Kisaka,Keiichi Maeda,Kotaro Niinuma*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文探讨磁星（宇宙中最强磁场的天体）的起源问题，提出增强型发电机效应在不规则超新星爆发中可能产生磁星，这会导致磁星具有更高的自行速度和超新星遗迹的不规则形态。文章介绍了MONSTER项目，并指出SKA-VLBI的高灵敏度和高角分辨率将能够在数月内测量磁星的自行，从而验证这一假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星作为宇宙中最强磁场的起源是一个长期未解的问题，目前缺乏精确的自行测量和宿主超新星遗迹的识别来验证增强型发电机效应的假说。

Method: 利用VLBI天体测量技术对磁星进行监测（MONSTER项目），借助SKA-VLBI的超高灵敏度和角分辨率，在射电暴发期间（数月内）测量磁星的自行运动。

Result: SKA-VLBI能够显著扩大磁星自行测量的巡天体积，使在射电暴发窗口期内完成测量成为可能。

Conclusion: SKA-VLBI的高灵敏度和高角分辨率将为验证磁星起源于不规则超新星爆发中增强发电机效应的假说提供关键观测手段。

Abstract: The origin of the strongest magnetic fields in the Universe, i.e., the origin of magnetars, is a longstanding question. An enhanced dynamo effect in an irregular supernova explosion is a possible origin, which implies a stronger kick velocity and a higher proper motion of the magnetar compared to those of ordinary pulsars as well as an irregular morphology of the host supernova remnant (SNR). However, this hypothesis is not well studied yet, because there is a lack of precise measurement of the proper motion of magnetar and of identification of the host SNR. VLBI astrometry of magnetars is a unique tool to examine the hypothesis. In this chapter, we introduce the MONSTER (Monitoring Observations of the Neutron Stars That Evolve Rapidly) Project. SKA-VLBI's unprecedented sensitivity and the highest angular resolution will allow us to dramatically expand the survey volume in which we can measure the proper motion of magnetars within a radio outburst period of a few months.

</details>


### [233] [Constraints on Hadronic Emission from Microquasars Detected by LHAASO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29830)
*V. Vecchiotti,E. Amato,G. Giacinti,G. Morlino,G. Peron*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: LHAASO合作组报告了6个微类星体的超高能伽马射线发射，其中5个超过100 TeV。研究发现这些辐射不能完全由强子相互作用解释，但GRS 1915+105和MAXI J1820+070在加速度效率>10%时可能成立。这些源对银河系宇宙线膝区谱的贡献最多约4%-37%，取决于注入时间和加速度效率参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究微类星体是否作为银河系PeVatron候选体，即其100 TeV左右的伽马射线是否来自加速宇宙线与周围介质的强子相互作用，并评估这些源对膝区宇宙线谱的贡献。

Method: 假设扩散主导的宇宙线传播，源附近扩散系数低于银河平均值，远处等于银河平均值；假设连续注入时间尺度0.1-1 Myr；利用气体密度实测数据计算强子贡献；对未探测到UHE辐射的源给出上限。

Result: 强子相互作用不能完全解释任何探测到的源；GRS 1915+105和MAXI J1820+070在加速度效率>10%时强子场景可能有效；探测源对1 PeV宇宙线贡献最多~4%（0.1 Myr, 10%效率）至37%（最大允许效率）；更长注入时间（1 Myr）会超出观测约束。

Conclusion: 微类星体作为银河PeVatron的强子辐射场景受到限制，仅在特定参数条件下可能成立；其对银河宇宙线膝区谱的贡献有限，短注入时间下贡献较小，长注入时间则面临观测约束挑战。

Abstract: Recently, the LHAASO collaboration reported ultra-high-energy (UHE) gamma rays from six microquasars. For five of these sources, the emission extends beyond $100$ TeV, making microquasars promising candidates for Galactic PeVatrons. We investigate whether gamma-rays around $100$ TeV originate from hadronic interactions of accelerated cosmic rays (CRs) with the ambient medium, and we estimate the contribution of these sources to the measured CR spectrum around the knee. We also place upper limits on six LHAASO microquasars with no detected UHE emission. We assume diffusion-dominated propagation of CR, with a diffusion coefficient suppressed compared to the average Galactic value near the source and equal to the Galactic value at large distances. We assume continuous injection over timescales of $t_{\rm age}=0.1-1$ Myrs. Using available measurements of the gas density, we find that hadronic interactions alone cannot fully account for the observed emission for any of the detected sources. However, in the case of GRS 1915+105 and MAXI J1820+070, the hadronic scenario may still be valid when considering acceleration efficiency higher than $10\%$. We then derive upper limits on the hadronic contribution to the observed gamma-ray flux. We estimate that the detected sources contribute at most $\sim4\%$ of the Galactic CR spectrum at $1$ PeV for an injection timescales of $0.1$ Myrs and a CR acceleration efficiency of $10 \%$. When adopting the maximum acceleration efficiency allowed by the gamma-ray observations the contribution rises to $37\%$. Longer injection timescales ($\sim 1$ Myrs) lead to contributions exceeding the observational constraints. For sources not detected at UHE, we obtained a maximum contribution of $\sim 17\%$, achieved assuming continuous injection over $1$ Myr.

</details>


### [234] [Optimising transient discovery with Swift-XRT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30141)
*S. Srivastava,P. A. Evans,M. R. Goad,R. A. J. Eyles-Ferris*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模拟的贝叶斯框架，用于校正Swift-XRT点源目录中的Eddington偏差，从而更准确地识别X射线暂现源。该方法将低显著性候选源的确认数量从原有的数十个提升至500多个（八倍以上），并通过真实图像模拟验证了其在不同曝光和背景条件下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swift-XRT实时点源目录中大量候选源因亮度接近探测阈值而被归类为"低显著性"，难以区分真实暂现源与统计涨落；同时这些源受到Eddington偏差影响，导致测量流量被高估。现有方法无法可靠判别，亟需一种能校正该偏差并提供更准确暂现概率的统计框架。

Method: 构建一个基于模拟的贝叶斯框架：利用真实Swift-XRT图像生成大量模拟数据，建模源的真实流量分布与探测过程，对Eddington偏差进行校正，计算每个源的真实强度超过历史3σ上限的后验概率，从而判定其是否为真实暂现源。

Result: 该方法将确认的暂现源数量从原始样本的数十个提升至500多个（超过八倍）；通过基于真实Swift-XRT图像的广泛模拟验证，该方法在不同曝光时间和背景条件下均保持稳定，证明了校正后暂现概率的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 校正Eddington偏差后的LSXPS暂现概率提供了一个可靠且内部一致的框架，可用于实时X射线暂现源识别，显著提升了低显著性候选源的分类可靠性。

Abstract: The Living Swift-XRT Point Source Catalogue (LSXPS) enables near real-time searches for X-ray transients. Many detected candidates are faint, often near the XRT detection limit, and are classed as "low significance," as it is often unclear whether their apparent brightening reflects a genuine transient or a statistical fluctuation. Some of these sources are affected by Eddington bias, a statistical effect that inflates measured fluxes near the detection threshold. We present a simulation-based Bayesian framework that corrects for this bias and provides more accurate probabilities for each source being truly transient, i.e. that its true intensity exceeds the historical 3$σ$ upper limit. Applied to LSXPS data, this method yields more reliable classifications, recovering over 500 transients above this threshold -- more than an eight-fold increase over the original confirmed sample. Using extensive simulations based on real Swift-XRT images, we validate the robustness of this approach, showing that it remains stable across varying exposure times and background conditions. These results demonstrate that the LSXPS transient probabilities, corrected for Eddington bias, provide a reliable and internally consistent framework for real-time X-ray transient identification.

</details>


### [235] [Joint population and strong-lensing inference for resolved gravitational-wave events probes the black-hole merger rate beyond the peak of star formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30144)
*Matthew Mould*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA的二元黑洞合并目录进行强引力透镜效应分析，同时推断每个事件是否被强透镜、其放大倍数以及潜在的合并群体。发现任何事件的后验透镜概率不超过1%，群体约束与未检测到透镜的假设一致，但红移峰值不确定度降低约10%，高红移率上限降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波在传播过程中可能被中间势场透镜化，强透镜会导致距离低估和质量高估，从而偏误天体物理结果。现有分析未系统考虑每个事件是否被强透镜及其对群体推断的影响。

Method: 同时推断三个层次：(1) 每个事件是否被强透镜，(2) 若被透镜则其放大倍数，(3) 底层并合群体。采用参数化和非参数化群体模型，以及两种透镜光学深度模型进行分析。

Result: 任何事件的后验透镜概率不超过1%；群体约束与假设未检测到强透镜或透镜从未发生的标准分析一致；红移峰值不确定度降低约10%，高红移率上限降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 虽然改进幅度不大，但这是首次利用已解析事件在通常地面引力波探测器不敏感的峰值恒星形成区及更高红移处获得约束，展示了考虑透镜效应对群体推断的潜在价值。

Abstract: Gravitational waves can be lensed by intervening potentials of various scales. Strong lensing leads to underestimated distances and overestimated masses, biasing astrophysical results if not accounted for. I present a novel analysis of the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA catalog of binary black-hole mergers, simultaneously inferring (1) whether or not each event is strongly lensed, (2) their magnifications if so, and (3) the underlying merger population, using both parametric and nonparametric population models as well as two models for the lensing optical depth. Posterior lensing probabilities do not exceed 1% for any event, so population constraints are consistent with those assuming nondetection of strong lensing or that lensing never occurs. This includes multiple subpopulations over black-hole mass and a component with high aligned spins. Compared to standard analyses, however, there are reductions of order 10% in uncertainty on the redshift at which the merger rate peaks and an order of magnitude in high-redshift rate upper limits. Though modest, these are the first constraints using only resolved events at redshifts where current ground-based gravitational-wave detectors are usually insensitive, at and beyond the peak of star formation.

</details>


### [236] [Systematic study of the morphology and length of slow stable hybrid star branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30264)
*Mauro Mariani,Milva G. Orsaria,Germán Lugones,Ignacio F. Ranea-Sandoval*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了慢速稳定混合星分支的长度，作为慢速相转换下混合中子星扩展稳定区域的定量指标。通过构建大量混合状态方程，识别出四种形态类型（瀑布型、桥接型、尾巴型、尾桥型），发现其分布主要由相变压力和能量密度跳跃决定。在慢速转换框架下，硬的强子状态方程仍可满足现有天体物理和微观物理约束，并在相变重子密度-密度跳跃平面上开辟了新的可行参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化慢速稳定混合星分支长度，作为混合中子星在慢速相转换下扩展稳定区域的度量；探究传统快速转换假设可能排除但在慢速转换下可行的参数空间。

Method: 结合广义分段多方位强子状态方程（不同刚度）与恒声速夸克物质模型，构建覆盖宽范围相变压力、能量密度跳跃和夸克声速的混合状态方程集合；分析质量-半径平面中慢速稳定分支的形态分类及参数依赖；施加当前天体物理与微观物理约束进行筛选。

Result: 识别出四种形态类型：瀑布型（单调下降）、桥接型（连接稳定分支）、尾巴型（延伸超越稳定分支）、尾桥型（兼具两者）；分支长度主要受相变压力和能量密度跳跃控制，强子刚度和夸克声速也有显著影响；在约束下，可行长分支主要为瀑布型；慢速转换框架下硬的强子状态方程仍与所有约束兼容；在相变重子密度-密度跳跃平面上开放了新的可行参数区域。

Conclusion: 慢速相转换假设使混合中子星模型具备更强的观测兼容性，特别是对硬的强子状态方程；该框架显著扩展了混合星的理论可行参数空间，为解释大质量中子星观测提供了新的理论途径。

Abstract: We introduce and systematically study the length of the slow stable hybrid star branch as a quantitative measure of the extended stability region that arises in hybrid neutron stars when the hadron-quark phase conversion is slow compared to the radial oscillation timescale. Combining generalized piecewise-polytropic hadronic equations of state of varying stiffness with a constant-speed-of-sound quark-matter model, we construct a large set of hybrid equations of state spanning a broad range of transition pressures, energy-density jumps, and quark-matter speeds of sound. We identify four morphological types for the slow stable branch in the mass-radius plane: waterfall branches that descend monotonically from the hadronic maximum mass, bridges that connect the hadronic branch to a second unconditionally stable hybrid branch, tails that extend briefly beyond the maximum mass of an unconditionally stable hybrid branch, and tail-bridges that combine features of the latter two. Their prevalence is governed primarily by the transition pressure and the energy-density jump, while the branch length is also significantly influenced by the stiffness of the hadronic sector and the quark-matter speed of sound. Imposing current astrophysical and microphysical constraints shows that viable long branches are predominantly of waterfall type, and that stiff hadronic equations of state -- strongly disfavored under the rapid-conversion assumption -- remain compatible with all current constraints within the slow-conversion framework. In the plane of transition baryon density versus density jump, slow stable configurations open a new region of viable parameter space inaccessible under rapid conversions.

</details>


### [237] [Enabling population studies on wind-driven Galactic binary systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30323)
*B. Marcote,P. Benaglia,V. Bosch-Ramon,M. De Becker,S. del Palacio,P. Atri,J. Moldón,J. M. Paredes,M. Ribó,G. E. Romero,A. Tej*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了星系中由恒星风激波驱动的双星系统（如碰撞风双星和伽马射线双星）在粒子加速方面的潜力，指出当前观测在低频射电和高分辨率方面的不足，并展望SKAO（包括SKA-low、SKA-mid和SKA-VLBI）将显著提升灵敏度和角分辨率，从而推动对这些双星系统风动力学、激波物理及整体种群的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管星系双星系统（如碰撞风双星和伽马射线双星）拥有宇宙中最有效的粒子加速引擎之一，但当前对这些源的研究仍相对匮乏，尤其在低频射电和高角分辨率领域，缺乏全面的种群研究和充分表征的个体系统。现有观测难以探测低能粒子群的弱非热同步辐射，也无法分辨亚天文单位尺度的激波结构。

Method: 利用SKAO的SKA-low和SKA-mid望远镜提升灵敏度和分辨率以解决当前观测难题；同时利用SKA-VLBI在不同轨道相位观测激波几何变化，将粒子加速过程与双星轨道特征及恒星风性质联系起来。

Result: SKAO将实现对双星系统中恒星风和激波相互作用的系统性研究，并能观测不同轨道相位下激波几何的变化，从而建立粒子加速过程与双星轨道特性及恒星风属性之间的联系。

Conclusion: SKAO将推动对这些高能双星系统进行全面的种群研究，填补当前在低频射电和高分辨率观测上的空白。

Abstract: Galactic binaries driven by stellar wind shocks, such as colliding wind binaries (CWBs) and gamma-ray binaries (gBs), harbor one of the most efficient particle acceleration engines known in the Universe. Despite their potential, these sources remain relatively unexplored, particularly in the domains of low radio frequencies and very high resolution. As a result, we lack comprehensive population studies and well-characterized individual systems. Only a few of these binaries, such as the iconic gB PSR B1259$-$63 or the massive CWB WR 140, have been studied in enough detail to probe their wind dynamics and shock physics. Current observations lack the sensitivity to detect weak non-thermal synchrotron emission from low-energy particle populations and the angular resolution to resolve shock structures on sub-au scales. The Square Kilometre Array Observatory (SKAO) will mark a significant improvement in both sensitivity and resolution with its SKA-low and SKA-mid telescopes, solving these challenges. This will enable systematic studies of the winds and shock interactions in these binary systems. Additionally, SKA-VLBI will facilitate the observation of changes in shock geometry at different orbital phases, linking particle acceleration processes to the binary's orbital characteristics and stellar wind properties. SKAO will pave the way for comprehensive population studies of these energetic binary systems.

</details>


### [238] [Sloshing Motions in Abell 3571 Revealed by XRISM/Resolve Velocity Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30413)
*Itsuki Aihara,Congyao Zhang,Sora Nakajima,Kyoko Matsushita,Hannah McCall,Irina Zhuravleva,Shogo B. Kobayashi,Kotaro Fukushima,William R. Forman,Christine Jones,Annie Heinrich,Daniele Rogantini,Kosuke Sato,Kazunori Suda,Ildar Khabibullin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过XRISM Resolve对Abell 3571星系团中心~300 kpc区域的观测，发现其速度弥散为亚声速（~100-150 km/s），北部冷区相对BCG蓝移~-60 km/s，南部和东部热区红移~170 km/s。数值模拟表明该热力学和运动学不对称性与早期晃动（sloshing）一致，可能由南侧1.6 Mpc处的贫气团Abell 3572作为扰动体引发的离轴并合所致。由于缺乏显著AGN反馈，晃动驱动的气体再分布可能延缓了强冷核的重新建立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Abell 3571星系团中心区域的热力学结构和气体运动学特征，检验其是否由小质量并合引发的晃动主导，并评估晃动对冷核再建立过程的抑制作用。

Method: 使用XRISM Resolve对Abell 3571中心进行四个部分重叠的指向观测，覆盖北、南、东三个连续视场，总曝光约575 ks，结合数值模拟对比分析热力学和运动学不对称性。

Result: 速度弥散为~100-150 km/s（亚声速）；北部冷区蓝移~-60 km/s，南部和东部热区红移~170 km/s；整体不对称性与离轴小并合引发的早期晃动模拟一致；潜在扰动体为南侧1.6 Mpc处的Abell 3572。

Conclusion: Abell 3571的晃动由离轴小并合驱动，缺乏显著AGN反馈，晃动导致的气体再分布可能延缓了强冷核的恢复。

Abstract: Minor mergers can induce sloshing motions in the intracluster medium, leaving characteristic signatures in the thermodynamic structure and gas kinematics of cluster cores. Abell 3571 is an X-ray-bright, apparently relaxed cluster at $z \sim 0.04$. We observed the central $\sim 300$ kpc region of Abell 3571 with four partially overlapping XRISM Resolve pointings, covering three contiguous Resolve fields to the north, south and east with a total exposure time of approximately 575 ks. The velocity dispersions are subsonic and are at the level of $\sim 100$--$150 \, \mathrm{km~ \, s^{-1}}$ across most regions. The cooler region associated with the northern surface-brightness excess is blueshifted by up to $\sim -60 \, \mathrm{km \, s^{-1}}$ relative to the brightest cluster galaxy (BCG), while the hotter region in the southern and eastern surface-brightness deficit regions is redshifted by up to $\sim 170 \, \mathrm{km \, s^{-1}}$. Numerical simulations suggest that this large-scale thermodynamic and kinematic asymmetry is broadly consistent with early-phase sloshing induced by an off-axis minor merger. Abell 3572, an X-ray-faint gas-poor cluster located 1.6 Mpc to the south, is a promising candidate for the perturber. Given the lack of clear signatures of prominent AGN feedback in Abell 3571, these results suggest that sloshing-driven gas redistribution may contribute to delaying the re-establishment of a strong cool core in Abell 3571.

</details>


### [239] [Expansion rate of the young, oxygen-rich supernova remnant G292.0+1.8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30643)
*Maria Aslanidou,Manan Agarwal,Jacco Vink*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用钱德拉X射线天文台2006-2016年间对超新星遗迹G292.0+1.8的深度观测，测量其前向激波的膨胀率，发现宽带加权平均膨胀率为0.016%/年，对应膨胀年龄约2500年（均匀介质）或4100年（Wolf-Rayet星风密度轮廓），并揭示了不同元素层和方位角的膨胀不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心坍缩超新星遗迹是研究抛射物与星际介质相互作用、激波动力学及爆炸特性的理想目标，而G292.0+1.8作为经典遗迹具有富氧抛射物、环星物质、快速移动脉冲星和脉冲星风云等特征，通过精确测量其膨胀率可约束遗迹年龄和爆炸模型。

Method: 基于钱德拉ACIS-I的深度观测数据，获取2006年和2016年两个相隔约10年的历元；利用Gaia DR3源进行天体测量校正；在遗迹前向激波周围划分19个扇区提取径向轮廓，测量各扇区的膨胀率，并进行宽带和窄带（O-Ne、Mg、Si-S等α元素）分析。

Result: 宽带加权平均膨胀率为0.016%±0.001%/年，对应均匀介质中膨胀年龄约2500年（与先前2000-3700年估计一致）；若采用Wolf-Rayet星风密度轮廓（1/r²），年龄约为4100年。窄带分析显示轻元素膨胀行为与宽带一致，重元素膨胀较慢；发现了显著的方位角不对称性，某些扇区（约2500 km/s）优先沿中子星反冲方向膨胀。

Conclusion: G292.0+1.8的膨胀测量结果支持其年龄约2500-4100年，且激波膨胀具有显著的各向异性，其方位角不对称性与中子星反冲方向的关联以及反向激波与脉冲星风云相互作用产生的反射激波效应值得进一步探讨。

Abstract: Core-collapse supernova remnants (CCSNRs) are ideal targets for studying ejecta--interstellar-medium interactions, shock dynamics, and explosion characteristics. G292.0+1.8 is a classic CCSNR featuring oxygen-rich ejecta, circumstellar material, a rapidly moving pulsar, and a pulsar wind nebula (PWN). We examine its expansion rate using deep Chandra ACIS-I observations over two nearly independent $\sim$10 yr baselines (2006--2016). After applying astrometric corrections based on Gaia DR3 sources, we extracted radial profiles in 19 sectors around the forward shock. The weighted-mean expansion rate is $0.016\% \pm 0.001\%\,\mathrm{yr^{-1}}$ in the broadband, implying an expansion age of ${\sim}2500$ yr for a uniform ambient medium, consistent with previous estimates of 2000--3700 yr. For a $1/r^2$ circumstellar density profile (Wolf-Rayet progenitor wind), the inferred age is ${\sim}4100$ yr. Narrow-band analysis of $α$ elements (O-Ne, Mg, Si-S) shows that lighter elements follow the broadband behaviour, while heavier elements expand more slowly, consistent with their origin in deeper stellar layers. We discuss the pronounced azimuthal asymmetry of the expansion, the apparent paradox that some sectors expand (with $\sim$2500 km/s) preferentially in the direction of the neutron-star kick, and the role of reflected shocks from the reverse-shock--PWN interaction.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Bayesian Joint Estimation of the Hurst Parameter and Volatility with Applications to Fractional Option Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28522)
*Hana H. Sagor,Edward L. Boone,Ryad A. Ghanam*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯框架，用于在分数阶随机微分方程模型中联合推断Hurst参数和波动率，并将后验不确定性直接传播到分数Black-Scholes模型下的期权定价分布中。模拟和实证（WTI原油、天然气数据）表明该方法能稳定地进行后验推断和不确定性量化，且市场行为差异主要由波动率变化驱动而非长记忆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数布朗运动在金融建模中广泛应用，但Hurst参数估计的不确定性会直接影响期权定价结果。现有方法多基于点估计，无法有效量化参数不确定性对定价的影响，因此需要一种能够联合推断参数并将不确定性传播到定价中的方法。

Method: 提出贝叶斯框架，对分数阶随机微分方程（fSDE）模型中的Hurst参数和波动率进行联合推断，并将后验不确定性直接传播到分数Black-Scholes模型的期权定价分布中。

Result: 模拟结果表明方法在Hurst参数和样本量变化下均能实现稳定的后验推断和一致的不确定性量化。实证分析显示WTI原油和天然气市场行为差异主要由波动率变化驱动，而非强长记忆性；后验期权价格分布在不同市场制度下反映了显著的不确定性差异。

Conclusion: 在分数阶金融模型中纳入联合参数不确定性具有重要意义，贝叶斯方法在期权定价应用中具有实用价值，能够更全面地刻画定价不确定性。

Abstract: Fractional Brownian motion has been widely used in financial modeling to capture long-range dependence and persistent behavior observed in asset dynamics. In the fractional Black--Scholes framework, accurate estimation of the Hurst parameter is essential, since estimation uncertainty can directly affect option pricing results. In this paper, we propose a Bayesian framework for joint inference on the Hurst parameter and volatility in fractional stochastic differential equation models. In contrast to approaches based solely on point estimation, the proposed method propagates posterior uncertainty directly into option pricing distributions under the fractional Black--Scholes model.
  Simulation studies are conducted across multiple values of the Hurst parameter and sample sizes to evaluate estimation accuracy, posterior coverage, and pricing uncertainty. The results demonstrate stable posterior inference and coherent uncertainty quantification for both model parameters and option prices. The methodology is further illustrated using WTI crude oil and natural gas data under different market regimes. The empirical analysis indicates that differences in market behavior are driven primarily by changes in volatility rather than strong long-range dependence, while posterior option price distributions reflect substantial variation in pricing uncertainty across regimes. These findings highlight the importance of incorporating joint parameter uncertainty in fractional financial models and demonstrate the practical value of Bayesian methods for option pricing applications.

</details>


### [241] [Methods to address measurement error in both Outcome and Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28810)
*Pamela A. Shaw,Bryan E. Shepherd*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本教程系统回顾了生物医学研究中处理结局变量和多重协变量测量误差的可用方法，通过实例演示、提供数据和代码以便复现，并讨论了不同方法的优劣及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学研究越来越依赖电子健康记录等常规数据，但这些数据常存在测量误差。若不加以处理，会导致研究结果偏倚，误导临床决策，甚至对患者造成伤害。因此，需要针对结局变量和多个协变量中的误差处理方法。

Method: 以教程形式回顾了可用的测量误差校正方法，通过一个具体运行实例直接比较不同方法，并提供完整的数据和分析代码以便用户复现结果。

Result: 通过实例演示了不同测量误差校正方法的应用和比较效果，提供了可复现的代码和数据。

Conclusion: 对不同的测量误差处理方法进行了讨论，指出了该领域未来需要进一步研究的方向。

Abstract: Biomedical research is increasingly relying on readily available routine data, such as electronic health records. Routinely collected data, as well as datasets from large cohorts, are often prone to measurement error which, if not addressed in analyses, can bias study results and ultimately mislead clinical decision-making and potentially harm patients. For this setting, methods that address errors in the outcome and multiple covariates are needed. In this tutorial, we will review available methods to address for errors in both outcomes and covariates. We will illustrate methods with use of a running example in order to compare the methods directly. Both the data and analytic code are provided for the user so that they may easily reproduce results in each example. We conclude the tutorial with a discussion of the different approaches and highlight areas of future work needed for this setting.

</details>


### [242] [Semantic insurance pricing with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29371)
*Christopher Blier-Wong,Derek Kusmenko*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究使用预训练大语言模型生成的文本嵌入向量替代传统手工特征工程，作为广义线性模型的输入进行泊松索赔频率回归。基于法国车险数据，嵌入模型在数据稀疏时表现更好，大样本下效果取决于模型和维度。保险领域微调可进一步提升性能，且需对提示词进行管控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统精算定价模型（如GLM）虽然透明且易于监管，但需要显式特征工程才能引入风险因子间的交互作用。本文探索能否用预训练大语言模型从保单持有人的自然语言描述中提取嵌入向量，替代手工构建的特征输入到标准精算定价模型中。

Method: 以泊松索赔频率回归为例，使用预训练大语言模型从保单自然语言描述中计算确定性嵌入协变量，然后将其作为输入特征输入到标准广义线性模型进行定价。在法国机动车第三方责任险数据上进行实验，并进行保险领域的微调以及提示敏感性诊断。

Result: 嵌入模型在数据稀疏时优于传统GLM；大样本下比较结果依赖于模型选择和嵌入维度。保险领域微调可进一步优化嵌入质量。提示敏感性诊断显示，追加非模板字段会改变输出，因此需要受控提示。

Conclusion: 大语言模型嵌入可以替代部分手工特征工程用于精算定价，尤其在数据稀缺场景下具有优势，但需注意提示词管理以确保监管合规性。

Abstract: Classical actuarial pricing models, such as the generalized linear model, are valued for transparency and ease of governance, but they use interactions among risk factors only when these are supplied through explicit feature engineering. We study whether embeddings from a pre-trained large language model, computed from a natural-language description of each policyholder, can replace hand-crafted features as inputs to a standard actuarial pricing model, taking Poisson claim-frequency regression as the main example. The language model is used only to construct deterministic embedding covariates; pricing is performed by a standard generalized linear model. Using French motor third-party liability data, the embedding-based model outperforms the generalized linear model, especially when data are scarce, whereas at larger sample sizes the comparison is model- and dimension-dependent. Insurance-specific fine-tuning further improves the embeddings, and a prompt-sensitivity diagnostic shows that the pipeline reacts to any appended out-of-template field, making controlled prompts a governance requirement.

</details>


### [243] [Critique of "Use of roster charts in the investigation and prosecution of nurses ..." by John O' Quigley](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29394)
*Richard D. Gill*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文批评了John O'Quigley教授关于Lucy Letby案排班表的统计分析，指出其中存在严重错误，数据实际上反驳了他的主要建模假设。但双方一致认为，从法医统计角度看，该排班表是虚假证据，不应向陪审团展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出O'Quigley教授在分析Lucy Letby案中37名护士排班表时的统计错误，并强调该排班表作为法医证据的不可靠性。

Method: 通过对原始数据的重新分析，检验O'Quigley教授的主要建模假设，发现数据实际上强烈反驳了他的假设。

Result: 数据中包含的信息强烈反驳了O'Quigley教授的主要建模假设，证明其统计分析存在严重错误。

Conclusion: 尽管在统计方法上存在分歧，双方一致认为该排班表从法医统计角度看是虚假证据，不应被用作法庭证据。

Abstract: The paper "Use of roster charts in the investigation and prosecution of nurses suspected of inflicting deliberate harm on patients" by Prof. John O'Quigley explores an interesting hypothesis concerning statistical information hidden in the part of the infamous Lucy Letby roster chart pertaining to the 37 other nurses. Unfortunately, we have to point out some serious errors in his statistical analyses. The data actually contains information which strongly disproves his main modelling assumption. We do, however, strongly agree with him that from a forensic statistical point of view, the roster chart is fake evidence which should not have been shown to jurors.

</details>


### [244] [Bayesian Copula Directional Dependence is Cross-Network Robust for Gene-Regulatory Pair Direction: A Benchmark Study on DREAM5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29402)
*Xiaoying Wei,Clara Grazian*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于copula的贝叶斯方向推断方法（CDD），在DREAM5三个核心网络（in silico、S. aureus、E. coli）及S. cerevisiae域外测试中，相比八种竞争方法，CDD是唯一在所有核心网络上准确率>60%、覆盖率>88%、方向AUROC>0.6的方法，且在小样本场景下表现稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因调控关系方向推断比存在性推断更困难，现有方法多仅在单一模拟基准上验证，缺乏在真实生物网络和小样本场景下的可靠性评估。

Method: 将基于copula的方向依赖度量（CDD）嵌入贝叶斯框架，为每个候选基因对输出方向对比的后验分布、95%可信区间、后验符号支持分数以及原则性的"不判定"机制，并在DREAM5三个核心网络（模拟、S. aureus、E. coli）及S. cerevisiae域外测试上，与八种方向推断方法（包括两个贝叶斯DAG后验基线）进行比较。

Result: 在三个核心网络上，贝叶斯CDD是唯一在所有网络上准确率>60%、覆盖率>88%、方向AUROC>0.6的方法；CDD在两个真实生物网络上排名第一，在最小样本网络上保持稳定（此时bootstrap区间方法失效），并且是唯一在95%后验门控下同时高于随机水平且高覆盖率的贝叶斯方法。

Conclusion: CDD可作为候选调控对的后筛查、不确定性感知的方向精炼工具，尤其适用于真实生物网络和小样本场景。

Abstract: Inferring the direction of a gene-regulatory relationship is harder than inferring whether a relationship exists, and most direction-inference methods are validated mainly on a single in silico benchmark. We ask which method remains reliable as the data move from a synthetic network to real organisms and as sample size decreases. We embed a copula-based measure of directional dependence (CDD) in a Bayesian framework that returns, for each candidate pair, a posterior distribution over a directional contrast, a 95% credible interval, a posterior sign-support score, and a principled no-call. We benchmark this estimator against eight direction-inference methods, including two Bayesian DAG-posterior baselines, on the three core DREAM5 networks (in silico, S. aureus, and E. coli), with S. cerevisiae used as an out-of-domain eukaryotic stress test. Across the three core networks, Bayesian CDD is the only method whose called accuracy is always above 60%, whose coverage is always above 88%, and whose direction AUROC is always above 0.6; every competing method falls to chance or below on at least one network. CDD ranks first on both real-organism networks, remains stable on the smallest-sample network where bootstrap-interval methods collapse, and is the only Bayesian method that is simultaneously above chance and high-coverage under a 95% posterior gate. We position CDD as a post-screening, uncertainty-aware direction-refinement tool for candidate regulatory pairs.

</details>


### [245] [Probing the Stochastic Machine: Engaging with LLMs in Statistics Curricula Through Veridical Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29754)
*Tian Zheng*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 论文主张在统计学课程中应将大语言模型（LLMs）不仅作为工具，更作为研究客体，通过设计小实验和分析输出分布来探究其变异性、偏差和提示敏感性，并基于Veridical Data Science框架和PCS原则提出了从入门到研究生层次的四类课程示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为交互式随机系统，其关键行为尚未被充分理解，现有统计教育缺乏引导学生批判性审视LLM的系统方法。

Method: 基于Veridical Data Science框架和PCS（可预测性-可计算性-稳定性）原则，设计了四个层级的课程示例：入门级“问两次”活动、中级实验、高级分析到研究生级别的PCS稳定性审计。

Result: 提出了将LLM作为统计教育探究对象的系统化课程整合方案，涵盖不同教育层次。

Conclusion: 统计课程应引导学生将LLM视为研究对象，通过实验设计批判性分析其行为特性，从而培养对AI系统更深入的理解和评估能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are interactive stochastic systems whose most consequential behaviors are still only partially understood. This discussion argues that statistics curricula should treat LLMs not only as tools, but as objects of inquiry: students can probe variability, bias, and prompt sensitivity by designing small experiments and analyzing distributions of outputs. Building on the Veridical Data Science framework and Predictability-Computability-Stability (PCS) principles, this discussion outlines how to organize critical LLM engagement across educational levels and propose four curricular examples, from introductory ``ask it twice'' activities to graduate PCS stability audits of LLM-based analysis workflows.

</details>
