# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [New columns in decomposition matrices of symmetric groups for every block](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28731)
*David J. Hemmer,Pavel Turek*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称群模表示论中的分解矩阵，确定了由偶臂长p-可除钩子分区标记的新列，并在奇特征p下为每个对称群S_n的每个块确定了至少一个完整列。这些列是无重根的，由新引入的奇序列组合统计量描述。作为应用，确定了Foulkes模H^{(2^m)}的不可分解直和项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称群模表示论的核心未解问题是寻找分解矩阵，该矩阵描述了零特征不可约表示在模素数特征p下约化时的分解方式。本文旨在推进这一问题的解决。

Method: 研究由偶臂长p-可除钩子分区标记的列，利用新引入的称为"奇序列"的组合统计量（依赖于p）来描述这些列的结构。

Result: 确定了大量新的分解矩阵列（对应偶臂长p-可除钩子分区）；在奇特征p下，为每个对称群S_n的每个块确定了至少一个完整列；这些列具有无重根性；确定了Foulkes模H^{(2^m)}的不可分解直和项。

Conclusion: 本文在对称群分解矩阵的确定问题上取得了重要进展，为后续研究提供了新的工具（奇序列统计量）和具体结果（Foulkes模的分解）。

Abstract: The central unsolved problem in the modular representation theory of symmetric groups is to find the decomposition matrices, which describe how irreducible representations in characteristic zero decompose upon reduction modulo a prime characteristic $p$. In this paper we determine a large number of new columns in these decomposition matrices, namely those labeled by partitions whose $p$-divisible hooks have all even arm lengths. In particular in odd characteristic $p$, for every possible block of every possible symmetric group $S_n$, we determine at least one complete column. These columns are multiplicity free and are described by a recently introduced combinatorial statistic of partitions (depending on $p$), called the odd sequence. As an application, we determine the indecomposable summands of Foulkes modules $H^{(2^m)}$.

</details>


### [247] [Representations of compatible Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29032)
*Xabier García-Martínez,Manuel Ladra,Bernardo Leite da Cunha,Samuel A. Lopes*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文从代数和表示论角度研究相容李代数，发现经典李代数理论中的Lie定理、Weyl定理和Levi定理在该框架下均存在反例，并对二维相容李代数进行了分类和表示理论研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索相容李代数这一代数结构是否满足经典李代数理论的基本定理，并系统建立其表示理论框架。

Method: 通过构造反例否定经典定理；对二维相容李代数进行同构分类；利用表示论方法研究不可分解表示、野表示类型和不可约线表示，并导出Clebsch-Gordan分解。

Result: 1) 给出了Lie定理、Weyl定理和Levi定理的反例；2) 完成了二维相容李代数的同构分类；3) 发现了无限族不可分解非半单表示；4) 证明了可解二维相容李代数具有野表示类型；5) 分类了所有有限维不可约线表示；6) 证明了线表示张量积的Clebsch-Gordan分解。

Conclusion: 相容李代数与经典李代数在结构理论和表示理论上存在本质差异，其表示理论更加丰富复杂，具有野表示类型等新现象。

Abstract: We study compatible Lie algebras from algebraic and representation-theoretic points of view, obtaining counterexamples to some fundamental theorems from classical Lie algebra theory, namely the theorems of Lie, Weyl and Levi. We also classify the two-dimensional compatible Lie algebras up to isomorphism and explore their representation theory, presenting families of indecomposable non-semisimple representations, showing that the solvable two-dimensional compatible Lie algebras have wild representation type, and classifying all irreducible finite-dimensional line representations. Finally, we prove a Clebsch-Gordan decomposition for tensor products of finite-dimensional irreducible line representations.

</details>


### [248] [Quantum Betti geometric Langlands functor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29585)
*Ekaterina Bogdanova*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过Whittaker系数构造了Betti设定下的量子几何Langlands函子，并证明了该函子与2-栈$\operatorname{Ge}_{Z_G}$和$\operatorname{Ge}_{π_1(\check{G})}$上的范畴层之间的2-Fourier-Mukai等价是兼容的，这两个2-栈分别分类了关于群$G$的中心$Z_G$和对偶群$\check{G}$的代数基本群$π_1(\check{G})$在曲线$X$上的gerbe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立量子几何Langlands对应在Betti设定下的具体实现，并通过Whittaker系数构造相应的函子，同时揭示其与2-Fourier-Mukai等价之间的深层联系。

Method: 利用Whittaker系数构造量子几何Langlands函子，并借助2-栈上的范畴层之间的2-Fourier-Mukai等价来验证相容性。

Result: 成功构造了Betti设定下的量子几何Langlands函子，并证明了该函子与2-Fourier-Mukai等价之间的相容性。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子几何Langlands对应提供了Betti设定下的函子性构造，并建立了与2-Fourier-Mukai等价的联系，深化了对几何Langlands程序的理解。

Abstract: We construct the quantum geometric Langlands functor in the Betti setting via Whittaker coefficients. We show that the functor is compatible with the 2-Fourier-Mukai equivalence between sheaves of categories over 2-stacks $\operatorname{Ge}_{Z_G}$ and $\operatorname{Ge}_{π_1(\check{G})}$, which classify gerbes on $X$ with respect to the center $Z_G$ of $G$ and algebraic fundamental group $π_1(\check{G})$ of $\check{G}$.

</details>


### [249] [Ideal $n$-cotorsion pairs in Frobenius extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29728)
*Yixia Zhang,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在Frobenius extriangulated范畴中引入理想n-余挠对的概念，建立了其与稳定范畴中理想n-余挠对的对应关系，并在Krull-Schmidt正合范畴中建立了完全余挠对与完全理想余挠对之间的一一对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Frobenius正合范畴中理想余挠对与其稳定范畴中对应关系的启发，将理想n-余挠对的概念推广到extriangulated范畴，并探究更高维的理想逼近理论在Frobenius extriangulated范畴与其稳定范畴之间的桥梁。

Method: 通过在extriangulated范畴中定义理想n-余挠对，系统研究其在Frobenius extriangulated范畴C与稳定范畴C/ω之间的保持性，并利用Krull-Schmidt正合范畴的结构建立完全余挠对与完全理想余挠对的双射对应。

Result: (J, J)是C中的理想n-余挠对当且仅当(J/ω, J/ω)是C/ω中的理想n-余挠对；在Krull-Schmidt正合范畴中，完全余挠对与完全理想余挠对之间存在双射对应。

Conclusion: 建立了更高维理想逼近理论在Frobenius extriangulated范畴与其稳定范畴之间的桥梁，并回答了Fu、Guil Asensio、Herzog和Torrecillas提出的关于完全余挠对与完全理想余挠对对应关系的公开问题。

Abstract: Motivated by the correspondence between ideal cotorsion pairs in Frobenius exact categories and those in their stable categories, we introduce the notion of an ideal $n$-cotorsion pair in an extriangulated category. We study the relationship between ideal $n$-cotorsion pairs in a Frobenius extriangulated category $\mathcal C$ and those in its stable category $\underline{\mathcal C}=\mathcal C/ω$. Our main result shows that $(\mathcal I,\mathcal J)$ is an ideal $n$-cotorsion pair in $\mathcal C$ if and only if $(\mathcal I/ω,\mathcal J/ω)$ is an ideal $n$-cotorsion pair in $\underline{\mathcal C}$. This provides a bridge between higher ideal approximation theory in Frobenius extriangulated categories and its counterpart in their stable categories. Additionally, in Krull--Schmidt exact categories, we establish a bijective correspondence between complete cotorsion pairs and complete ideal cotorsion pairs, answering a question of Fu, Guil Asensio, Herzog and Torrecillas.

</details>


### [250] [On the structure of the singular triplet monoid and its virtual extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29865)
*Carmen Caprau,Mohamad N. Nasser*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入了与n股三元组群L_n相关联的两个新代数结构——奇异三元组幺半群SLM_n及其虚拟扩展VSLM_n，研究了将L_n的表示扩展至这两个幺半群的问题，给出了两种扩展方法（k-局部型和Φ-型），并应用于一个具体表示μ，确定了齐次2-局部扩展及Φ-型扩展，比较了两种扩展方法在SLM_n和VSLM_n上的异同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受奇异辫幺半群和虚拟奇异辫幺半群的启发，建立与三元组群L_n相对应的奇异三元组幺半群SLM_n及其虚拟扩展VSLM_n，并系统研究将L_n的表示扩展到这些幺半群的方法，为相关表示理论提供统一框架。

Method: 首先用生成元和关系给出SLM_n和VSLM_n的定义及多种呈现；接着发展了两类扩展方法：适用于k-局部表示的k-局部型扩展，和适用于满足交换性条件的表示的Φ-型扩展；然后证明每个2-局部表示均可通过两种方法扩展到SLM_n和VSLM_n；最后以Nasser等人引入的表示μ为例，具体计算其齐次2-局部扩展和Φ-型扩展，并比较两类扩展的异同。

Result: 1) 给出了SLM_n和VSLM_n的生成元与关系定义及其多种呈现方式；2) 证明了每个2-局部表示都可通过k-局部型和Φ-型两种方法扩展到SLM_n和VSLM_n；3) 对表示μ确定了所有齐次2-局部扩展并计算了Φ-型扩展；4) 发现在SLM_n上两种扩展在一定参数条件下一致，而在VSLM_n上不一致。

Conclusion: 建立了将L_n表示扩展到SLM_n和VSLM_n的系统性框架，阐明了两种扩展方法的内在联系与差异，为三元组群的相关表示理论提供了新的代数工具和扩展途径。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce two new algebraic structures associated with the triplet group on $n$ strands, $L_n$: the singular triplet monoid $SLM_n$ and its virtual extension $VSLM_n$, defined in analogy with the singular braid monoid and the virtual singular braid monoid. We begin by presenting these monoids in terms of generators and relations, and then derive several alternative presentations of $VSLM_n$. Second, we investigate the problem of extending representations of $L_n$ to these monoids. Two extension methods are developed: the $k$-local type extension, which applies to $k$-local representations, and the $Φ$-type extension, which applies to representations satisfying suitable commutativity conditions. We show that every $2$-local representation of $L_n$ admits extensions to both $SLM_n$ and $VSLM_n$ via the two methods. As an application, we consider a specific representation $μ: L_n \longrightarrow \mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{Z}[t^{\pm1}])$ introduced recently by Nasser et al. We explicitly determine all homogeneous $2$-local extensions of $μ$ to $SLM_n$ and $VSLM_n$, and compute the corresponding $Φ$-type extensions. Furthermore, we compare these two extension methods, showing that they coincide for $SLM_n$ under suitable parameter conditions, while they do not coincide for $VSLM_n$. These results provide a systematic framework for extending representations of $L_n$ to $SLM_n$ and $VSLM_n$.

</details>


### [251] [Orbits on a product of two flags and a line and the Bruhat order, II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30478)
*Mark Colarusso,Sam Evens*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了n×n复一般线性群GL(n)的旗簇B_n与射影空间P^{n-1}的乘积上Borel子群B的对角作用轨道，建立了这些轨道到GL(n+1)的旗簇B_{n+1}上B-轨道的一个嵌入，证明了该嵌入保持闭包关系和幺半群作用，从而通过Bruhat order理解轨道结构，并推广了Magyar等人的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Borel子群在旗簇与射影空间乘积上的轨道结构及其闭包关系，并简化已有的闭包关系描述。

Method: 构造了B_n × P^{n-1}上的B-轨道到B_{n+1}上B-轨道的嵌入，利用对称群上的Bruhat order和Demazure product来刻画轨道闭包关系和幺半群作用。

Result: 建立了轨道之间的嵌入对应，证明了该对应保持闭包关系和幺半群作用；给出了S_i-轨道在B_n上的闭包序由Richardson-Springer标准序给出的结论。

Conclusion: 通过嵌入到更高维旗簇的B-轨道，利用对称群的Bruhat order可以更透明地理解B_n × P^{n-1}上的轨道结构，并推广了先前文献中的结果。

Abstract: Let $G=GL(n)$ be the $n\times n$ complex general linear group and let $\B_{n}$ be its flag variety. A Borel subgroup $B$ of $G$ acts on $\B_{n}\times \mathbb{P}^{n-1}$ diagonally with finitely many orbits. In this paper, we give an embedding of the $B$-orbits on $\B_{n}\times \mathbb{P}^{n-1}$ into the $B$-orbits on the flag variety $\B_{n+1}$ of $GL(n+1)$ and show that this correspondence respects closure relations and preserves monoid actions. As a consequence both closure relations and monoid actions on the set of all $B$-orbits on $\B_{n}\times\mathbb{P}^{n-1}$ can be understood via the Bruhat order on the symmetric group on $n+1$ letters by using our results in \cite{Shpairs}. This amplifies work of Magyar \cite{Magyar} by making the closure relation more transparent and allows us to compute the monoid action using Demazure products. If $S_i$ is the stabilizer in $B$ of the line through the ith standard basis vector, we give an embedding of the $S_i$-orbits on $\B_n$ into the $B$-orbits in a single $G$-orbit in $\B_{n+1},$ and this embedding plays an essential role in the above results. We extend results from our papers \cite{CE21I}, \cite{CE21II}, and \cite{Shpairs}, and in particular show that for $S_i$-orbits on $\B_n,$ the closure ordering is given by the Richardson-Springer standard order.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Monotonic Impurity Entropy beyond Unitarity: the $\mathscr{PT}-$Symmetric Quantum Impurity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28495)
*Pradip Kattel,Abay Zhakenov,Natan Andrei*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个$\mathscr{PT}$对称的量子杂质模型（幺正$SU(2)_1$ Wess-Zumino-Witten体耦合两个通过共轭边界Kondo相互作用的杂质自旋），通过可积晶格实现、Bethe Ansatz求解和有限温度矩阵乘积态计算，发现即使在非幺正边界相互作用下，Kondo屏蔽区间的杂质熵仍从紫外$\ln 4$单调降至红外$0$，突破了$g$-定理的标准假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米多体体系中Kondo屏蔽、边界临界性和杂质熵等现象的演化尚不清楚，作者旨在探索$\mathscr{PT}$对称量子杂质模型中这些现象的普适行为。

Method: 构建$\mathscr{PT}$对称边界杂质的可积晶格实现，通过Bethe Ansatz精确求解，并用有限温度矩阵乘积态计算进行交叉验证。

Result: 在Kondo屏蔽区（谱完全实），杂质熵从紫外$\ln 4$单调降至红外$0$，尽管边界相互作用是非幺正的。

Conclusion: 杂质熵的单调下降在非幺正$\mathscr{PT}$对称体系中仍然成立，超出了$g$-定理的适用条件。

Abstract: Quantum impurity models provide a paradigmatic setting for studying Kondo screening, boundary criticality, and impurity entropies. While these phenomena are well understood in unitary systems, their fate in non-Hermitian many-body settings remains largely unexplored. We study a $\mathscr{PT}$-symmetric quantum impurity model consisting of a unitary $SU(2)_1$ Wess--Zumino--Witten bulk coupled to two impurity spins through complex-conjugate boundary Kondo interactions. Using an integrable lattice realization with $\mathscr{PT}$-symmetric boundary impurities, solved by the Bethe Ansatz and benchmarked against finite-temperature matrix-product-state calculations, we determine the impurity contribution to the free energy and entropy. In the Kondo-screened regime, where the spectrum remains entirely real and the impurities are screened by many-body Kondo clouds, we find that the impurity entropy decreases monotonically from $\ln 4$ in the ultraviolet to $0$ in the infrared. This monotonic flow persists despite the nonunitary nature of the boundary interaction, which places the system beyond the standard assumptions of the $g$-theorem.

</details>


### [253] [Vortex-enhanced photovoltaic current in disordered topological materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28509)
*Pavlo Sukhachov,Penghao Zhu,Ella Banyas,Liang Z. Tan,A. Alexandradinata*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了拓扑材料中实空间晶体缺陷与动量空间波函数奇点（光学涡旋）之间的相互作用如何增强体光伏电流，并通过频率指数和光伏张量的磁对称性约束，建立了拓扑材料类别、缺陷对称性类别和光偏振三者与光伏响应的系统关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无序拓扑材料中晶体缺陷如何与动量空间中的波函数奇点相互作用以增强体光伏电流，并建立拓扑材料类别、缺陷对称性与光电响应之间的系统性关联。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示光学偶极子矩阵元的相位涡旋结构（光学涡旋性）是拓扑材料中普遍存在的特征，并从频域指数和光伏张量约束两个维度，结合多类拓扑材料（多费米子、三维m阶Weyl半金属、二维n阶Dirac体系等）进行案例研究。

Result: 光学涡旋性存在于所有具有非平庸Chern数的拓扑材料中，它增强电子-杂质散射，产生弹道光伏电流，该电流对拓扑材料类别、缺陷对称性类别和光偏振敏感，具体表现为：(1) 拓扑半金属中光伏电流的频率指数依赖关系；(2) 时间反演不变拓扑材料中涌现的磁对称性对光伏张量的约束。

Conclusion: 为结合频率调谐光电导测量、缺陷表征和缺陷工程的三管齐下实验方案提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In disordered topological materials, real-space crystalline defects interplay with momentum-space wave function singularities to \textit{enhance} the bulk photovoltaic current. What's singular is the interband Berry phase, or equivalently the phase of the \textit{optical} dipole matrix element, which has a \textit{vortex} structure in momentum space. Such \textit{optical vorticity} is guaranteed to exist in all topological materials associated with nontrivial Chern numbers. These vortices enhance electron-impurity skew scattering, which manifests as a ballistic photovoltaic current that is sensitive to (a) the topological material class, (b) the symmetry class of crystalline defects, and (c) the light polarization. This sensitivity manifests in two ways: firstly, by (a-c)-dependent frequency exponents for the photovoltaic current $\propto ω^{\text{exponent}}$ in topological semimetals, with $ω$ the frequency of the light source. Secondly, by (a-c)-dependent constraints of the bulk photovoltaic tensor, which are explainable only by emergent, \textit{magnetic} symmetries of \textit{time-reversal-invariant} topological materials. These ideas are concretized by case studies on multifold fermions, 3D $m$-order Weyl semimetals and 2D $n$-order Dirac systems, which include $n$-layer rhombohedral graphene, transition metal dichalcogenides, and topological surface states. Theoretical guidance is provided for a tri-pronged experimental program that combines frequency-tuned photoconductivity measurements, defect characterization and defect engineering.

</details>


### [254] [Mixed-spin Heisenberg ladders in a magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28521)
*D. S. Almeida,A. S. Bibiano,W. M. da Silva,R. R. Montenegro-Filho*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用密度矩阵重正化群和线性自旋波计算，系统研究了磁场中交替混合自旋 (s,S) 海森堡梯子在磁场中的相图，重点分析了 (1/2,1) 情况下的 h-J⊥ 相图，观测到 1/3 磁化平台，并通过有限尺寸分析确定了 Kosterlitz-Thouless 临界转变点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究交替混合自旋海森堡梯子在磁场中的相图，特别是 (1/2,1) 情形下磁场 h 与链间耦合 J⊥ 的相图，以及 1/3 磁化平台的出现条件和临界行为。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）和线性自旋波（LSW）计算方法，通过变 J⊥ 和 h 扫描分析临界线与磁有序的兼容性，并对横向自旋关联函数进行有限尺寸分析来确定 KT 转变点。

Result: 观测到 J⊥>0 时存在 1/3 磁化平台，J⊥<0 时仅在有限范围内出现；通过有限尺寸分析确定了 1/3 平台闭合的 Kosterlitz-Thouless 临界转变点。

Conclusion: 交替混合自旋海森堡梯子在磁场中表现出丰富的相图，1/3 磁化平台的存在依赖链间耦合符号和强度，其闭合属于 KT 型相变。

Abstract: In this work, we study alternating mixed-spin $(s,S)$ Heisenberg ladders in the magnetic field $h$ using density matrix renormalization group and linear spin-wave calculations. The $h$ \textit{versus} interchain coupling $J_\perp$ phase diagram for the $(1/2,1)$ case is investigated in detail. { In particular, we demonstrate the compatibility between the critical line estimates and magnetic ordering by analyzing chains with variable values of $J_\perp$ and of $h$ along the chain, $J_\perp$ and $h$ scans, and considering the usual case of chains with uniform couplings}. The magnetization plateau at 1/3 of saturation magnetization, 1/3 - plateau, is observed for $J_\perp>0$ and in a limited range for $J_\perp<0$. The critical Kosterlitz-Thouless transition point, where the 1/3 - plateau closes, is identified through a finite-size analysis of the transverse spin correlation functions.

</details>


### [255] [Magnetic symmetry implications of the zero- and applied-field Hall effect of UNi$_4$B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28582)
*Z. W. Riedel,W. S. Simeth,S. M. Thomas,F. Ronning,E. D. Bauer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过霍尔效应测量和对称性分析，质疑了UNi₄B非共线反铁磁态中此前提出的环形磁矩排列，提出Cm'或Pm'磁对称群更符合实验观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UNi₄B的反铁磁态（T_N=20 K）的磁结构存在争议，近期磁电测量和理论工作质疑了此前提出的环形磁偶极矩排列方案，需要新的实验证据和对称性分析来澄清。

Method: 测量了UNi₄B在零场和外加磁场下的霍尔电阻率（ρ_yz、ρ_zx、ρ_yx），利用传统霍尔效应经验关系分析贝里曲率贡献，并结合对称性论证与中子衍射、磁电效应结果进行比对。

Result: 面内磁场下ρ_yz和ρ_zx呈现弯曲响应（~8 T, 2 K），归因于动量空间贝里曲率的内禀贡献；面外磁场下ρ_yx呈线性行为（~16 T）。零场霍尔效应在磁有序起始处出现，进一步支持内禀起源。此前提出的零场磁空间群无法解释两个霍尔取向的有限零场效应。

Conclusion: 提出Cm'或Pm'磁对称性（取决于母相非磁空间群）能够同时与霍尔电阻率、中子衍射和磁电效应测量结果自洽一致。

Abstract: The zero-field and applied-field Hall effects in noncollinear antiferromagnets provide evidence for topological states of matter and are tied to materials' magnetic symmetry. For UNi$_4$B, the antiferromagnetic state with $T_\mathrm{N}=20$ K at zero and low magnetic field is debated due to recent magnetoelectric measurements and theory work calling into question the proposed toroidal arrangement of magnetic dipole moments. For a magnetic field applied within the plane of uranium magnetic moments, the field-dependent Hall resistivity of UNi$_4$B shows a curved response for $ρ_{yz}$ ($H{\parallel}x$, $I{\parallel}z$) and $ρ_{zx}$ ($H{\parallel}y$, $I{\parallel}x$) up to $\sim$8 T at 2 K, while an out-of-plane field results in linear behavior of $ρ_{yx}$ ($H{\parallel}z$, $I{\parallel}x$) up to 16 T. Analysis using conventional empirical relationships for the Hall effect indicate that an intrinsic effect from momentum-space Berry curvature contributes significantly to the curved transverse resistivity. Moreover, a finite zero-field Hall effect emerges at the onset of magnetic order for $ρ_{yz}$ and $ρ_{zx}$, further supporting an intrinsic origin of the Hall response. Symmetry arguments for a finite Berry curvature, an observable magnetoelectric effect, and reported magnetic structures suggest that the previously proposed magnetic space groups for the zero-field magnetic structure cannot account for the observed finite zero-field effect for two Hall orientations. Instead, we propose that $Cm'$ or $Pm'$ magnetic symmetry, depending on the parent nonmagnetic space group, is consistent with Hall resistivity, neutron diffraction, and magnetoelectric effect measurements.

</details>


### [256] [Sixteen-Fold Way for Fermionic Topological Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28682)
*Ryohei Kobayashi,Abhinav Prem,Matthew Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发现了(2+1)D 费米子拓扑序的十六重分类族，它们具有纯费米子性质的 ℤ₂ 1-形式对称性反常，并可通过与费米子耦合的 Walker-Wang 模型在格点中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子拓扑序可以拥有玻色子体系所没有的't Hooft反常，但此前缺乏对这种纯费米子反常拓扑序的系统分类和理解，本文旨在发现并刻画这类新家族。

Method: 通过分析 ℤ₂ 1-形式对称性在模16下的't Hooft反常来区分拓扑序；利用(3+1)D 费米子SPT相作为体态，以其有隙边界实现(2+1)D拓扑序；通过Walker-Wang模型耦合局域费米子构建微观格点模型。

Result: 识别出一个新的十六重家族，其中最简单的例子是包含自旋1/8的单个ℤ₂阿贝尔任意子的阿贝尔费米子拓扑序；这些拓扑序对应(3+1)D ℤ₂ 1-形式对称性费米子SPT相，该SPT相在扭曲自旋结构下的分类为ℤ₁₆。

Conclusion: 建立了费米子拓扑序中纯费米子反常的十六重分类，拓展了Kitaev十六重路径的费米子类比，为研究费米子拓扑物相提供了新框架。

Abstract: Fermionic topological orders can host 't Hooft anomalies with no bosonic counterpart. We identify a new sixteen-fold family of (2+1)D fermionic topological orders, forming a fermionic analogue of Kitaev's sixteen-fold way. This family is distinguished by the mod 16 't Hooft anomaly of a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ one-form symmetry, generated in each theory by a single nontrivial $\mathbb{Z}_2$ anyon. This intrinsically fermionic anomaly permits anyon spins that are forbidden in bosonic phases; the simplest new example is an Abelian fermionic topological order containing a single $\mathbb{Z}_2$ Abelian anyon of spin 1/8. Each theory can be realized as the gapped boundary of a (3+1)D fermionic symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phase protected by the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ one-form symmetry, which acquires a $\mathbb{Z}_{16}$ classification once the spacetime spin structure is twisted by the one-form symmetry. We realize these phases microscopically via lattice models built from Walker-Wang models coupled to local fermions.

</details>


### [257] [Survival of the metallic state in a single-hole multiband $p$-orbital molecular system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28836)
*Keisuke Matsui,Ryan A. Klein,Naoya Yoshikane,John Arvanitidis,Matjaž Gomilšek,Urh Klopčič,Shogo Kawaguchi,Hitoshi Yamaoka,Nozomu Hiraoka,Hirofumi Ishii,Qiang Zhang,Shigeo Mori,Hiroki Ishibashi,Yoshiki Kubota,Craig M. Brown,Denis Arčon,Kosmas Prassides*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种单空穴多轨道关联p电子体系（正交结构的Yb₂CsC₆₀，含五价C₆₀⁵⁻阴离子），该体系表现出强金属态且无Mott转变，类似于亚稳态单电子立方结构CsC₆₀。研究表明，在n=1,5的富勒烯体系中，粒子-空穴对称性成立，其p电子衍生态与d轨道固体中的态类似，为关联电子体系研究提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究远离半填充（n=1,5）时，p电子多轨道体系（富勒烯化物）中Hund耦合与强关联的竞争关系，验证其是否与d轨道体系类似能维持金属性而非发生Mott转变。

Method: 通过合成正交结构的Yb₂CsC₆₀（含C₆₀⁵⁻），研究其作为单空穴多轨道关联p电子体系的电子态和输运性质，并与已知的d轨道体系及立方CsC₆₀进行对比。

Result: Yb₂CsC₆₀在单空穴（n=5）掺杂下表现出鲁棒的金属态，没有发生Mott转变，与单电子（n=1）立方CsC₆₀行为一致。

Conclusion: 在富勒烯化物中，n=1和n=5体系具有良好的粒子-空穴对称性，其p电子行为与d轨道过渡金属氧化物类似，表明Hund耦合在远离半填充时能抑制Mott绝缘体转变，维持金属态。

Abstract: Strong correlations and ferromagnetic Hund's coupling lead to diverse electronic phenomena in transition-metal oxides that sensitively depend on the $d$-orbital electron filling. Fullerides, their $p$-electron counterparts, exhibit effective antiferromagnetic Hund's coupling in a different energy range. At half-filling ($n=3$, three electrons in triply degenerate orbitals), both $d-$ and $p$-electron systems are Mott insulators due to strong correlations and Hund's coupling. Away from half-filling, in single-electron/hole ($n=1,5$) $d$-orbital systems, Hund's coupling opposes the correlations, reducing the Mott gap and allowing survival of metallicity. Here we report a single-hole multiorbital correlated $p$-electron system, orthorhombic-structured Yb$_2$CsC$_{60}$ comprising pentavalent C$_{60}^{5-}$ anions, which also exhibits a robust metallic state with no Mott transition, just like in the metastable single-electron cubic-structured CsC$_{60}$. We assert that particle-hole symmetry holds well in ($n=1,5$) fullerides and that their $p$-electron-derived states are analogous to those in $d$-orbital solids, providing impetus for further study of these correlated systems.

</details>


### [258] [Nonequilibrium electron-phonon dynamics with high momentum resolution: Thermalization bottlenecks and the effects of phonon dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28855)
*Maksymilian Środa,Philipp Werner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用量子张量列(QTT)非平衡格林函数(NEGF)框架，在大规模晶格上实现了具有任意声子色散关系的动量分辨电子-声子非平衡动力学模拟。通过对光学和声学声子模型的比较研究，揭示了超越传统声子窗口瓶颈效应的多层级弛豫瓶颈，包括动量空间中的约化窗口、声子热化瓶颈以及方向性散射导致的低动量声子模持续瓶颈。在高达256x256格点上实现了全电子-声子反馈的动量分辨模拟，为时间分辨光谱学提供了准确的参考数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的NEGF研究受限于局域声子和局域自能近似，无法探索动量依赖的动力学过程，且受限于晶格尺寸和传播时间。本文旨在利用QTT-NEGF框架克服这些限制，系统研究动量和频率分辨的电子-声子非平衡弛豫机制。

Method: 采用量子张量列(QTT)表示双时格林函数，实现内存高效表示；在大规模晶格(256x256)上进行动量分辨模拟；比较光学声子和声学声子模型；通过全电子-声子反馈计算自能。

Result: 发现了超越传统声子窗口瓶颈的多层级弛豫瓶颈：对于光学声子，确认主声子能量窗口并发现动量空间中过剩与不足电子布居分离的约化窗口；识别出声子热化中源于动量依赖粒子-空穴连续谱耦合的单独瓶颈。对于声学声子，声子能量窗口呈现显著动量依赖性；约化窗口不对称；布里渊区域间的方向性散射造成低动量声子模的持续瓶颈。高分辨谱揭示声子弛豫与电荷响应的直接对应关系。

Conclusion: QTT-NEGF框架是一种可扩展且受控的定量非平衡电子-声子动力学模拟方法，克服了先前晶格尺寸和传播时间的限制，为时间分辨光谱学提供精确参考数据。

Abstract: The nonequilibrium electron-phonon interplay is central to thermalization of solids, yet the microscopic picture of transient states and relaxation pathways remains incomplete. Previous nonequilibrium Green's function (NEGF) studies were restricted to local phonons and local self-energy approximations, leaving momentum-dependent dynamics largely unexplored. In this work, we demonstrate the power of the recently developed quantics-tensor-train (QTT) NEGF framework through large-scale lattice simulations with arbitrary phonon dispersions. QTTs provide a memory-efficient representation of two-time Green's functions, enabling momentum-resolved simulations with full electron-phonon feedback on lattices up to 256x256 sites. Comparing optical and acoustic phonon models, we reveal a hierarchy of relaxation bottlenecks that extends the well-known phonon-window bottleneck effect. For optical phonons, we confirm the main phonon-energy window and uncover a reduced window separating momentum-space regions of excess and deficit electronic population. We also identify a separate bottleneck in phonon thermalization, rooted in the momentum-dependent coupling to the particle-hole continuum. For acoustic phonons, the phonon-energy window acquires pronounced momentum dependence dictated by simultaneous energy-momentum conservation. The reduced window becomes asymmetric; directional scattering between Brillouin-zone regions creates a persistent bottleneck for low-momentum phonon modes. The high momentum and frequency resolution of our spectra further reveals a direct correspondence between phonon relaxation and charge response. Our results establish QTT-NEGF simulations as a scalable and controlled framework for quantitative nonequilibrium electron-phonon dynamics, overcoming previous lattice-size and propagation-time limitations and providing accurate reference data for time-resolved spectroscopies.

</details>


### [259] [Unusual upper critical field in UTe2 revealed by magnetotransport measurements up to 42 T](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28904)
*Macha Méplan,Ilya Sheikin,Pierre Pugnat,Romain Barbier,François Debray,Cédric Grandclément,Steffen Krämer,Yuriy Krupko,Frédéric Molinié,Kévin Paillot,Robert Pankow,Rolf Pfister,Luc Ronayette,Benjamin Vincent,Charles Simon,Midori Amano Patino,Gérard Lapertot,Georg Knebel,Dai Aoki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过对UTe2单晶在静磁场（最高42T）下的磁阻测量，系统研究了磁场沿bc面倾斜时，第三个超导相SC3的起源和特性。结果表明SC3在20°角首次出现，其起始（电阻率最大值）在更大角度下低于Hm，但零电阻仅在Hm以上达到。此外，SC3在21°角对温度敏感，而在24°角更稳定（~1K），并在c轴附近观测到SdH振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重费米子超导体UTe2中第三个磁场诱导超导相SC3的起源，特别是其是否局限于Hm以上的自旋极化态，还是在更低场就已形成。

Method: 在高达42T的静磁场中，对高质量UTe2单晶进行磁阻测量（温度低至0.35K），磁场施加在(bc)面内，通过改变角度系统绘制场-角相图。

Result: SC3在20°从b轴首次出现；大角度下SC3起始（电阻率最大值）低于Hm，但零电阻仅出现在Hm以上；SC3在21°角对温度敏感，在24°角更稳定（~1K）；c轴附近观测到SdH振荡。

Conclusion: SC3相并非完全局限于Hm以上，其起始（电阻率最大值）可在低于Hm出现，但宏观超导（零电阻）仅发生在完全极化态（Hm以上），揭示了该奇异超导相的复杂本质。

Abstract: The heavy-fermion superconductor UTe2 is unique in that, at ambient pressure, it exhibits three distinct superconducting phases, two of which are induced by magnetic field. When the field is applied along the crystallographic b axis in the orthorhombic structure, the field-induced phase SC2 develops above approximately 20 T and persists up to the metamagnetic transition at Hm about 34 T. When the magnetic field is tilted towards the c axis, another superconducting phase, SC3, emerges at very high fields above about 40 T over a certain angular range. The origin of this exotic phase remains under debate. One of the key open questions regarding the origin of SC3 is whether it is confined to the spin-polarized state above Hm, or whether it already develops at lower fields. Here, we report magnetoresistance measurements performed on a high-quality single crystal of UTe2 in static magnetic fields up to 42 T applied in the (bc) plane at temperatures down to 0.35 K. At this temperature, we find that the SC3 phase first appears at an angle of 20 deg from the b axis. At larger angles, the onset of the SC3 phase, defined by a maximum in resistivity, occurs below Hm. However, zero resistivity is reached only above Hm throughout the entire angular range investigated. These results are summarized in the resulting field-angle phase diagram. Furthermore, we find that at 21 deg the SC3 phase is rapidly suppressed with increasing temperature, whereas at 24 deg it becomes considerably more robust and persists up to about 1 K. Finally, we observe Shubnikov de Haas (SdH) oscillations in the vicinity of the c axis. The observed oscillation frequencies are in good agreement with our previous results. The field dependence of the strongest SdH frequency and of the effective mass is discussed.

</details>


### [260] [Multiple closely spaced transitions and multi-band Hall response in clean ScV$_6$Sn$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29048)
*Jonathan M. DeStefano,Elliott Rosenberg,Chaowei Hu,Xiaodong Xu,Jiun-Haw Chu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过改进助熔剂生长法，制备出残余电阻比（RRR）超过50的高质量ScV₆Sn₆单晶，利用扩大的迁移率范围重新审视了电荷密度波（CDW）相的对称性和磁输运性质。研究揭示了在RRR≥4时出现的接近T_CDW的连续相变序列，并通过弹性电阻证明中间相破坏了母晶格的三重旋转对称性。霍尔效应分析表明，其与内禀反常霍尔效应（AHE）不一致，而是由多能带输运中的小、高迁移率口袋解释。这些结果明确了ScV₆Sn₆的对称性破缺图景，并建立了系统性迁移率调谐作为区分内禀AHE与多能带霍尔贡献的诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ScV₆Sn₆作为笼目金属，其CDW相中的对称性破缺和低场霍尔异常（被认为是AHE）的机制尚不明确，且此前样品质量差（RRR≤9）限制了研究。本文旨在通过提升晶体质量来澄清中间相的本质和霍尔效应的起源。

Method: 改进助熔剂生长法获得RRR>50的高质量单晶；利用弹性电阻测量探测对称性破缺；结合不同RRR范围的母体样品和Cr掺杂样品的霍尔效应分析，对比量子振荡识别的小高迁移率口袋。

Result: 成功制备RRR>50的晶体（此前最高~9）；发现RRR≥4时在T_CDW附近出现连续相变序列，中间相破坏三重旋转对称性；霍尔效应定量上与内禀AHE不一致，而符合多能带输运模型。

Conclusion: 明确了ScV₆Sn₆中CDW相附近的对称性破缺序列，排除了此前报道的低场AHE解释，证明了系统性迁移率调控是区分内禀AHE与多能带霍尔效应的有效诊断工具。

Abstract: The kagome metal ScV$_6$Sn$_6$ has attracted attention as a platform for exploring the interplay between charge density wave (CDW) order and symmetry-breaking phenomena, including a recently reported intermediate phase and a low-field Hall anomaly that has been attributed to an anomalous Hall effect (AHE). The interpretation of both observations has been limited by the modest sample quality achieved by previous growth procedures, which produced crystals with in-plane residual resistivity ratios (RRR) of at most $\approx$9. Here, we report a simple modification of the flux growth procedure that yields ScV$_6$Sn$_6$ single crystals with RRR exceeding 50, more than five times the previous highest reported value, and use this expanded mobility range to revisit both the symmetry and the magnetotransport of the CDW phase. We resolve a sequence of closely spaced transitions in the immediate vicinity of $T_{CDW}$ that emerges above a sharp threshold of RRR $\approx 4$, and demonstrate through elastoresistivity that the intermediate phase breaks the three-fold rotational symmetry of the parent lattice. We examine the Hall response from both the parent samples across the full RRR range as well as Cr-doped samples, and conclude it is quantitatively inconsistent with an intrinsic AHE and is instead explained by ordinary multi-band transport involving small, high-mobility pockets identified through quantum oscillations. These results refine the symmetry-breaking landscape of ScV$_6$Sn$_6$ and establish systematic mobility tuning as a diagnostic for disentangling an intrinsic AHE from multi-band Hall contributions in kagome CDW systems.

</details>


### [261] [Winding-Sector Transitions and Thermodynamic Incommensurability in Helical Valence Bond Phase under Tilted Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29242)
*Yan Liu,Jie Lou,Yan Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用投影量子蒙特卡洛模拟，在最大各向异性极限下研究S=1/2阶梯J-Q₃模型的基态。通过45°倾斜周期边界条件消除边界诱导的有限尺寸模糊性，定义畴壁密度量化螺旋价键相的 spatial modulation，证明了该相是真正的二维非公度相，并确定了柱状价键固相与螺旋价键相之间的相变点为g_c=0.046(2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服空间调制结构研究中边界诱导的有限尺寸 ambiguities，澄清螺旋价键相的本质（是否为真正的二维非公度相）以及 winding-sector 转变的物理来源。

Method: 采用投影量子蒙特卡洛模拟，实施45°倾斜周期边界条件以消除中间相并直接访问 winding-sector 转变；定义畴壁密度量化螺旋价键相的 spatial modulation，并进行热力学外推。

Result: 畴壁密度和特征波矢随耦合比连续演化，无公度钉扎行为；螺旋价键相是真正的二维非公度相，在热力学极限下具有长程键-键序；winding-sector 转变是边界可公度性强制产生的有限尺寸效应；柱状价键固相到螺旋价键相的相变点为g_c=0.046(2)。

Conclusion: 螺旋价键相是二维非公度长程序相，有限尺寸下的 winding-sector 转变并非真正热力学相变，而是边界条件的人为产物。

Abstract: We investigate the ground states of the $S = 1/2$ staircase $J$-$Q_3$ model in the maximally anisotropic limit by employing projector quantum Monte Carlo simulations. To overcome boundary-induced finite-size ambiguities inherent in the study of spatially modulated structures, we implement a $45^{\circ}$ tilted periodic boundary condition that eliminates intermediate phases and provides direct access to winding-sector transitions of the system. By defining a domain wall density to quantify the spatial modulation of the helical valence bond phase, we perform thermodynamic extrapolations and demonstrate that both the domain wall density and the characteristic wavevector evolve continuously with the coupling ratio, exhibiting no commensurate lock-in behavior. Our results establish that the helical valence bond phase is a genuine two-dimensional incommensurate phase with long-range bond-bond order in the thermodynamic limit, clarifying that winding-sector transitions are finite-size effects enforced by boundary commensurability. Furthermore, we determine the phase transition point between columnar valence bond solid phase and helical valence bond phase to be $g_c = 0.046(2)$.

</details>


### [262] [Energy Gap in Weakly Disordered Fractional Quantum Hall Liquids: Quantitative Comparison to GaAs Quantum Well Experiments at $ν= 1/3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29305)
*Yi-Han Zhou,Zi-Ang Wang,Xin Wan,Zhao Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过结合有限层厚和 disorder 效应，微观研究了 GaAs 量子阱中ν=1/3填充因子下的能隙，结果与窄量子阱实验激活能隙定量吻合，并指出宽量子阱需考虑更高子带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于近期高质量GaAs量子阱实验[Phys. Rev. Lett. 127, 056801 (2021)]，旨在微观研究二维电子气在填充因子ν=1/3时的能隙，明确纳入有限层厚和 disorder 效应。

Method: 通过求解泊松-薛定谔方程对实验器件建模得到垂直方向电子波函数，结合实验提取的 disorder 能量，估算弱无序最低朗道能级下ν=1/3的电荷隙和迁移率隙。

Result: 两个能隙与窄量子阱实验测量的激活能隙展现出良好的定量一致性。

Conclusion: 宽量子阱的理论预测可能需要纳入更高子带才能准确预测能隙。

Abstract: Based on a recent experiment in high-quality GaAs quantum wells [Phys. Rev. Lett. 127, 056801 (2021)], we present a microscopic study of the energy gap in two-dimensional electron gases at filling factor $ν=1/3$, explicitly incorporating both finite layer thickness and disorder effects. The finite layer thickness is modeled by solving the Poisson-Schrödinger equations for the experimental devices, yielding the electron wave functions in the perpendicular direction. Using these and the disorder energy extracted from the experiment, we estimate the charge gap and the mobility gap at $ν=1/3$ in the weakly disordered lowest Landau level. Remarkably, both gaps show good quantitative agreement with the activation gap measured from the experiment in narrow quantum wells. Our results also indicate the potential need of incorporating higher subbands to make accurate theoretical predictions of the energy gap in wide quantum wells.

</details>


### [263] [What Does the Single-Particle Spectrum Imply on the Pairing Nature and Pairing Mechanism in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29470)
*Yu-Bo Liu,Zhi-Yan Shao,Zhiming Pan,Chen Lu,Fan Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过分析布里渊区对角线上的配对能隙，区分了双层镍氧化物La₃Ni₂O₇中不同的配对机制。研究发现，轨道杂化驱动的d_{z²}轨道主导配对机制会在α/β-pocket上产生能隙节点，与实验观测的全能隙矛盾；而Hund规则驱动的d_{x²-y²}轨道主导配对机制则产生无节点的全能隙，与ARPES和STM实验结果一致，从而确立了Hund规则驱动机理是最相关的配对机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: La₃Ni₂O₇的配对机制存在争议：强耦合理论分为层内d波配对和层间s波配对两类，后者又细分为d_{z²}轨道主导（轨道杂化驱动）和d_{x²-y²}轨道主导（Hund规则驱动）。实验已揭示无节点、低各向异性的全能隙支持s波配对，但需要进一步区分具体配对机制。

Method: 对称性分析表明布里渊区对角线上轨道杂化消失，因此γ-pocket和α/β-pocket上的配对能隙分别反映d_{z²}和d_{x²-y²}轨道的配对强度。通过对比不同配对机制下沿BZ对角线的能隙结构，并结合RPA微扰计算进行验证。

Result: d_{z²}轨道主导（轨道杂化驱动）的配对机制在α/β-pocket沿BZ对角线必然产生能隙节点，与ARPES的全能隙和STM的U形dI/dV曲线冲突。Hund规则驱动的d_{x²-y²}轨道主导配对机制产生无节点全能隙，与实验一致。弱耦合RPA计算也倾向于d_{z²}轨道主导，同样产生节点或近节点，与实验矛盾。

Conclusion: d_{x²-y²}轨道在配对中起主导作用，Hund规则驱动的配对机制是La₃Ni₂O₇中最相关的配对机理。

Abstract: The pairing mechanism of the bilayer nickelates La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ remains a hotly-debated open question. Existing strong-coupling theories are divided into class favoring intralayer d-wave pairing and that favoring interlayer s-wave pairing, with the latter further divided into $d_{z^2}$ orbital dominated mechanism driven by orbital hybridization and $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital dominated mechanism driven by Hund's rule. Recent angle-resolved-photoemission-spectrum (ARPES) and scanning-tunneling-microscope (STM) combinedly reveal a nodeless full pairing gap with low anisotropy, supporting the s-wave pairing. Here we propose that the pairing gap along the Brillouin zone (BZ) diagonal can serve as a useful probe of pairing mechanism. Symmetry analysis suggests that orbital hybridization vanishes along the BZ diagonal, rendering that the pairing gaps on the $γ$- and $α/β$- pockets reflect the $d_{z^2}$- and $d_{x^2-y^2}$- orbital pairing strength respectively. Under the $d_{z^2}$ orbital dominated pairing mechanism driven by orbital hybridization, gap nodes are inevitable on the $α$- and $β$- pockets along the BZ diagonal, which conflicts with the full gap revealed by ARPES and the U-shaped dI/dV curve observed by STM. The Hund's rule driven pairing mechanism instead leads to a full pairing gap, which well fits the ARPES and STM results. Furthermore, through a random-phase-approximation based calculation, we show that the weak-coupling theory, which tends to yield a $d_{z^2}$-orbital dominated pairing, also leads to nodes or near-nodes on the $α$- and $β$- pockets along the BZ diagonal, conflicting with experiments. This analysis clarifies the dominant role of $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital in the pairing and establishes the Hund's rule driven pairing mechanism as the most relevant one in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$.

</details>


### [264] [Density waves in low-pressure bilayer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29527)
*Lauro B. Braz,Steffen Bötzel,Frank Lechermann,Igor Plokhikh,Rustem Khasanov,Luis G. G. V. Dias da Silva,Ilya M. Eremin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过无约束Hartree-Fock计算，揭示了La₃Ni₂O₇中第二个密度波转变（TDW≈130 K）的起源：在正交相中，纯双条纹自旋态对电荷密度波不稳定，形成自旋调制的双条纹序，建立环境压力下有序态的层级结构，为理解其压力诱导的高温超导提供关键联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: La₃Ni₂O₇的低压相图对理解其压力诱导的高温超导至关重要，但第二个密度波转变（TDW≈130 K）的起源尚未解决，亟需理论解释。

Method: 采用无约束Hartree-Fock计算，在正交相中分析不同序波矢的简并解除，并通过稳定性分析研究双条纹自旋态对电荷密度波不稳定的响应。

Result: 正交相中序波矢Q_Y=(0,π)和Q_X=(π,0)的简并被解除，系统形成Q_Y=(0,π)的双条纹自旋密度波；纯双条纹自旋态对公度电荷密度波不稳定，演变为自旋调制的双条纹序，电荷与自旋不稳定性相互交织。

Conclusion: 建立了La₃Ni₂O₇环境压力下有序态的层级结构，为连接其环境压力相与超导高压相提供了重要桥梁，并提出了进一步的实验验证方案。

Abstract: The low-pressure phase diagram of La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ provides an important reference for understanding its pressure-induced high-temperature superconductivity. While the spin-density-wave transition at $T_{\text{SDW}}\approx150$ K is increasingly well established, the origin of the second density-wave transition at $T_{\text{DW}}\approx130$ K has remained unresolved. Here, we perform unrestricted Hartree-Fock calculations to investigate the potential origin of the second transition. {Within the orthorhombic phase, the degeneracy between possible ordering wavevectors at $\boldsymbol{Q}_{Y}=(0,π)$ and at $\boldsymbol{Q}_{X}=(π,0)$ is lifted and the electronic system} develops a double-stripe spin-density wave with ordering vector $\boldsymbol{Q}_{Y}=(0,π)$. We identify that the pure double stripe spin state is unstable in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ towards a commensurate charge-density wave instability, which favors a spin-modulated double stripe order with intertwined charge and spin instabilities and establish the hierarchy of ordered states in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$, providing an important link between its ambient-pressure and superconducting high-pressure phases. We further discuss our results in the context of available experimental literature and propose further experimental tests to elucidate the origin of the SDW/DW states in this system.

</details>


### [265] [Trimers in the Extended Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30176)
*R. R. Montenegro-Filho,D. R. B. Silva,D. Cogollo,M. D. Coutinho-Filho*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了三聚体链上扩展Hubbard模型在半个填充下，Lieb绝缘铁磁相在最近邻库仑耦合V增加时的稳定性。结果表明铁磁绝缘相在相分离线(V≳U/4)以下保持稳定，相分离后形成两种共存相：一种是由一个子格上的双占据主导的相，与金属性非饱和铁磁相(U≳t)或单态相(U≲t)共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Lieb定理所预言的铁磁绝缘相在扩展Hubbard模型中的稳定性，特别是当最近邻库仑相互作用V增强时该相是否会失稳。

Method: 利用密度矩阵重正化群(DMRG)方法对三聚体链上的扩展Hubbard模型进行数值计算，分析不同U和V参数下的相行为。

Result: 铁磁绝缘相在V≳U/4的相分离线以下保持稳定；相分离后，一个共存相由单一子格上的双占据主导，另一共存相在U≳t时为金属性非饱和铁磁相，在U≲t时为单态相。

Conclusion: Lieb铁磁绝缘相在相当强的最近邻库仑相互作用下仍保持鲁棒性，其失稳发生在相分离而非连续相变，且相分离后的性质取决于U/t的相对大小。

Abstract: The Lieb theorem is a cornerstone of quantum magnetism theory in condensed matter. In this work, we investigate the instability of the Lieb insulating ferrimagnetic phase in the extended Hubbard model on a trimer chain at half-filling, with one electron per site, under increasing the nearest-neighbor Coulomb coupling $V$. Our results show that despite a noticeable increase in doublon density with $V$, the ferrimagnetic insulating phase remains robust up to the phase separation (PS) line, which is observed at $V \gtrsim U/4$, where $U$ is the local Coulomb repulsion. Above the PS line, one of the coexisting phases is primarily populated by doublons on one of the two sublattices of the chain. This phase coexists with a metallic, unsaturated ferromagnetic phase for $U \gtrsim t$, and with a singlet phase for $U \lesssim t$, where $t$ is the intra-trimer hopping amplitude. We estimate the PS and the crossover lines with the help of density matrix renormalization group calculations.

</details>


### [266] [NQS-Agent: Health-Aware Agentic Hyperparameter Optimization for Neural-Network Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30464)
*Jia-Qi Wang,Xiao-Qi Han,Ze-Feng Gao,Rong-Qiang He,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出NQS-Agent，一个用于神经量子态计算中健康感知超参数优化的开源框架。该框架通过监测能量轨迹、检测破坏性优化事件、停止不稳定计算、调整学习率、从安全检查点恢复优化以及使用异常感知评分排序候选方案，在J1-J2海森堡模型的残差卷积NQS上比人工调优的aCNN基线表现更好，并发现了一个结构不同的宽而浅竞争候选架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经量子态（NQS）对强关联量子多体系统的表达能力依赖于架构级超参数和优化调度，但实际精度对这些超参数敏感，缺乏系统化的调优协议。现有方法通常只选择单一最低能量结果，忽视了优化轨迹的稳定性与恢复历史，因此需要一种健康感知的超参数优化方法。

Method: 开发了NQS-Agent开源软件框架，其工作流程包括：监测能量轨迹、检测破坏性优化事件、停止不稳定计算、修改学习率调度、从安全检查点恢复优化、以及使用异常感知评分对候选方案进行排序。在平方晶格Heisenberg J1-J2模型上使用残差卷积NQS进行验证，参数数量与aCNN（参考卷积NQS架构）相当。

Result: NQS-Agent在aCNN参考架构上优于已报道的人工调优aCNN基线，并在参数数量匹配的残差卷积搜索空间中识别出一个结构上不同的宽而浅竞争候选架构。

Conclusion: 评估NQS结果时应考虑优化轨迹的稳定性和恢复历史。健康感知的超参数优化提供了一种可复现的调优协议，超越了仅选择单一最低能量计算的方法。

Abstract: Neural-network quantum states (NQS) provide expressive variational representations for strongly correlated quantum many-body systems, but their practical accuracy depends sensitively on architecture-level hyperparameters and optimization schedules. Here we develop NQS-Agent, an implemented open-source software framework for health-aware hyperparameter optimization (HPO) in NQS calculations. Its workflow monitors energy trajectories, detects destructive optimization events, stops unstable calculations, modifies the learning-rate schedule, resumes optimization from safe checkpoints, and ranks candidates with an anomaly-aware score. We demonstrate the approach on a residual convolutional NQS for the square-lattice Heisenberg $J_1$-$J_2$ model, using architectures with parameter counts comparable to aCNN, a convolutional NQS architecture used here as a reference. The results show that NQS-Agent improves over the reported human-tuned aCNN baseline for the aCNN reference architecture and identifies a structurally distinct wide-and-shallow competitive candidate within the parameter-count-matched residual-CNN search space. These results show that the stability and recovery history of an optimization trajectory should be considered when assessing an NQS result. Health-aware HPO therefore provides a reproducible tuning protocol that goes beyond selecting a single lowest-energy calculation.

</details>


### [267] [Ferromagnetic ordering in Hubbard models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30607)
*Wojciech Niedziółka,Jacek Wojtkiewicz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过密度展开公式研究了 Hubbard 模型在五种受挫晶格（包括面心立方晶格）上的基态铁磁有序问题，证实了即使在中等或低密度下这些晶格也能出现铁磁序，为理解晶格结构对铁磁性的关键作用提供了有力证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态物理和数学物理中长期悬而未决的问题之一是证明某些 Hubbard 模型版本的基态能表现出铁磁有序。已有猜测认为晶格结构（尤其是受挫晶格）是实现铁磁性的关键，但该问题仅得到部分解决。

Method: 利用 Lieb、Seiringer 和 Solovej 在连续情形以及 Giuliani 在简单立方晶格上严格证明的相互作用费米子基态能量的密度展开公式，假设该展开在受挫晶格上也成立，将其应用于五种受挫晶格（包括面心立方晶格）进行分析。

Result: 大多数在受挫晶格上构建的模型确实在中等甚至低密度下就具有铁磁基态。虽然该方法不能作为基态铁磁性的严格证明，但结果强烈表明铁磁序确实可以出现。在某些低密度情况下，有望证明密度展开的收敛性并严格证明"丰富铁磁性"的存在。

Conclusion: 受挫晶格结构是 Hubbard 模型中实现基态铁磁有序的关键因素，即使在低磁矩载流子密度下也能自然产生铁磁性，这为后续严格的数学证明提供了方向。

Abstract: One of the long-standing and only partially solved problems of theoretical condensed matter physics and mathematical physics is to demonstrate that ground states of some of the versions of the Hubbard model can exhibit a ferromagnetic ordering. It has long been speculated that the opportunity crucial for the occurrence of ferromagnetism is the structure of the lattice on which the Hubbard model is formulated \cite{TasakiMB}. As a consequence, while on simple cubic lattices no ferromagnetic ordering seems to be possible, it can naturally arise, even for low densities of magnetic moment carriers, on so-called frustrated lattices.
  We investigate the problem of ground state ferromagnetic ordering with the use of the formula for ground-state energy of interacting fermions as the first term of `density expansion', proven rigorously by Lieb, Seiringer and Solovej \cite{fermi exact} in continuum and by Giuliani \cite{hub exact} for the simple cubic lattice. Assuming that analogous expansion holds also for certain another lattices we apply this formula to five frustrated lattices -- among them to the face-centered cubic one. The hypothesis is confirmed: most of examined models formulated on frustrated lattices do indeed have ferromagnetic ground states already for densities being moderate or even low. Although the approach adopted cannot be treated as a rigorous proof that the ground state is ferromagnetic, the results obtained here strongly indicate that it can be the case. Moreover, as in some cases FM occurs at low densities, one can hope that it would be possible to prove convergence of the density expansion and prove rigorously the occurrence of `wealthy ferromagnetism' in these cases.

</details>

### [268] [Study of the $e^+e^-\to π^+π^-D_s^+D_s^-$ process from $\sqrt{s}$ = 4.42 to 4.95 GeV at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29141)

Zhemchugov,B. Zheng,B. M. Zheng,J. P. Zheng,W. J. Zheng,W. Q. Zheng,X. R. Zheng,Y. H. Zheng,B. Zhong,C. Zhong,X. Zhong,H. Zhou,J. Q. Zhou,S. Zhou,X. Zhou,X. K. Zhou,X. R. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器收集的8.5 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据（质心能量4.42–4.95 GeV），研究了e⁺e⁻→π⁺π⁻Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻过程。未观测到显著信号，给出了各能量点下Born截面90%置信水平的上限；同时在Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻不变质量谱中搜索类粲偶素共振态，但当前统计量下未发现显著共振结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在4.42–4.95 GeV能量区间研究e⁺e⁻→π⁺π⁻Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻过程，并搜索其中可能存在的类粲偶素共振态，以探索粲物理中的新强子态和奇特态。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的8.5 fb⁻¹对撞数据，分析e⁺e⁻→π⁺π⁻Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻过程；对未观测到显著信号的各能量点，采用统计方法设定Born截面90%置信水平的上限；通过分析Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻不变质量谱搜索中间类粲偶素共振结构。

Result: 未观测到e⁺e⁻→π⁺π⁻Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻的显著信号；给出了各质心能量点下Born截面的90%置信水平上限；在Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻不变质量谱中未发现显著的类粲偶素共振结构。

Conclusion: 在当前统计量下，e⁺e⁻→π⁺π⁻Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻过程截面较低，且该过程中不存在显著的类粲偶素共振态信号，需积累更多数据进一步研究。

Abstract: Based on $8.5~{\rm fb}^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected at center-of-mass energies between 4.42 and 4.95 GeV with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII storage ring, we investigate the process $e^+e^-\to π^+π^-D_s^+D_s^-$. With no significant signal observed, upper limits on the Born cross sections of $e^+e^-\to π^+π^-D_s^+D_s^-$ at each energy value are determined at the 90% confidence level. Additionally, a search for intermediate charmonium-like resonances is performed in the $M(D_s^+D_s^-)$ invariant-mass spectrum, but no significant resonant structures are observed with the current statistics.

</details>


### [269] [Influence of the KK graviton decay into hh on the triple Higgs measurement at LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29922)
*Alain Le Yaouanc,François Richard*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 论文报告了在380 GeV和700 GeV处两个KK引力子候选者的证据，并根据Randall-Sundrum模型预测在1000 GeV和1300 GeV处应出现额外共振。ATLAS在三重希格斯耦合搜索中发现了与该预测一致的质量区域过剩现象。局部截面明显超过标准模型预测，尽管边际显著，但该区域背景低，有望在RUN3数据中发现。该结果可解释kl测量中的过量并确认LHC上KK引力子共振系列的存在。此外，在ZZ/WW半轻子和全强子衰变模式中以及精确测量中也呈现了RS模型的间接证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前已在380 GeV和700 GeV处发现两个KK引力子候选者，根据Randall-Sundrum模型的预测，应在1000 GeV和1300 GeV处出现另外两个共振。本文旨在验证该预测，并探索LHC数据中是否存在与RS模型一致的新共振信号。

Method: 分析ATLAS在三重希格斯耦合搜索中的实验数据，关注1000-1300 GeV质量区域的局部截面；同时分析ZZ/WW衰变通道（半轻子和全强子模式）以及来自精确测量的间接证据。

Result: 在1000-1300 GeV质量区域观察到与RS模型预测一致的过量事件，局部截面明显超过标准模型预期。该效应在低背景区域出现，虽仅边际显著，但RUN3数据有望实现发现。ZZ/WW衰变通道和精确测量也提供了间接支持证据。

Conclusion: 这些结果可解释kl测量中的过量，并确认LHC上存在一系列KK引力子共振，为Randall-Sundrum模型提供支持。随着RUN3数据的积累，该发现有望得到确认。

Abstract: Evidence for two KK graviton candidates has been previously reported at 380 GeV and 700 GeV. Following a Randall Sundrum interpretation, two extra resonances should appear at 1000 GeV and 1300 GeV. Recently ATLAS, in its search for triple Higgs coupling, has reported an excess in that mass region in conformity with this prediction. Local cross sections are therefore clearly in excess of the standard predictions even for large values of kl. While still marginally significant, this effect appears in a mass region with low background which allows to expect good prospects of discovery with RUN3 data. Such a result could therefore allow to interpret an excess in the measurement of kl and confirm the existence of a series of KK graviton resonances observable at LHC. Other opportunities seem to appear in searches for heavy resonances decaying into ZZ/WW in semi-leptonic and fully hadronic modes. Indirect evidences for the RS model coming from precision measurements are also presented.

</details>


### [270] [Tracking performance study of the LHCb UP Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29927)
*Yisheng Fu,Jianchun Wang,Xuhao Yuan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于MAPS像素探测器的LHCb UP探测器布局设计及其独立径迹重建算法，实现了效率约98%、鬼影率低于4%的高质量重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为LHCb Upgrade II应对高亮度环境下的径迹重建挑战，设计UP探测器并开发专用独立径迹重建算法。

Method: 采用MAPS（单片有源像素传感器）技术设计四个探测平面的UP像素径迹探测器，并开发专用独立径迹重建算法。

Result: 独立径迹重建效率接近98%，鬼影率保持在4%以下，重建具有高纯度。

Conclusion: UP独立径迹可为LHCb全局重建提供高质量输入，为未来高亮度下的径迹重建和实时重建提供可行方案。

Abstract: This work presents the layout design of the LHCb UP detector, a MAPS-based pixel tracker composed of four detection planes, and several approaches for its standalone track reconstruction. The dedicated UP tracking algorithms demonstrates that efficient standalone reconstruction can be achieved for LHCb Upgrade II with high purity, reaching efficiencies close to $98\%$ while maintaining a ghost rate below $4\%$. These results indicate that UP standalone tracks can provide high-quality inputs for global LHCb reconstruction and offer a viable solution for future high-luminosity tracking and real-time reconstruction challenges.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [Homotopy Frobenius structures on the cohomology of a manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28466)
*Florian Naef,Thomas Willwacher*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明松弛对合n-弗罗贝尼乌斯代数范畴与n-泊松余运算子的右余模范畴是Quillen等价的，由此推出配边化n-流形的上同调自然带有同伦对合n-弗罗贝尼乌斯结构，扩展了M的有理同伦型，解决了长期悬而未决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长期存在的问题：证明配边化n-流形的上同调自然具有同伦对合n-弗罗贝尼乌斯结构，扩展流形的有理同伦型。

Method: 通过建立松弛对合n-弗罗贝尼乌斯代数范畴与n-泊松余运算子的右余模范畴之间的Quillen等价来实现。

Result: 两类范畴是Quillen等价的，并由此推出配边化n-流形的上同调自然带有同伦对合n-弗罗贝尼乌斯结构。

Conclusion: 解决了关于流形上同调结构的长期问题，表明配边化流形的上同调承载更丰富的代数结构。

Abstract: We show that the category of lax involutive $n$-Frobenius algebras is Quillen equivalent to the category of right comodules of the $n$-Poisson cooperad. It follows in particular, that the cohomology of a parallelized $n$-manifold is naturally endowed with a homotopy involutive $n$-Frobenius structure extending the rational homotopy type of $M$, solving a long-standing question.

</details>


### [272] [Discrete homotopy groups of cubical sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28693)
*Daisuke Kishimoto,Yichen Tong*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文将离散同伦群的概念从图推广到任意立方集，证明了拟对称立方集的离散同伦群与其几何实现的同伦群自然同构，并给出了遗忘函子左伴随的纯组合构造，从而为任意立方集几何实现的同伦群提供了纯组合描述，并建立了拟对称立方集的Hurewicz定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将离散同伦群从图论推广到更一般的立方集框架，建立组合与拓扑之间的桥梁，为计算任意立方集几何实现的同伦群提供纯组合工具。

Method: 定义了拟对称立方集（具有坐标置换对称性但不一定具有连接的立方集），构造了从立方集范畴到拟对称立方集范畴的遗忘函子的左伴随，并证明该伴随的单位是对象态弱等价。

Result: 拟对称立方集的离散同伦群与其几何实现的同伦群自然同构；获得了任意立方集几何实现同伦群的纯组合描述；建立了拟对称立方集的Hurewicz定理。

Conclusion: 离散同伦群理论成功推广至立方集，纯组合方法可有效刻画拓扑不变量，为代数拓扑的组合化提供了新途径。

Abstract: We extend the notion of discrete homotopy groups of graphs to arbitrary cubical sets, and show that the discrete homotopy groups of quasisymmetric cubical sets are naturally isomorphic to the homotopy groups of their geometric realizations. Here, quasisymmetric cubical sets are cubical sets equipped with coordinate permutation symmetries that are compatible with faces and degeneracies, but not necessarily with connections. We give a purely combinatorial construction of the left adjoint of the forgetful functor from the category of quasisymmetric cubical sets to the category of cubical sets, and prove that the unit of this adjunction is an objectwise weak equivalence. As a consequence, we obtain a purely combinatorial description of the homotopy groups of the geometric realizations of arbitrary cubical sets. As an application, we establish the Hurewicz theorem for the discrete homotopy groups of quasisymmetric cubical sets.

</details>


### [273] [Homotopy models for $L_{\infty}[1]$-algebras in higher degrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28985)
*Taesu Kim*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出了$L_{\infty}[1]$-态射的高阶同伦理论模型，作为Fukaya-Oh-Ohta-Ono定义的$A_{\infty}$-同伦的自然推广，并证明了在顶点被赋予拟同构的单纯形上填充条件成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将$A_{\infty}$-同伦理论推广到$L_{\infty}[1]$-态射的高阶同伦情形，建立更一般的同伦理论框架。

Method: 通过构建$L_{\infty}[1]$-态射的高阶同伦理论模型，推广Fukaya-Oh-Ohta-Ono的$A_{\infty}$-同伦定义。

Result: 证明了在顶点被赋予拟同构的单纯形上填充条件成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为$L_{\infty}$-代数及其态射的同伦理论提供了重要推广，丰富了高阶同伦填充性质的理论结果。

Abstract: We propose a model of higher homotopy theory of $L_{\infty}[1]$-morphisms as a natural generalization of the $A_{\infty}$-homotopies defined by Fukaya-Oh-Ohta-Ono \cite{FOOO1}. Within this framework, we show that a filling condition holds for simplices whose vertices are assigned quasi-isomorphisms.

</details>


### [274] [An Oriented Street--Roberts Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29373)
*David Gepner,Hadrian Heine*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文定义了有向多面体的概念（包括Street的有向单形和Gray的有向立方体），并证明了有向版本的Street–Roberts猜想，将$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴表示为有向多面体族上的层，推广了Campion的工作。这使我们能从几何角度理解$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴，将其视为同伦型的有向类比。这些有向多面体族诱导了高阶范畴论中的基本操作：例如，join、Gray张量积和bicone分别来源于orientals、立方体和orthoplexes的几何。我们研究了这些操作的相互作用并推导出一些几何公式，推广了Ara–Maltsiniotis、Verity等人的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了从几何视角理解$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴，将其视为同伦型的有向类比，并证明有向版本的Street–Roberts猜想。

Method: 定义了有向多面体（oriented polytope）的统一定义框架，包含Street的有向单形和Gray的有向立方体等具体实例，利用这些有向多面体族上的层来刻画$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴。

Result: 证明了有向版本的Street–Roberts猜想，将$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴表示为有向多面体族上的层；揭示了join、Gray张量积和bicone等操作分别对应orientals、立方体和orthoplexes的几何结构。

Conclusion: 为$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴提供了统一的几何框架，推广了Campion、Ara–Maltsiniotis、Verity等人的先前工作，建立了高阶范畴论基本操作与有向多面体几何之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We formulate a notion of oriented polytope, including Street's oriented simplices and Gray's oriented cubes, and use this to prove an oriented version of the Street--Roberts conjecture, presenting $(\infty,\infty)$-categories as sheaves on suitable families of oriented polytopes, generalizing work of Campion. This allows us to understand $(\infty, \infty)$-categories from a geometric perspective, as directed analogues of homotopy types. These familes of oriented polytopes induce basic operations in higher category theory: for instance, the join, Gray tensor, and bicone arise from the geometry of the orientals, cubes, and orthoplexes, respectively. We study the interaction of these operations and derive some geometric formulae, generalizing work of Ara--Maltsiniotis, Verity, and others.

</details>


### [275] [A note on the second James-Hopf invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29486)
*John R. Klein*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过三条公理刻画了稳定的第二James-Hopf不变量，证明它是满足Cartan公式、在悬浮上消失以及亚稳EHP性质的唯一自然变换。证明结合了James构造的自然稳定分裂与Goodwillie演算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为稳定的第二James-Hopf不变量提供一个公理化的刻画，明确其在同伦论中的唯一性特征。

Method: 通过三条公理（Cartan公式、悬浮消失性、亚稳EHP性质）定义不变量，利用James构造的自然稳定分裂与Goodwillie演算相结合进行证明。

Result: 证明了稳定的第二James-Hopf不变量是满足所述三条公理的唯一自然变换。

Conclusion: 建立了该不变量的公理化刻画，并展示了James构造与Goodwillie演算在其中的关键作用。

Abstract: This paper characterizes the stabilized second James-Hopf invariant by means of three axioms. Specifically, we show that it is the unique natural transformation satisfying the Cartan formula, vanishing on suspensions, and a metastable EHP property. The proof combines the natural stable splitting of the James construction with Goodwillie calculus.

</details>


### [276] [Equivariant cohomology of slice groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30056)
*Zhenxi Huang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过构造Weil/Cartan模型之间的自然链映射，证明了等变上同调在切片邻域与切片之间的同构性，并引入切片群胚的概念，将其等变理论推广到群胚框架下，同时证明了等变上同调在Morita等价下的不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在群胚框架下统一描述群作用，并建立局部模型（切片定理）与整体等变上同调理论之间的精确联系。

Method: (1) 显式构造Weil模型或Cartan模型之间的自然链映射；(2) 引入切片（局部可线性化）群胚的概念；(3) 利用层论方法将等变上同调推广到群胚情形；(4) 证明Morita等价下的不变性。

Result: 构造的链映射诱导了等变上同调的同构；定义了切片群胚并给出了若干例子与应用；证明了群胚的等变上同调在Morita等价下保持不变。

Conclusion: 等变上同调的局部-整体对应在群胚语言下得到了精确的代数实现，切片群胚为研究具有奇异性的群作用提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a compact Lie group, $M$ be a smooth manifold with a $G$ action, then all the data of this model is contained in the action groupoid $G\ltimes M$. If $U_y$ is a small enough neighbourhood of $y\in M/G$, the slice theorem says that \begin{equation*}
  π^{-1}(U_y)=S_{x}\times_{G_{x}} G \end{equation*} where $x$ is a point in the $y$ orbit, $S_x$ is the slice of $x$ and $G_x$ is the isotropy group of $x$. An alternative approach to describe group actions on spaces is through the language of groupoids. Local properties of Lie groupoids are often studied via linearization theorems.
  One can compute the equivariant cohomology $H_G(π^{-1}(U_y))$ of $π^{-1}(U_y)$ using the Weil model or the Cartan model. Also by the homotopy theory, the equivariant cohomologies $H_G(π^{-1}(U_y))$ and $H_{G_x}(S_x)$ are isomorphic.
  In this paper, we explicitly construct a natural chain map between the Weil (or Cartan) models of $(π^{-1}(U_y), G)$ and $(S_x, G_x)$, and prove that it induces an isomorphism in equivariant cohomology. We then introduce the notion of slice (or local linearizable) groupoids, which are locally modeled on Lie group actions on manifolds with gluing data, several examples and applications are discussed. In the last section, we generalize the equivariant theory to these groupoids using sheaf-theoretic methods. We further show that the equivariant cohomology is invariant under Morita equivalence.

</details>


### [277] [Continuous homology of topological periodic homology of complex cobordism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30240)
*Sverre Lunøe-Nielsen,John Rognes*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文计算了复配边谱的拓扑周期同调 TP(MU) 的连续模 p 同调，给出了带有 Steenrod 运算的分次代数结构，答案由一个显式的纯代数构造 C_+ 给出（类似于 Singer 的 R_+ 构造），其 Ext-代数为收敛到 p-完备化 TP(MU) 同伦群的乘法 Adams 型谱序列提供 E_2-项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是确定拓扑周期同调 TP(MU) 的模 p 同调结构，这是理解复配边谱相关同伦理论的关键步骤。

Method: 采用显式纯代数构造 C_+（类比 Singer 的 R_+ 构造），通过计算连续模 p 同调作为分次代数并配备 Steenrod 运算，并利用其 Ext-代数构建乘法 Adams 型谱序列。

Result: 给出了 TP(MU) 的连续模 p 同调作为带 Steenrod 运算的分次代数的完整描述，其结构由 C_+ 构造刻画；该构造的 Ext-代数作为 E_2-项提供了收敛到 p-完备化 TP(MU) 同伦群的谱序列。

Conclusion: 通过纯代数构造 C_+ 完整刻画了 TP(MU) 的模 p 同调，并为计算其同伦群建立了有效的 Adams 型谱序列框架。

Abstract: We determine the continuous mod $p$ homology of the topological periodic homology $TP(MU)$ of the complex cobordism spectrum, as a graded algebra with Steenrod operations. The answer is given in terms of an explicit and purely algebraic construction $C_+$, analogous to Singer's construction $R_+$. Its $Ext$-algebra provides the $E_2$-term for a multiplicative Adams-type spectral sequence converging strongly to the homotopy of $p$-completed $TP(MU)$.

</details>


### [278] [From Frames to Features: Scalable Zigzag Persistence for Binary Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30490)
*David Lanners*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接从连通分量动态中提取$H_0$和$H_1$条形码的方法，通过图编码绕过立方复形，利用Dey和Hou的近线性时间条形码分解算法，在消费级硬件上实现了对4K视频的实时zigzag持续同源性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的zigzag持续同源性方法在处理高分辨率视频时，受限于构建巨大的立方复形以及对大型边界矩阵进行高斯消元，导致计算瓶颈，无法处理高分辨率视频。

Method: 证明$H_0$和$H_1$条形码可以直接从连通分量动态中提取；将这些动态编码为图结构，从而完全绕过立方复形；利用Dey和Hou的近线性时间条形码分解算法；图结构的构建与像素数量成线性关系且帧间高度可并行。

Result: 在消费级硬件上实现了对4K视频的实时zigzag持续同源性分析；总运行时主要由底层图结构构建主导，具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过图编码和近线性时间算法，显著加速了zigzag持续同源性在视频分析中的应用，使高分辨率视频的实时分析成为可能。

Abstract: Zigzag persistence tracks topological features in spatio-temporal data through combinatorial invariants called barcodes. For binary videos, existing methods are bottlenecked by the construction of prohibitively large cubical complexes and performing Gaussian elimination on large boundary matrices, rendering high-resolution videos out of reach. We show that the $H_0$ and $H_1$ barcodes can be extracted directly from connected-component dynamics. By encoding these dynamics in a graph, we bypass cubical complexes entirely and are able to leverage the near-linear time barcode decomposition algorithm by Dey and Hou, leading to significant speedups. The total runtime of our pipeline is dominated by the construction of the underlying graph structures, which scales linearly with pixel count and is embarrassingly parallel across frames, ensuring excellent scalability. We demonstrate how this approach enables zigzag persistence on 4k video at real-time rates on consumer hardware.

</details>
